--
-- Name: email(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: auth; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION auth.email() RETURNS text
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$
  select 
  coalesce(
    nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claim.email', true), ''),
    (nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true), '')::jsonb ->> 'email')
  )::text
$$;


--
-- Name: FUNCTION email(); Type: COMMENT; Schema: auth; Owner: -
--

COMMENT ON FUNCTION auth.email() IS 'Deprecated. Use auth.jwt() -> ''email'' instead.';


--
-- Name: jwt(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: auth; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION auth.jwt() RETURNS jsonb
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$
  select 
    coalesce(
        nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claim', true), ''),
        nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true), '')
    )::jsonb
$$;


--
-- Name: role(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: auth; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION auth.role() RETURNS text
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$
  select 
  coalesce(
    nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claim.role', true), ''),
    (nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true), '')::jsonb ->> 'role')
  )::text
$$;


--
-- Name: FUNCTION role(); Type: COMMENT; Schema: auth; Owner: -
--

COMMENT ON FUNCTION auth.role() IS 'Deprecated. Use auth.jwt() -> ''role'' instead.';

-- Name: uid(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: auth; Owner: -
--

    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$

    nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claim.sub', true), ''),
    (nullif(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true), '')::jsonb ->> 'sub')
  )::uuid
$$;

COMMENT ON FUNCTION auth.uid() IS 'Deprecated. Use auth.jwt() -> ''sub'' instead.';


--
-- Name: grant_pg_cron_access(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: extensions; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION extensions.grant_pg_cron_access() RETURNS event_trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT
    FROM pg_event_trigger_ddl_commands() AS ev
    JOIN pg_extension AS ext
    ON ev.objid = ext.oid
    WHERE ext.extname = 'pg_cron'
  )
  THEN
    grant usage on schema cron to postgres with grant option;

    alter default privileges in schema cron grant all on tables to postgres with grant option;
    alter default privileges in schema cron grant all on functions to postgres with grant option;
    alter default privileges in schema cron grant all on sequences to postgres with grant option;

    alter default privileges for user supabase_admin in schema cron grant all
        on sequences to postgres with grant option;
    alter default privileges for user supabase_admin in schema cron grant all
        on tables to postgres with grant option;
    alter default privileges for user supabase_admin in schema cron grant all
        on functions to postgres with grant option;

    grant all privileges on all tables in schema cron to postgres with grant option;
    revoke all on table cron.job from postgres;
    grant select on table cron.job to postgres with grant option;
  END IF;
END;
$$;


--
-- Name: FUNCTION grant_pg_cron_access(); Type: COMMENT; Schema: extensions; Owner: -
--

COMMENT ON FUNCTION extensions.grant_pg_cron_access() IS 'Grants access to pg_cron';


--
-- Name: grant_pg_graphql_access(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: extensions; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION extensions.grant_pg_graphql_access() RETURNS event_trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $_$
DECLARE
    func_is_graphql_resolve bool;
BEGIN
    func_is_graphql_resolve = (
        SELECT n.proname = 'resolve'
        FROM pg_event_trigger_ddl_commands() AS ev
        LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_proc AS n
        ON ev.objid = n.oid
    );

    IF func_is_graphql_resolve
    THEN
        -- Update public wrapper to pass all arguments through to the pg_graphql resolve func
        DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS graphql_public.graphql;
        create or replace function graphql_public.graphql(
            "operationName" text default null,
            query text default null,
            variables jsonb default null,
            extensions jsonb default null
        )
            returns jsonb
            language sql
        as $$
            select graphql.resolve(
                query := query,
                variables := coalesce(variables, '{}'),
                "operationName" := "operationName",
                extensions := extensions
            );
        $$;

        -- This hook executes when `graphql.resolve` is created. That is not necessarily the last
        -- function in the extension so we need to grant permissions on existing entities AND
        -- update default permissions to any others that are created after `graphql.resolve`
        grant usage on schema graphql to postgres, anon, authenticated, service_role;
        grant select on all tables in schema graphql to postgres, anon, authenticated, service_role;
        grant execute on all functions in schema graphql to postgres, anon, authenticated, service_role;
        grant all on all sequences in schema graphql to postgres, anon, authenticated, service_role;
        alter default privileges in schema graphql grant all on tables to postgres, anon, authenticated, service_role;
        alter default privileges in schema graphql grant all on functions to postgres, anon, authenticated, service_role;
        alter default privileges in schema graphql grant all on sequences to postgres, anon, authenticated, service_role;

        -- Allow postgres role to allow granting usage on graphql and graphql_public schemas to custom roles
        grant usage on schema graphql_public to postgres with grant option;
        grant usage on schema graphql to postgres with grant option;
    END IF;

END;
$_$;


--
-- Name: FUNCTION grant_pg_graphql_access(); Type: COMMENT; Schema: extensions; Owner: -
--

COMMENT ON FUNCTION extensions.grant_pg_graphql_access() IS 'Grants access to pg_graphql';


--
-- Name: grant_pg_net_access(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: extensions; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION extensions.grant_pg_net_access() RETURNS event_trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM pg_event_trigger_ddl_commands() AS ev
    JOIN pg_extension AS ext
    ON ev.objid = ext.oid
    WHERE ext.extname = 'pg_net'
  )
  THEN
    IF NOT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1
      FROM pg_roles
      WHERE rolname = 'supabase_functions_admin'
    )
    THEN
      CREATE USER supabase_functions_admin NOINHERIT CREATEROLE LOGIN NOREPLICATION;
    END IF;

    GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA net TO supabase_functions_admin, postgres, anon, authenticated, service_role;

    IF EXISTS (
      SELECT FROM pg_extension
      WHERE extname = 'pg_net'
      -- all versions in use on existing projects as of 2025-02-20
      -- version 0.12.0 onwards don't need these applied
      AND extversion IN ('0.2', '0.6', '0.7', '0.7.1', '0.8', '0.10.0', '0.11.0')
    ) THEN
      ALTER function net.http_get(url text, params jsonb, headers jsonb, timeout_milliseconds integer) SECURITY DEFINER;
      ALTER function net.http_post(url text, body jsonb, params jsonb, headers jsonb, timeout_milliseconds integer) SECURITY DEFINER;

      ALTER function net.http_get(url text, params jsonb, headers jsonb, timeout_milliseconds integer) SET search_path = net;
      ALTER function net.http_post(url text, body jsonb, params jsonb, headers jsonb, timeout_milliseconds integer) SET search_path = net;

      REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION net.http_get(url text, params jsonb, headers jsonb, timeout_milliseconds integer) FROM PUBLIC;
      REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION net.http_post(url text, body jsonb, params jsonb, headers jsonb, timeout_milliseconds integer) FROM PUBLIC;

      GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION net.http_get(url text, params jsonb, headers jsonb, timeout_milliseconds integer) TO supabase_functions_admin, postgres, anon, authenticated, service_role;
      GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION net.http_post(url text, body jsonb, params jsonb, headers jsonb, timeout_milliseconds integer) TO supabase_functions_admin, postgres, anon, authenticated, service_role;
    END IF;
  END IF;
END;
$$;


--
-- Name: FUNCTION grant_pg_net_access(); Type: COMMENT; Schema: extensions; Owner: -
--

COMMENT ON FUNCTION extensions.grant_pg_net_access() IS 'Grants access to pg_net';


--
-- Name: pgrst_ddl_watch(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: extensions; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION extensions.pgrst_ddl_watch() RETURNS event_trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
  cmd record;
BEGIN
  FOR cmd IN SELECT * FROM pg_event_trigger_ddl_commands()
  LOOP
    IF cmd.command_tag IN (
      'CREATE SCHEMA', 'ALTER SCHEMA'
    , 'CREATE TABLE', 'CREATE TABLE AS', 'SELECT INTO', 'ALTER TABLE'
    , 'CREATE FOREIGN TABLE', 'ALTER FOREIGN TABLE'
    , 'CREATE VIEW', 'ALTER VIEW'
    , 'CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW', 'ALTER MATERIALIZED VIEW'
    , 'CREATE FUNCTION', 'ALTER FUNCTION'
    , 'CREATE TRIGGER'
    , 'CREATE TYPE', 'ALTER TYPE'
    , 'CREATE RULE'
    , 'COMMENT'
    )
    -- don't notify in case of CREATE TEMP table or other objects created on pg_temp
    AND cmd.schema_name is distinct from 'pg_temp'
    THEN
      NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema';
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
END; $$;


--
-- Name: pgrst_drop_watch(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: extensions; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION extensions.pgrst_drop_watch() RETURNS event_trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
DECLARE
  obj record;
BEGIN
  FOR obj IN SELECT * FROM pg_event_trigger_dropped_objects()
  LOOP
    IF obj.object_type IN (
      'schema'
    , 'table'
    , 'foreign table'
    , 'view'
    , 'materialized view'
    , 'function'
    , 'trigger'
    , 'type'
    , 'rule'
    )
    AND obj.is_temporary IS false -- no pg_temp objects
    THEN
      NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema';
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
END; $$;


--
-- Name: set_graphql_placeholder(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: extensions; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION extensions.set_graphql_placeholder() RETURNS event_trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $_$
    DECLARE
    graphql_is_dropped bool;
    BEGIN
    graphql_is_dropped = (
        SELECT ev.schema_name = 'graphql_public'
        FROM pg_event_trigger_dropped_objects() AS ev
        WHERE ev.schema_name = 'graphql_public'
    );

    IF graphql_is_dropped
    THEN
        create or replace function graphql_public.graphql(
            "operationName" text default null,
            query text default null,
            variables jsonb default null,
            extensions jsonb default null
        )
            returns jsonb
            language plpgsql
        as $$
            DECLARE
                server_version float;
            BEGIN
                server_version = (SELECT (SPLIT_PART((select version()), ' ', 2))::float);

                IF server_version >= 14 THEN
                    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
                        'errors', jsonb_build_array(
                            jsonb_build_object(
                                'message', 'pg_graphql extension is not enabled.'
                            )
                        )
                    );
                ELSE
                    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
                        'errors', jsonb_build_array(
                            jsonb_build_object(
                                'message', 'pg_graphql is only available on projects running Postgres 14 onwards.'
                            )
                        )
                    );
                END IF;
            END;
        $$;
    END IF;

    END;
$_$;


--
-- Name: FUNCTION set_graphql_placeholder(); Type: COMMENT; Schema: extensions; Owner: -
--

COMMENT ON FUNCTION extensions.set_graphql_placeholder() IS 'Reintroduces placeholder function for graphql_public.graphql';


--
-- Name: graphql(text, text, jsonb, jsonb); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: graphql_public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION graphql_public.graphql("operationName" text DEFAULT NULL::text, query text DEFAULT NULL::text, variables jsonb DEFAULT NULL::jsonb, extensions jsonb DEFAULT NULL::jsonb) RETURNS jsonb
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
            DECLARE
                server_version float;
            BEGIN
                server_version = (SELECT (SPLIT_PART((select version()), ' ', 2))::float);

                IF server_version >= 14 THEN
                    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
                        'errors', jsonb_build_array(
                            jsonb_build_object(
                                'message', 'pg_graphql extension is not enabled.'
                            )
                        )
                    );
                ELSE
                    RETURN jsonb_build_object(
                        'errors', jsonb_build_array(
                            jsonb_build_object(
                                'message', 'pg_graphql is only available on projects running Postgres 14 onwards.'
                            )
                        )
                    );
                END IF;
            END;
        $$;


--
-- Name: get_auth(text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: pgbouncer; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION pgbouncer.get_auth(p_usename text) RETURNS TABLE(username text, password text)
    LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER
    SET search_path TO ''
    AS $_$
  BEGIN
      RAISE DEBUG 'PgBouncer auth request: %', p_usename;

      RETURN QUERY
      SELECT
          rolname::text,
          CASE WHEN rolvaliduntil < now()
              THEN null
              ELSE rolpassword::text
          END
      FROM pg_authid
      WHERE rolname=$1 and rolcanlogin;
  END;
  $_$;


--
-- Name: activate_costing_master_revision(uuid, text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.activate_costing_master_revision(p_revision_id uuid, p_updated_by text DEFAULT NULL::text) RETURNS uuid
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  v_costing_type text;
begin
  select costing_type
    into v_costing_type
    from public.costing_master_revisions
   where id = p_revision_id
   limit 1;

  if v_costing_type is null then
    raise exception 'Costing master revision not found';
  end if;

  update public.costing_master_revisions
     set is_current = false,
         status = case when status = 'ACTIVE' then 'ARCHIVED' else status end,
         updated_by = coalesce(p_updated_by, updated_by),
         updated_at = now()
   where costing_type = v_costing_type
     and id <> p_revision_id
     and is_current = true;

  update public.costing_master_revisions
     set is_current = true,
         status = 'ACTIVE',
         effective_from = coalesce(effective_from, now()),
         updated_by = coalesce(p_updated_by, updated_by),
         updated_at = now()
   where id = p_revision_id;

  return p_revision_id;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: activate_item_bom_revision(uuid, text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.activate_item_bom_revision(p_revision_id uuid, p_approved_by text DEFAULT NULL::text) RETURNS uuid
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  v_target public.item_bom_revisions%rowtype;
begin
  select *
    into v_target
    from public.item_bom_revisions
   where id = p_revision_id
   limit 1;

  if v_target.id is null then
    raise exception 'BOM revision not found: %', p_revision_id;
  end if;

  update public.item_bom_revisions
     set is_current = false,
         status = case when id = p_revision_id then status else 'ARCHIVED' end,
         archived_at = case when id = p_revision_id then archived_at else now() end,
         archived_by = case when id = p_revision_id then archived_by else p_approved_by end,
         updated_by = p_approved_by
   where item_code = v_target.item_code
     and is_current = true
     and id <> p_revision_id;

  update public.item_bom_revisions
     set is_current = true,
         status = 'ACTIVE',
         effective_from = coalesce(effective_from, now()),
         approved_at = now(),
         approved_by = p_approved_by,
         updated_by = p_approved_by
   where id = p_revision_id;

  update public.items
     set manufacturing_enabled = true,
         revision_controlled = true,
         bom_basis = 'PER_UNIT',
         current_revision_no = v_target.revision_no,
         item_lifecycle_status = case
           when active = false then 'INACTIVE'
           else 'ACTIVE'
         end
   where item_code = v_target.item_code;

  return p_revision_id;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: backfill_missing_sales_order_artworks(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.backfill_missing_sales_order_artworks() RETURNS bigint
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  v_inserted bigint := 0;
begin
  with inserted as (
    insert into public.artworks (
      so_id,
      line_no,
      artwork_no,
      product_type,
      plate_status,
      die_status,
      status,
      sheet_length,
      sheet_width,
      sheet_ups,
      printing_colors
    )
    select
      sol.so_id,
      sol.line_no,
      null,
      coalesce(sol.category, ''),
      'PENDING',
      'PENDING',
      'NO_ART',
      null,
      null,
      null,
      null
    from public.sales_order_lines sol
    join public.sales_orders so
      on so.id = sol.so_id
    left join public.artworks a
      on a.so_id = sol.so_id
     and a.line_no::text = sol.line_no::text
    where a.id is null
      and sol.so_id is not null
      and sol.line_no is not null
      and upper(coalesce(so.status, 'OPEN')) not in ('CANCELLED', 'CLOSED')
      and upper(coalesce(sol.status, 'OPEN')) not in ('CANCELLED', 'CLOSED')
      and upper(coalesce(so.order_prefix, '')) <> 'MB'
      and upper(coalesce(so.sales_type, '')) <> 'MANUAL_BILLING'
      and upper(trim(coalesce(sol.product_code, ''))) not in ('SLC001', 'SLC002', 'SLC003')
      and upper(trim(coalesce(sol.category, ''))) not in ('FLEXO PRINTING PLATE', 'OFFSET PRINTING PLATE', 'DIE')
      and upper(trim(coalesce(sol.hsn_group, ''))) not in ('FLEXO PRINTING PLATE', 'OFFSET PRINTING PLATE', 'DIE')
      and upper(trim(coalesce(sol.product_name, ''))) not in ('FLEXO PRINTING PLATE', 'OFFSET PRINTING PLATE', 'DIE')
    returning 1
  )
  select count(*) into v_inserted
  from inserted;

  return coalesce(v_inserted, 0);
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: billing_document_register_v2(date, date, text, text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.billing_document_register_v2(p_date_from date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_date_to date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_status text DEFAULT 'ALL'::text, p_q text DEFAULT NULL::text) RETURNS TABLE(id text, invoice_no text, invoice_date date, document_type text, client_code text, client_name text, status text, billing_mode text, total_qty numeric, line_count integer, product_preview text, remarks text, subtotal numeric, tax_total numeric, grand_total numeric, posted_at timestamp with time zone)
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$
  with base as (
    select
      inv.id::text as id,
      inv.invoice_no,
      inv.invoice_date,
      case
        when upper(coalesce(inv.document_type, '')) in ('INVOICE', 'CHALLAN') then upper(inv.document_type)
        when coalesce(inv.invoice_no, '') like 'DC-H/%' then 'CHALLAN'
        else 'INVOICE'
      end as document_type,
      coalesce(inv.client_code, 'MANUAL') as client_code,
      coalesce(nullif(inv.bill_to_name, ''), nullif(inv.client_name, ''), c.client_name, inv.client_code, 'MANUAL') as client_name,
      coalesce(inv.status, 'DRAFT') as status,
      coalesce(inv.billing_mode, '') as billing_mode,
      coalesce(inv.remarks, '') as remarks,
      coalesce(inv.subtotal, 0)::numeric(18,2) as subtotal,
      coalesce(inv.tax_total, greatest(coalesce(inv.grand_total, 0) - coalesce(inv.subtotal, 0) - coalesce(inv.freight, 0), 0))::numeric(18,2) as tax_total,
      coalesce(inv.grand_total, 0)::numeric(18,2) as grand_total,
      inv.posted_at
    from public.invoices inv
    left join public.clients c on c.client_code = inv.client_code
    where (p_date_from is null or inv.invoice_date >= p_date_from)
      and (p_date_to is null or inv.invoice_date <= p_date_to)
      and (
        coalesce(upper(p_status), 'ALL') = 'ALL'
        or upper(coalesce(inv.status, '')) = upper(p_status)
      )
      and (
        coalesce(trim(p_q), '') = ''
        or (
          coalesce(inv.invoice_no, '') || ' ' ||
          coalesce(inv.client_code, '') || ' ' ||
          coalesce(inv.client_name, '') || ' ' ||
          coalesce(inv.bill_to_name, '') || ' ' ||
          coalesce(inv.status, '') || ' ' ||
          coalesce(inv.invoice_date::text, '')
        ) ilike '%' || trim(p_q) || '%'
      )
  ),
  line_meta as (
    select
      il.invoice_id::text as id,
      count(*)::integer as line_count,
      coalesce(sum(il.qty), 0)::numeric(18,3) as total_qty,
      min(upper(coalesce(nullif(il.source_mode, ''), 'UNKNOWN'))) as derived_billing_mode
    from public.invoice_lines il
    group by il.invoice_id
  ),
  line_preview_raw as (
    select
      s.id,
      s.product_label,
      row_number() over (partition by s.id order by s.product_label) as rn
    from (
      select distinct
        il.invoice_id::text as id,
        coalesce(nullif(il.product_name, ''), nullif(il.product_code, ''), 'Item') as product_label
      from public.invoice_lines il
    ) s
  ),
  line_preview as (
    select
      id,
      string_agg(product_label, ', ' order by product_label) as product_preview
    from line_preview_raw
    where rn <= 2
    group by id
  )
  select
    b.id,
    b.invoice_no,
    b.invoice_date,
    b.document_type,
    b.client_code,
    b.client_name,
    b.status,
    coalesce(nullif(b.billing_mode, ''), lm.derived_billing_mode, 'UNKNOWN') as billing_mode,
    coalesce(lm.total_qty, 0)::numeric(18,3) as total_qty,
    coalesce(lm.line_count, 0)::integer as line_count,
    coalesce(lp.product_preview, '') as product_preview,
    b.remarks,
    b.subtotal,
    b.tax_total,
    b.grand_total,
    b.posted_at
  from base b
  left join line_meta lm on lm.id = b.id
  left join line_preview lp on lp.id = b.id
  order by b.invoice_date desc, b.id desc
  limit 500;
$$;


--
-- Name: billing_invoice_register(date, date, text, text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.billing_invoice_register(p_date_from date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_date_to date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_status text DEFAULT 'ALL'::text, p_q text DEFAULT NULL::text) RETURNS TABLE(id text, invoice_no text, invoice_date date, client_code text, client_name text, status text, subtotal numeric, tax_total numeric, grand_total numeric, posted_at timestamp with time zone)
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$
  select
    inv.id,
    inv.invoice_no,
    inv.invoice_date,
    inv.client_code,
    coalesce(inv.client_code, 'MANUAL') as client_name,
    inv.status,
    inv.subtotal,
    (coalesce(inv.grand_total, 0) - coalesce(inv.subtotal, 0))::numeric as tax_total,
    inv.grand_total,
    null::timestamptz as posted_at
  from public.invoices inv
  where (p_date_from is null or inv.invoice_date >= p_date_from)
    and (p_date_to is null or inv.invoice_date <= p_date_to)
    and (
      coalesce(upper(p_status), 'ALL') = 'ALL'
      or upper(coalesce(inv.status, '')) = upper(p_status)
    )
    and (
      coalesce(trim(p_q), '') = ''
      or (
        coalesce(inv.invoice_no, '') || ' ' ||
        coalesce(inv.client_code, '') || ' ' ||
        coalesce(inv.status, '') || ' ' ||
        coalesce(inv.invoice_date::text, '')
      ) ilike '%' || trim(p_q) || '%'
    )
  order by inv.invoice_date desc, inv.id desc
  limit 200;
$$;


--
-- Name: billing_job_dataset(text, text, text, text, boolean); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.billing_job_dataset(p_client_code text, p_so_number text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_mode text DEFAULT 'DISPATCH'::text, p_q text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_only_open boolean DEFAULT true) RETURNS TABLE(so_id uuid, so_line_id uuid, so_number text, so_date date, client_code text, client_name text, client_state text, client_gstin text, client_address text, po_number text, po_date date, currency text, sales_remarks text, mode_of_transport text, transport_preference text, transport_payment text, billing_remarks text, sales_rep text, line_no integer, product_code text, product_name text, category text, unit text, rate numeric, gst_pct numeric, hsn_group text, order_qty numeric, packed_qty numeric, dispatched_qty numeric, fg_adjusted_qty numeric, billed_qty numeric, posted_qty numeric, draft_qty numeric, dispatch_billable_qty numeric, fg_billable_qty numeric, direct_billable_qty numeric, billable_qty numeric, latest_packed_at timestamp with time zone, dispatch_no text, dispatch_date timestamp with time zone, transporter text, vehicle_no text, lr_no text, job_reference text, division text, quote_no text, pm_code text, expected_delivery date, final_delivery date, prepress_remarks text, product_remarks text, invoice_refs text, advance_payment_required boolean, advance_payment_received boolean, advance_payment_status text)
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$
  select
    v.so_id,
    v.so_line_id,
    v.so_number,
    v.so_date,
    v.client_code,
    v.client_name,
    v.client_state,
    v.client_gstin,
    v.client_address,
    v.po_number,
    v.po_date,
    v.currency,
    v.sales_remarks,
    v.mode_of_transport,
    v.transport_preference,
    v.transport_payment,
    v.billing_remarks,
    v.sales_rep,
    v.line_no,
    v.product_code,
    v.product_name,
    v.category,
    v.unit,
    v.rate,
    v.gst_pct,
    v.hsn_group,
    v.order_qty,
    v.packed_qty,
    v.dispatched_qty,
    v.fg_adjusted_qty,
    v.billed_qty,
    v.posted_qty,
    v.draft_qty,
    v.dispatch_billable_qty,
    v.fg_billable_qty,
    v.direct_billable_qty,
    case
      when upper(coalesce(p_mode, 'DISPATCH')) = 'DIRECT' then v.direct_billable_qty
      when upper(coalesce(p_mode, 'DISPATCH')) = 'FG' then v.fg_billable_qty
      else v.dispatch_billable_qty
    end as billable_qty,
    v.latest_packed_at,
    v.dispatch_no,
    v.dispatch_date,
    v.transporter,
    v.vehicle_no,
    v.lr_no,
    v.job_reference,
    v.division,
    v.quote_no,
    v.pm_code,
    v.expected_delivery,
    v.final_delivery,
    v.prepress_remarks,
    v.product_remarks,
    v.invoice_refs,
    v.advance_payment_required,
    v.advance_payment_received,
    v.advance_payment_status
  from public.v_billing_line_read_model v
  where v.client_code = p_client_code
    and (coalesce(trim(p_so_number), '') = '' or v.so_number = trim(p_so_number))
    and (coalesce(trim(p_q), '') = '' or v.match_text like '%' || lower(trim(p_q)) || '%')
    and (
      not coalesce(p_only_open, true)
      or (
        case
          when upper(coalesce(p_mode, 'DISPATCH')) = 'DIRECT' then v.direct_billable_qty
          when upper(coalesce(p_mode, 'DISPATCH')) = 'FG' then v.fg_billable_qty
          else v.dispatch_billable_qty
        end
      ) > 0
    )
  order by v.so_date desc nulls last, v.so_number desc, v.line_no asc;
$$;


--
-- Name: create_item_bom_revision(text, text, text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.create_item_bom_revision(p_item_code text, p_created_by text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_revision_reason text DEFAULT NULL::text) RETURNS uuid
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  v_current public.item_bom_revisions%rowtype;
  v_new_id uuid;
  v_next_revision integer;
begin
  select *
    into v_current
    from public.item_bom_revisions
   where item_code = p_item_code
     and is_current = true
   limit 1;

  select coalesce(max(revision_no), 0) + 1
    into v_next_revision
    from public.item_bom_revisions
   where item_code = p_item_code;

  insert into public.item_bom_revisions (
    item_code,
    revision_no,
    status,
    is_current,
    bom_basis,
    base_quantity,
    output_quantity,
    output_uom,
    revision_reason,
    change_summary,
    item_info_json,
    source_revision_id,
    created_by,
    updated_by
  )
  values (
    p_item_code,
    v_next_revision,
    'DRAFT',
    false,
    coalesce(v_current.bom_basis, 'PER_UNIT'),
    coalesce(v_current.base_quantity, 1),
    coalesce(v_current.output_quantity, 1),
    coalesce(v_current.output_uom, 'NOS'),
    p_revision_reason,
    null,
    coalesce(v_current.item_info_json, '{}'::jsonb),
    v_current.id,
    p_created_by,
    p_created_by
  )
  returning id into v_new_id;

  if v_current.id is not null then
    insert into public.item_bom_materials (
      revision_id,
      line_no,
      material_type,
      material_group,
      material_item_code,
      material_name,
      qty_per_unit,
      uom,
      wastage_pct,
      gsm,
      deckle_mm,
      cut_size_mm,
      flute,
      ply_no,
      remarks,
      extra_json,
      created_by,
      updated_by
    )
    select
      v_new_id,
      line_no,
      material_type,
      material_group,
      material_item_code,
      material_name,
      qty_per_unit,
      uom,
      wastage_pct,
      gsm,
      deckle_mm,
      cut_size_mm,
      flute,
      ply_no,
      remarks,
      extra_json,
      p_created_by,
      p_created_by
    from public.item_bom_materials
    where revision_id = v_current.id;

    insert into public.item_bom_routing (
      revision_id,
      sequence_no,
      department,
      operation_name,
      machine_name,
      setup_time_min,
      run_time_min,
      standard_rate_qty_per_hour,
      outsource,
      remarks,
      extra_json,
      created_by,
      updated_by
    )
    select
      v_new_id,
      sequence_no,
      department,
      operation_name,
      machine_name,
      setup_time_min,
      run_time_min,
      standard_rate_qty_per_hour,
      outsource,
      remarks,
      extra_json,
      p_created_by,
      p_created_by
    from public.item_bom_routing
    where revision_id = v_current.id;
  end if;

  return v_new_id;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: ensure_artwork_row_for_sales_order_line_trg(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.ensure_artwork_row_for_sales_order_line_trg() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  v_order_status text := 'OPEN';
  v_order_prefix text := '';
  v_sales_type text := '';
  v_item_code text := upper(trim(coalesce(new.product_code, '')));
  v_category text := upper(trim(coalesce(new.category, '')));
  v_hsn_group text := upper(trim(coalesce(new.hsn_group, '')));
  v_product_name text := upper(trim(coalesce(new.product_name, '')));
begin
  if new.so_id is null or new.line_no is null then
    return new;
  end if;

  select
    upper(coalesce(so.status, 'OPEN')),
    upper(coalesce(so.order_prefix, '')),
    upper(coalesce(so.sales_type, ''))
  into
    v_order_status,
    v_order_prefix,
    v_sales_type
  from public.sales_orders so
  where so.id = new.so_id;

  if coalesce(v_order_status, 'OPEN') in ('CANCELLED', 'CLOSED') then
    return new;
  end if;

  if upper(coalesce(new.status, 'OPEN')) in ('CANCELLED', 'CLOSED') then
    return new;
  end if;

  if v_order_prefix = 'MB' or v_sales_type = 'MANUAL_BILLING' then
    return new;
  end if;

  if v_item_code in ('SLC001', 'SLC002', 'SLC003')
     or v_category in ('FLEXO PRINTING PLATE', 'OFFSET PRINTING PLATE', 'DIE')
     or v_hsn_group in ('FLEXO PRINTING PLATE', 'OFFSET PRINTING PLATE', 'DIE')
     or v_product_name in ('FLEXO PRINTING PLATE', 'OFFSET PRINTING PLATE', 'DIE') then
    return new;
  end if;

  insert into public.artworks (
    so_id,
    line_no,
    artwork_no,
    product_type,
    plate_status,
    die_status,
    status,
    sheet_length,
    sheet_width,
    sheet_ups,
    printing_colors
  )
  select
    new.so_id,
    new.line_no,
    null,
    coalesce(new.category, ''),
    'PENDING',
    'PENDING',
    'NO_ART',
    null,
    null,
    null,
    null
  where not exists (
    select 1
    from public.artworks a
    where a.so_id = new.so_id
      and a.line_no::text = new.line_no::text
  );

  return new;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: gen_item_code(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.gen_item_code() RETURNS text
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
begin
  return 'IT' || lpad(nextval('item_code_seq')::text, 6, '0');
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: generate_so_number(text, date); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.generate_so_number(prefix text, so_date date) RETURNS text
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  fy text;
  seq bigint;
begin
  fy :=
    case
      when extract(month from so_date) >= 4
      then to_char(so_date, 'YY') || '_' || to_char(so_date + interval '1 year', 'YY')
      else to_char(so_date - interval '1 year', 'YY') || '_' || to_char(so_date, 'YY')
    end;

  if prefix = 'SL' then
    seq := nextval('so_seq_sl');
  else
    seq := nextval('so_seq_m');
  end if;

  return prefix || lpad(seq::text, 6, '0') || '_' || fy;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: generate_so_number(text, text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.generate_so_number(p_prefix text, p_fy text) RETURNS TABLE(so_number text)
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  next_no integer;
begin
  -- Ensure sequence row exists
  insert into invoice_sequences(prefix, fy, last_no)
  values (p_prefix, p_fy, 0)
  on conflict (prefix) do nothing;

  -- Increment and fetch
  update invoice_sequences
  set last_no = last_no + 1
  where prefix = p_prefix
  returning last_no into next_no;

  -- Build SO number
  so_number := p_prefix || '/' || p_fy || '/' || lpad(next_no::text, 6, '0');

  return next;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: get_next_dispatch_no(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.get_next_dispatch_no() RETURNS text
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
 n int;
begin

 update dispatch_sequence
 set last_no = last_no + 1
 where id=1
 returning last_no into n;

 return 'DIS-'||lpad(n::text,5,'0');

end;
$$;


--
-- Name: get_next_invoice_no(text, date); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.get_next_invoice_no(p_prefix text, p_invoice_date date) RETURNS text
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  v_fy text;
  v_next integer;
begin
  -- FY logic (India)
  if extract(month from p_invoice_date) >= 4 then
    v_fy := extract(year from p_invoice_date)::int || '-' ||
            right((extract(year from p_invoice_date)::int + 1)::text, 2);
  else
    v_fy := (extract(year from p_invoice_date)::int - 1) || '-' ||
            right(extract(year from p_invoice_date)::text, 2);
  end if;

  insert into invoice_sequences (fy, prefix, last_no)
  values (v_fy, p_prefix, 0)
  on conflict (fy, prefix) do nothing;

  update invoice_sequences
  set last_no = last_no + 1
  where fy = v_fy and prefix = p_prefix
  returning last_no into v_next;

  return p_prefix || '/' || v_fy || '/' || v_next;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: inv_avg_rate(uuid, text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.inv_avg_rate(p_item_id uuid, p_location text) RETURNS numeric
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$
  select
    case
      when sum(qty_in) = 0 then 0
      else sum(value)/sum(qty_in)
    end
  from inv_ledger
  where item_id = p_item_id
    and (p_location is null or location = p_location);
$$;


--
-- Name: inv_stock_qty(uuid, text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.inv_stock_qty(p_item_id uuid, p_location text) RETURNS numeric
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$
  select coalesce(sum(qty_in - qty_out), 0)
  from inv_ledger
  where item_id = p_item_id
    and (p_location is null or location = p_location);
$$;


--
-- Name: inv_stock_snapshot(text, text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.inv_stock_snapshot(p_location text, p_search text) RETURNS TABLE(itemcode text, itemname text, location text, qty numeric, avgrate numeric, value numeric)
    LANGUAGE sql
    AS $$
  SELECT
    i.item_code,
    i.item_name,
    l.location,

    -- Current stock qty
    SUM(l.qty_in - l.qty_out) AS qty,

    -- Weighted average based ONLY on receipts
    CASE
      WHEN SUM(l.qty_in) = 0 THEN 0
      ELSE
        SUM(
          CASE
            WHEN l.qty_in > 0 THEN l.value
            ELSE 0
          END
        )
        /
        SUM(l.qty_in)
    END AS avgrate,

    -- Stock value = current qty × avg rate
    SUM(l.qty_in - l.qty_out)
    *
    CASE
      WHEN SUM(l.qty_in) = 0 THEN 0
      ELSE
        SUM(
          CASE
            WHEN l.qty_in > 0 THEN l.value
            ELSE 0
          END
        )
        /
        SUM(l.qty_in)
    END AS value

  FROM inv_ledger l
  JOIN inv_items i ON i.id = l.item_id

  WHERE
    (p_location IS NULL OR l.location = p_location)
    AND (
      p_search IS NULL OR
      LOWER(i.item_code || ' ' || i.item_name)
      LIKE '%' || LOWER(p_search) || '%'
    )

  GROUP BY
    i.item_code,
    i.item_name,
    l.location;
$$;


--
-- Name: inv_stock_snapshot_mv(text, text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.inv_stock_snapshot_mv(p_location text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_search text DEFAULT NULL::text) RETURNS TABLE(itemcode text, itemname text, category text, location text, qty numeric, avgrate numeric, value numeric)
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$
  select
    m.item_code::text as itemcode,
    m.item_name::text as itemname,
    coalesce(m.category, '')::text as category,
    coalesce(m.location, '')::text as location,
    coalesce(m.qty, 0)::numeric as qty,
    coalesce(m.avgrate, 0)::numeric as avgrate,
    coalesce(m.value, 0)::numeric as value
  from public.inv_stock_mv m
  where (p_location is null or m.location = p_location)
    and (
      p_search is null
      or m.item_code ilike '%' || p_search || '%'
      or m.item_name ilike '%' || p_search || '%'
      or coalesce(m.category, '') ilike '%' || p_search || '%'
    )
  order by m.item_name;
$$;


--
-- Name: inv_stock_snapshot_ui(text, text, integer); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.inv_stock_snapshot_ui(p_location text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_search text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_limit integer DEFAULT 20000) RETURNS TABLE(itemcode text, itemname text, category text, department text, uom text, location text, qty numeric, avgrate numeric, avg_rate numeric, value numeric, last_movement_at timestamp with time zone, oldest_receipt_at timestamp with time zone, ageing_days integer, movement_class text, next_batch_no text, batch_count integer, avg_lead_time_days numeric, avg_daily_consumption numeric, safety_factor numeric, minimum_stock_level numeric, msl_status text, msl_gap numeric)
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$
  with base_rows as (
    select
      s.item_id,
      s.item_code,
      s.item_name,
      s.category,
      s.department,
      s.uom,
      s.location,
      s.qty,
      s.avg_rate,
      s.value,
      s.last_movement_at
    from public.inv_stock_mv s
    where (p_location is null or trim(p_location) = '' or upper(s.location) = upper(trim(p_location)))
      and upper(coalesce(s.location, '')) <> 'FG'
      and (
        p_search is null or trim(p_search) = '' or
        lower(
          concat_ws(
            ' ',
            s.item_code,
            s.item_name,
            s.category,
            s.department,
            s.uom,
            s.location
          )
        ) like '%' || lower(trim(p_search)) || '%'
      )

    union all

    select
      i.id as item_id,
      i.item_code,
      i.item_name,
      coalesce(i.category, ''::text) as category,
      coalesce(i.department, ''::text) as department,
      coalesce(i.uom, ''::text) as uom,
      coalesce(nullif(upper(trim(p_location)), ''), 'MAIN') as location,
      0::numeric as qty,
      0::numeric as avg_rate,
      0::numeric as value,
      null::timestamptz as last_movement_at
    from public.inv_items i
    where coalesce(i.active, true) = true
      and not exists (
        select 1
        from public.inv_stock_mv s0
        where s0.item_id = i.id
          and upper(coalesce(s0.location, '')) <> 'FG'
          and (p_location is null or trim(p_location) = '' or upper(s0.location) = upper(trim(p_location)))
      )
      and (
        p_search is null or trim(p_search) = '' or
        lower(
          concat_ws(
            ' ',
            i.item_code,
            i.item_name,
            i.category,
            i.department,
            i.uom,
            coalesce(nullif(upper(trim(p_location)), ''), 'MAIN')
          )
        ) like '%' || lower(trim(p_search)) || '%'
      )
  )
  select
    b.item_code as itemcode,
    b.item_name as itemname,
    b.category,
    b.department,
    b.uom,
    b.location,
    b.qty,
    b.avg_rate as avgrate,
    b.avg_rate,
    b.value,
    b.last_movement_at,
    a.oldest_receipt_at,
    a.ageing_days,
    a.movement_class,
    a.next_batch_no,
    a.batch_count,
    a.avg_lead_time_days,
    a.avg_daily_consumption,
    a.safety_factor,
    a.minimum_stock_level,
    case when b.qty < coalesce(a.minimum_stock_level, 0) then 'BELOW_MSL' else 'OK' end as msl_status,
    round((b.qty - coalesce(a.minimum_stock_level, 0))::numeric, 2) as msl_gap
  from base_rows b
  left join public.inv_stock_analytics_mv a
    on a.item_id = b.item_id
   and a.location = b.location
  order by b.item_name asc, b.item_code asc, b.location asc
  limit greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 20000), 1);
$$;


--
-- Name: inv_work_orders_for_issue_ui(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.inv_work_orders_for_issue_ui() RETURNS TABLE(wo_no text, issue_department text, material_status text, material_key text, item_label text, gsm text, deckle_mm numeric, cut_mm numeric, uom text, required_qty numeric, issued_qty numeric, remaining_qty numeric)
    LANGUAGE sql STABLE
    AS $$
  select
    r.wo_number::text as wo_no,
    s.issue_department::text as issue_department,
    s.material_status::text as material_status,
    r.material_key::text,
    r.item_label::text,
    r.gsm::text,
    r.deckle_mm,
    r.cut_mm,
    r.uom::text,
    r.required_qty,
    r.issued_qty,
    r.remaining_qty
  from public.inv_wo_issue_requirement_v r
  join public.inv_wo_issue_status_v s on s.wo_number = r.wo_number
  order by r.wo_number desc, r.item_label asc, r.material_key asc;
$$;


--
-- Name: lock_invoice_lines(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.lock_invoice_lines() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
begin
  if exists (
    select 1 from invoices
    where id = old.invoice_id
      and status = 'POSTED'
  ) then
    raise exception 'Cannot modify invoice lines after posting';
  end if;
  return new;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: prevent_over_billing(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.prevent_over_billing() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  v_order_qty numeric;
  v_billed numeric;
begin
  select qty into v_order_qty
  from sales_order_lines
  where id = new.so_line_id;

  select coalesce(sum(qty),0)
  into v_billed
  from invoice_lines il
  join invoices i on i.id = il.invoice_id
  where il.so_line_id = new.so_line_id
    and i.status <> 'CANCELLED';

  if (v_billed + new.qty) > v_order_qty then
    raise exception
      'Over billing not allowed. Ordered: %, Already billed: %, Trying: %',
      v_order_qty, v_billed, new.qty;
  end if;

  return new;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: refresh_sales_order_line_status(uuid); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.refresh_sales_order_line_status(p_so_line_id uuid) RETURNS void
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  v_order_qty numeric := 0;
  v_dispatched_qty numeric := 0;
  v_billed_qty numeric := 0;
  v_header_status text := 'OPEN';
  v_current_status text := 'OPEN';
  v_current_closed_by text := null;
  v_next_status text := 'OPEN';
  v_next_closed_by text := null;
  v_latest_dispatch_by text := null;
  v_latest_invoice_by text := null;
  v_manual_closed boolean := false;
  v_is_manual_billing boolean := false;
begin
  if p_so_line_id is null then
    return;
  end if;

  select
    coalesce(sol.qty, 0),
    upper(coalesce(so.status, 'OPEN')),
    upper(coalesce(sol.status, 'OPEN')),
    nullif(sol.closed_by, ''),
    (
      upper(coalesce(so.order_prefix, '')) = 'MB'
      or upper(coalesce(so.sales_type, '')) = 'MANUAL_BILLING'
      or coalesce(so.so_number, '') ~* '^MB[/-]'
    )
  into v_order_qty, v_header_status, v_current_status, v_current_closed_by, v_is_manual_billing
  from public.sales_order_lines sol
  join public.sales_orders so
    on so.id = sol.so_id
  where sol.id = p_so_line_id;

  if not found then
    return;
  end if;

  v_manual_closed :=
    v_current_status = 'CLOSED'
    and (
      upper(coalesce(v_current_closed_by, '')) like '%MANUAL%'
      or upper(coalesce(v_current_closed_by, '')) like '%LEGACY%'
    );

  select coalesce(sum(coalesce(dr.dispatch_qty, 0)), 0)
  into v_dispatched_qty
  from public.dispatch_records dr
  where dr.so_line_id = p_so_line_id;

  select nullif(dr.created_by, '')
  into v_latest_dispatch_by
  from public.dispatch_records dr
  where dr.so_line_id = p_so_line_id
  order by coalesce(dr.dispatch_date, dr.created_at) desc nulls last,
           dr.created_at desc nulls last,
           dr.id desc
  limit 1;

  select coalesce(sum(coalesce(il.qty, 0)), 0)
  into v_billed_qty
  from public.invoice_lines il
  left join public.invoices inv
    on inv.id = il.invoice_id
  where il.so_line_id = p_so_line_id
    and upper(coalesce(inv.status, 'DRAFT')) not in ('CANCELLED', 'VOID', 'DELETED')
    and upper(coalesce(inv.document_type, 'INVOICE')) <> 'CHALLAN'
    and coalesce(inv.invoice_no, '') not like 'DC-H/%';

  select nullif(inv.created_by, '')
  into v_latest_invoice_by
  from public.invoice_lines il
  left join public.invoices inv
    on inv.id = il.invoice_id
  where il.so_line_id = p_so_line_id
    and upper(coalesce(inv.status, 'DRAFT')) not in ('CANCELLED', 'VOID', 'DELETED')
    and upper(coalesce(inv.document_type, 'INVOICE')) <> 'CHALLAN'
    and coalesce(inv.invoice_no, '') not like 'DC-H/%'
  order by coalesce(inv.posted_at, inv.created_at, inv.invoice_date::timestamptz) desc nulls last,
           inv.id desc
  limit 1;

  if v_header_status = 'CANCELLED' then
    v_next_status := 'CANCELLED';
    v_next_closed_by := null;
  elsif v_is_manual_billing then
    v_next_status := 'CLOSED';
    v_next_closed_by := coalesce(v_current_closed_by, 'System - manual billing');
  elsif v_manual_closed then
    v_next_status := 'CLOSED';
    v_next_closed_by := v_current_closed_by;
  elsif v_billed_qty >= v_order_qty and v_order_qty > 0 then
    v_next_status := 'CLOSED';
    v_next_closed_by := coalesce(v_latest_invoice_by, 'System - fully billed');
  elsif v_dispatched_qty >= v_order_qty and v_order_qty > 0 then
    v_next_status := 'CLOSED';
    v_next_closed_by := coalesce(v_latest_dispatch_by, 'System - fully dispatched');
  elsif v_dispatched_qty > 0 then
    v_next_status := 'PARTIAL_DISPATCHED';
    v_next_closed_by := null;
  else
    v_next_status := 'OPEN';
    v_next_closed_by := null;
  end if;

  update public.sales_order_lines
  set
    status = v_next_status,
    closed_at = case
      when v_next_status = 'CLOSED' then coalesce(closed_at, now())
      else null
    end,
    closed_by = case
      when v_next_status = 'CLOSED' then coalesce(nullif(closed_by, ''), v_next_closed_by)
      else null
    end,
    status_updated_at = now()
  where id = p_so_line_id
    and (
      upper(coalesce(status, '')) is distinct from v_next_status
      or (v_next_status = 'CLOSED' and closed_at is null)
      or (v_next_status = 'CLOSED' and nullif(closed_by, '') is null and v_next_closed_by is not null)
      or (v_next_status <> 'CLOSED' and closed_at is not null)
      or (v_next_status <> 'CLOSED' and closed_by is not null)
    );
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: refresh_stock_mv(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.refresh_stock_mv() RETURNS void
    LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER
    AS $$
begin
  refresh materialized view public.inv_stock_mv;
  refresh materialized view public.inv_stock_analytics_mv;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: sales_order_line_status_from_dispatch_trg(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.sales_order_line_status_from_dispatch_trg() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
begin
  if tg_op in ('DELETE', 'UPDATE') and old.so_line_id is not null then
    perform public.refresh_sales_order_line_status(old.so_line_id);
  end if;

  if tg_op in ('INSERT', 'UPDATE') and new.so_line_id is not null then
    perform public.refresh_sales_order_line_status(new.so_line_id);
  end if;

  return null;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: sales_order_line_status_from_invoice_line_trg(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.sales_order_line_status_from_invoice_line_trg() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
begin
  if tg_op in ('DELETE', 'UPDATE') and old.so_line_id is not null then
    perform public.refresh_sales_order_line_status(old.so_line_id);
  end if;

  if tg_op in ('INSERT', 'UPDATE') and new.so_line_id is not null then
    perform public.refresh_sales_order_line_status(new.so_line_id);
  end if;

  return null;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: sales_order_line_status_from_invoice_trg(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.sales_order_line_status_from_invoice_trg() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  v_invoice_id uuid;
  v_line record;
begin
  v_invoice_id := case when tg_op = 'DELETE' then old.id else new.id end;

  for v_line in
    select distinct so_line_id
    from public.invoice_lines
    where invoice_id = v_invoice_id
      and so_line_id is not null
  loop
    perform public.refresh_sales_order_line_status(v_line.so_line_id);
  end loop;

  return null;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: sales_order_line_status_from_line_trg(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.sales_order_line_status_from_line_trg() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
begin
  if tg_op in ('INSERT', 'UPDATE') then
    perform public.refresh_sales_order_line_status(new.id);
  end if;

  return null;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: sales_order_line_status_from_order_trg(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.sales_order_line_status_from_order_trg() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
declare
  v_line record;
begin
  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and upper(coalesce(old.status, 'OPEN')) is not distinct from upper(coalesce(new.status, 'OPEN')) then
    return null;
  end if;

  for v_line in
    select id
    from public.sales_order_lines
    where so_id = new.id
  loop
    perform public.refresh_sales_order_line_status(v_line.id);
  end loop;

  return null;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: sales_order_refresh_rollup_by_id(text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.sales_order_refresh_rollup_by_id(p_so_id text) RETURNS void
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
begin
  if p_so_id is null or btrim(p_so_id) = '' then
    return;
  end if;

  if not exists (
    select 1
    from public.sales_orders so
    where so.id::text = p_so_id
  ) then
    delete from public.sales_order_rollup_cache
    where so_id = p_so_id;
    return;
  end if;

  with line_rollup as (
    select
      count(*)::integer as line_count,
      coalesce(sum(coalesce(sol.qty, 0)), 0)::numeric as total_qty,
      max(coalesce(sol.final_delivery, sol.expected_delivery)) as latest_delivery,
      case
        when bool_or(upper(coalesce(sol.accounts_status, '')) = 'REJECTED') then 'REJECTED'
        when bool_or(upper(coalesce(sol.accounts_status, '')) = 'HOLD') then 'HOLD'
        when count(*) > 0 and bool_and(upper(coalesce(sol.accounts_status, 'PENDING')) = 'APPROVED') then 'APPROVED'
        else 'PENDING'
      end as accounts_status,
      case
        when bool_or(upper(coalesce(sol.business_status, '')) = 'REJECTED') then 'REJECTED'
        when bool_or(upper(coalesce(sol.business_status, '')) = 'HOLD') then 'HOLD'
        when count(*) > 0 and bool_and(upper(coalesce(sol.business_status, 'PENDING')) = 'APPROVED') then 'APPROVED'
        else 'PENDING'
      end as business_status
    from public.sales_order_lines sol
    where sol.so_id::text = p_so_id
  ),
  wo_rollup as (
    select
      case
        when exists (
          select 1
          from public.work_order_jobs wj
          join public.sales_orders so2
            on so2.so_number = wj.so_number
          where so2.id::text = p_so_id
          limit 1
        ) then 'CREATED'
        else 'PENDING'
      end as wo_status
  )
  insert into public.sales_order_rollup_cache (
    so_id,
    so_number,
    line_count,
    total_qty,
    latest_delivery,
    accounts_status,
    business_status,
    wo_status,
    is_approval_hold,
    can_edit,
    can_hold,
    can_cancel,
    hold_target,
    hold_label,
    updated_at
  )
  select
    so.id::text as so_id,
    so.so_number,
    coalesce(lr.line_count, 0) as line_count,
    coalesce(lr.total_qty, 0) as total_qty,
    lr.latest_delivery,
    coalesce(lr.accounts_status, nullif(upper(coalesce(so.accounts_approved, '')), ''), 'PENDING') as accounts_status,
    coalesce(lr.business_status, nullif(upper(coalesce(so.business_approved, '')), ''), 'PENDING') as business_status,
    case
      when wr.wo_status = 'CREATED' then 'CREATED'
      else coalesce(nullif(upper(coalesce(so.wo_status, '')), ''), 'PENDING')
    end as wo_status,
    (
      coalesce(lr.accounts_status, nullif(upper(coalesce(so.accounts_approved, '')), ''), 'PENDING') = 'HOLD'
      or coalesce(lr.business_status, nullif(upper(coalesce(so.business_approved, '')), ''), 'PENDING') = 'HOLD'
    ) as is_approval_hold,
    case
      when upper(coalesce(so.status, 'OPEN')) = 'CANCELLED' then false
      when upper(coalesce(so.status, 'OPEN')) = 'HOLD' then true
      when (
        coalesce(lr.accounts_status, nullif(upper(coalesce(so.accounts_approved, '')), ''), 'PENDING') = 'HOLD'
        or coalesce(lr.business_status, nullif(upper(coalesce(so.business_approved, '')), ''), 'PENDING') = 'HOLD'
      ) then true
      when (
        coalesce(lr.accounts_status, nullif(upper(coalesce(so.accounts_approved, '')), ''), 'PENDING') <> 'APPROVED'
        and coalesce(lr.business_status, nullif(upper(coalesce(so.business_approved, '')), ''), 'PENDING') <> 'APPROVED'
      ) then true
      else false
    end as can_edit,
    (upper(coalesce(so.status, 'OPEN')) <> 'CANCELLED') as can_hold,
    (upper(coalesce(so.status, 'OPEN')) <> 'CANCELLED') as can_cancel,
    case
      when upper(coalesce(so.status, 'OPEN')) = 'HOLD' then 'OPEN'
      else 'HOLD'
    end as hold_target,
    case
      when upper(coalesce(so.status, 'OPEN')) = 'HOLD' then 'Reopen'
      else 'Hold'
    end as hold_label,
    now() as updated_at
  from public.sales_orders so
  left join line_rollup lr
    on true
  left join wo_rollup wr
    on true
  where so.id::text = p_so_id
  on conflict (so_id) do update set
    so_number = excluded.so_number,
    line_count = excluded.line_count,
    total_qty = excluded.total_qty,
    latest_delivery = excluded.latest_delivery,
    accounts_status = excluded.accounts_status,
    business_status = excluded.business_status,
    wo_status = excluded.wo_status,
    is_approval_hold = excluded.is_approval_hold,
    can_edit = excluded.can_edit,
    can_hold = excluded.can_hold,
    can_cancel = excluded.can_cancel,
    hold_target = excluded.hold_target,
    hold_label = excluded.hold_label,
    updated_at = excluded.updated_at;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: sales_order_refresh_rollup_by_number(text); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.sales_order_refresh_rollup_by_number(p_so_number text) RETURNS void
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
begin
  if p_so_number is null or btrim(p_so_number) = '' then
    return;
  end if;

  perform public.sales_order_refresh_rollup_by_id(so.id::text)
  from public.sales_orders so
  where so.so_number = p_so_number;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: sales_order_rollup_from_lines_trg(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.sales_order_rollup_from_lines_trg() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
begin
  if tg_op in ('DELETE', 'UPDATE') then
    perform public.sales_order_refresh_rollup_by_id(old.so_id::text);
  end if;

  if tg_op in ('INSERT', 'UPDATE') then
    perform public.sales_order_refresh_rollup_by_id(new.so_id::text);
  end if;

  return null;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: sales_order_rollup_from_sales_orders_trg(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.sales_order_rollup_from_sales_orders_trg() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
begin
  if tg_op = 'DELETE' then
    delete from public.sales_order_rollup_cache
    where so_id = old.id::text;
    return null;
  end if;

  perform public.sales_order_refresh_rollup_by_id(new.id::text);

  if tg_op = 'UPDATE' and old.id::text <> new.id::text then
    delete from public.sales_order_rollup_cache
    where so_id = old.id::text;
  end if;

  return null;
end;
$$;


--
-- Name: sales_order_rollup_from_work_order_jobs_trg(); Type: FUNCTION; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE FUNCTION public.sales_order_rollup_from_work_order_jobs_trg() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    AS $$
begin
  if tg_op in ('DELETE', 'UPDATE') then
    perform public.sales_order_refresh_rollup_by_number(old.so_number);
  end if;

  if tg_op in ('INSERT', 'UPDATE') then
    perform public.sales_order_refresh_rollup_by_number(new.so_number);
  end if;

  return null;
end;
$$;
