--
-- Name: inv_stock_mv; Type: MATERIALIZED VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW public.inv_stock_mv AS
 WITH ledger_balance AS (
         SELECT l.item_id,
            upper(COALESCE(l.location, 'MAIN'::text)) AS location,
            sum((COALESCE(l.qty_in, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(l.qty_out, (0)::numeric))) AS qty,
            max(l.created_at) AS last_movement_at,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (COALESCE(l.qty_in, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN COALESCE(l.value, (0)::numeric)
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS inbound_value,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (COALESCE(l.qty_in, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN COALESCE(l.qty_in, (0)::numeric)
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS inbound_qty
           FROM public.inv_ledger l
          GROUP BY l.item_id, (upper(COALESCE(l.location, 'MAIN'::text)))
        ), lot_balance AS (
         SELECT lt.item_id,
            upper(COALESCE(lt.location, 'MAIN'::text)) AS location,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (COALESCE(lt.qty_available, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN COALESCE(lt.qty_available, (0)::numeric)
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS lot_qty,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (COALESCE(lt.qty_available, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN (COALESCE(lt.qty_available, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(lt.rate, (0)::numeric))
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS lot_value
           FROM public.inv_lots lt
          GROUP BY lt.item_id, (upper(COALESCE(lt.location, 'MAIN'::text)))
        )
 SELECT i.id AS item_id,
    i.item_code,
    i.item_name,
    COALESCE(i.category, ''::text) AS category,
    COALESCE(i.department, ''::text) AS department,
    COALESCE(i.uom, ''::text) AS uom,
    lb.location,
    lb.qty,
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(lotb.lot_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN round((lotb.lot_value / NULLIF(lotb.lot_qty, (0)::numeric)), 6)
            WHEN (COALESCE(lb.inbound_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN round((lb.inbound_value / NULLIF(lb.inbound_qty, (0)::numeric)), 6)
            ELSE (0)::numeric
        END AS avg_rate,
    round((lb.qty *
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(lotb.lot_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN (lotb.lot_value / NULLIF(lotb.lot_qty, (0)::numeric))
            WHEN (COALESCE(lb.inbound_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN (lb.inbound_value / NULLIF(lb.inbound_qty, (0)::numeric))
            ELSE (0)::numeric
        END), 2) AS value,
    lb.last_movement_at
   FROM ((ledger_balance lb
     JOIN public.inv_items i ON ((i.id = lb.item_id)))
     LEFT JOIN lot_balance lotb ON (((lotb.item_id = lb.item_id) AND (lotb.location = lb.location))))
  WHERE (lb.qty <> (0)::numeric)
  WITH NO DATA;


--
-- Name: inv_stock_analytics_mv; Type: MATERIALIZED VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW public.inv_stock_analytics_mv AS
 WITH lot_ranked AS (
         SELECT lt_1.item_id,
            upper(COALESCE(lt_1.location, 'MAIN'::text)) AS location,
            TRIM(BOTH FROM COALESCE(lt_1.batch_no, ''::text)) AS batch_no,
            lt_1.receipt_date,
            lt_1.created_at,
            COALESCE(lt_1.qty_available, (0)::numeric) AS qty_available,
            row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY lt_1.item_id, (upper(COALESCE(lt_1.location, 'MAIN'::text))) ORDER BY lt_1.receipt_date, lt_1.created_at, lt_1.id) AS rn
           FROM public.inv_lots lt_1
          WHERE (COALESCE(lt_1.qty_available, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric)
        ), lot_agg AS (
         SELECT lr.item_id,
            lr.location,
            (count(*))::integer AS batch_count,
            min(lr.receipt_date) AS oldest_receipt_at,
            max(
                CASE
                    WHEN (lr.rn = 1) THEN lr.batch_no
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END) AS next_batch_no
           FROM lot_ranked lr
          GROUP BY lr.item_id, lr.location
        ), issue_30 AS (
         SELECT l.item_id,
            upper(COALESCE(l.location, 'MAIN'::text)) AS location,
            sum(COALESCE(l.qty_out, (0)::numeric)) AS issue_qty_30
           FROM public.inv_ledger l
          WHERE ((l.ref_type = 'ISSUE'::text) AND (l.created_at >= (now() - '30 days'::interval)))
          GROUP BY l.item_id, (upper(COALESCE(l.location, 'MAIN'::text)))
        ), pr_first_receipt AS (
         SELECT l.item_id,
            upper(COALESCE(l.location, 'MAIN'::text)) AS location,
            l.ref_no AS pr_no,
            min(l.created_at) AS first_receipt_at
           FROM public.inv_ledger l
          WHERE ((l.ref_type = 'PR-RECEIPT'::text) AND (COALESCE(l.qty_in, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (l.ref_no IS NOT NULL))
          GROUP BY l.item_id, (upper(COALESCE(l.location, 'MAIN'::text))), l.ref_no
        ), lead_time AS (
         SELECT pr.item_id,
            r.location,
            avg(GREATEST((EXTRACT(epoch FROM (r.first_receipt_at - pr.created_at)) / 86400.0), (0)::numeric)) AS avg_lead_time_days
           FROM (public.inv_purchase_requests pr
             JOIN pr_first_receipt r ON (((r.pr_no = pr.pr_no) AND (r.item_id = pr.item_id))))
          GROUP BY pr.item_id, r.location
        )
 SELECT s.item_id,
    s.location,
    s.last_movement_at,
    la.oldest_receipt_at,
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(la.oldest_receipt_at, s.last_movement_at) IS NULL) THEN NULL::integer
            ELSE (ceil((EXTRACT(epoch FROM (now() - COALESCE(la.oldest_receipt_at, s.last_movement_at))) / 86400.0)))::integer
        END AS ageing_days,
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(la.oldest_receipt_at, s.last_movement_at) IS NULL) THEN 'UNKNOWN'::text
            WHEN (ceil((EXTRACT(epoch FROM (now() - COALESCE(la.oldest_receipt_at, s.last_movement_at))) / 86400.0)) > (60)::numeric) THEN 'NON_MOVING'::text
            WHEN (ceil((EXTRACT(epoch FROM (now() - COALESCE(la.oldest_receipt_at, s.last_movement_at))) / 86400.0)) > (30)::numeric) THEN 'SLOW_MOVING'::text
            ELSE 'FAST_MOVING'::text
        END AS movement_class,
    COALESCE(la.next_batch_no, ''::text) AS next_batch_no,
    COALESCE(la.batch_count, 0) AS batch_count,
    round((COALESCE(i30.issue_qty_30, (0)::numeric) / 30.0), 4) AS avg_daily_consumption,
    round(COALESCE(lt.avg_lead_time_days, (7)::numeric), 2) AS avg_lead_time_days,
    1.2 AS safety_factor,
    round(((COALESCE(lt.avg_lead_time_days, (7)::numeric) * (COALESCE(i30.issue_qty_30, (0)::numeric) / 30.0)) * 1.2), 2) AS minimum_stock_level
   FROM (((public.inv_stock_mv s
     LEFT JOIN lot_agg la ON (((la.item_id = s.item_id) AND (la.location = s.location))))
     LEFT JOIN issue_30 i30 ON (((i30.item_id = s.item_id) AND (i30.location = s.location))))
     LEFT JOIN lead_time lt ON (((lt.item_id = s.item_id) AND (lt.location = s.location))))
  WITH NO DATA;

CREATE TABLE public.work_orders (
    id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
    wo_number text NOT NULL,
    wo_date date,
    status text,
    created_by text,
    created_at timestamp without time zone DEFAULT now(),
    snapshot_json jsonb
);


--
-- Name: inv_wo_issue_requirement_v; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.inv_wo_issue_requirement_v AS
 WITH material_rows AS (
         SELECT wom.wo_id,
            wom.material_key,
            max(wom.item_name) AS item_name,
            max(wom.gsm) AS gsm,
            max(wom.deckle) AS deckle,
            max(wom.cut_size) AS cut_size,
            max(wom.uom) AS uom,
            sum(COALESCE(wom.required_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS required_qty
           FROM public.work_order_materials wom
          GROUP BY wom.wo_id, wom.material_key
        ), issued_rows AS (
         SELECT il.ref_no AS wo_number,
            il.remarks AS material_key,
            sum(COALESCE(il.qty_out, (0)::numeric)) AS issued_qty
           FROM public.inv_ledger il
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(il.ref_type, ''::text)) = 'ISSUE'::text) AND (COALESCE(il.qty_out, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(il.remarks, ''::text) <> ''::text) AND (NOT (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
                   FROM public.inv_ledger rev
                  WHERE ((rev.ref_no = (il.id)::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(rev.ref_type, ''::text)) ~~ 'REV-%'::text))))))
          GROUP BY il.ref_no, il.remarks
        ), wo_base AS (
         SELECT wo.id,
            wo.wo_number,
            wo.status,
            wo.snapshot_json,
            NULLIF(upper(TRIM(BOTH FROM COALESCE((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{flexoDetails,itemCode}'::text[]), ''::text))), ''::text) AS flexo_item_code,
            NULLIF(upper(TRIM(BOTH FROM COALESCE((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{flexoDetails,itemName}'::text[]), ''::text))), ''::text) AS flexo_item_name,
            COALESCE((NULLIF((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{flexoDetails,totalRunningMeter}'::text[]), ''::text))::numeric, (0)::numeric) AS flexo_running_meter,
            COALESCE(NULLIF((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{jobDetails,department}'::text[]), ''::text), NULLIF((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{department}'::text[]), ''::text), NULLIF((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{departmentCategory}'::text[]), ''::text), NULLIF((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{jobDetails,category}'::text[]), ''::text), NULLIF((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{jobDetails,type}'::text[]), ''::text), ''::text) AS raw_department
           FROM public.work_orders wo
          WHERE (COALESCE(upper(wo.status), ''::text) <> 'CLOSED'::text)
        ), prepared AS (
         SELECT wb.id AS wo_id,
            wb.wo_number,
            wb.status,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(wb.raw_department) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'Flexo'::text
                    WHEN (upper(wb.raw_department) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'Offset'::text
                    WHEN (upper(wb.raw_department) = 'FLAT'::text) THEN 'Offset'::text
                    WHEN (upper(wb.raw_department) ~~ '%FLAT%'::text) THEN 'Offset'::text
                    WHEN (upper(wb.raw_department) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'Digital'::text
                    WHEN (upper(wb.raw_department) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'Corrugation'::text
                    ELSE 'Offset'::text
                END AS issue_department,
            mr.material_key,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(mr.item_name, ''::text), NULLIF(split_part(mr.material_key, '|'::text, 1), ''::text), ''::text) AS item_label,
            mr.gsm,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(mr.deckle, (0)::numeric), NULLIF((NULLIF(split_part(mr.material_key, '|'::text, 3), ''::text))::numeric, (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric) AS deckle_mm,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(mr.cut_size, (0)::numeric), NULLIF((NULLIF(split_part(mr.material_key, '|'::text, 4), ''::text))::numeric, (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric) AS cut_mm,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((upper(wb.raw_department) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) AND (wb.flexo_running_meter > (0)::numeric) AND ((upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(mr.item_name, ''::text), NULLIF(split_part(mr.material_key, '|'::text, 1), ''::text), ''::text)) = COALESCE(wb.flexo_item_code, '##'::text)) OR (upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(mr.item_name, ''::text), NULLIF(split_part(mr.material_key, '|'::text, 1), ''::text), ''::text)) = COALESCE(wb.flexo_item_name, '##'::text)))) THEN 'RM'::text
                    ELSE COALESCE(NULLIF(mr.uom, ''::text), NULLIF(split_part(mr.material_key, '|'::text, 5), ''::text), ''::text)
                END AS uom,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((upper(wb.raw_department) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) AND (wb.flexo_running_meter > (0)::numeric) AND ((upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(mr.item_name, ''::text), NULLIF(split_part(mr.material_key, '|'::text, 1), ''::text), ''::text)) = COALESCE(wb.flexo_item_code, '##'::text)) OR (upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(mr.item_name, ''::text), NULLIF(split_part(mr.material_key, '|'::text, 1), ''::text), ''::text)) = COALESCE(wb.flexo_item_name, '##'::text)))) THEN wb.flexo_running_meter
                    ELSE COALESCE(mr.required_qty, (0)::numeric)
                END AS required_qty,
            COALESCE(ir.issued_qty, (0)::numeric) AS issued_qty
           FROM ((wo_base wb
             JOIN material_rows mr ON ((mr.wo_id = wb.id)))
             LEFT JOIN issued_rows ir ON (((ir.wo_number = wb.wo_number) AND (ir.material_key = mr.material_key))))
        )
 SELECT wo_id,
    wo_number,
    status,
    issue_department,
    material_key,
    item_label,
    gsm,
    deckle_mm,
    cut_mm,
    uom,
    required_qty,
    issued_qty,
    GREATEST((required_qty - issued_qty), (0)::numeric) AS remaining_qty,
        CASE
            WHEN (GREATEST((required_qty - issued_qty), (0)::numeric) > 0.0001) THEN 'PENDING'::text
            ELSE 'ISSUED'::text
        END AS line_status
   FROM prepared;


--
-- Name: inv_wo_issue_status_v; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.inv_wo_issue_status_v AS
 SELECT wo_number,
    max(issue_department) AS issue_department,
        CASE
            WHEN bool_or((line_status = 'PENDING'::text)) THEN 'PENDING'::text
            ELSE 'ISSUED'::text
        END AS material_status,
    sum(required_qty) AS required_qty,
    sum(issued_qty) AS issued_qty,
    sum(remaining_qty) AS remaining_qty
   FROM public.inv_wo_issue_requirement_v
  GROUP BY wo_number;

--
-- Name: sales_orders_list; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.sales_orders_list WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 SELECT so.id,
    so.so_number,
    so.so_date,
    so.client_code,
    c.client_name,
    so.sales_rep,
    so.po_number,
    so.sales_type,
    so.currency,
    so.subtotal,
    so.discount_total,
    so.cgst_total,
    so.sgst_total,
    so.igst_total,
    so.grand_total,
    so.remarks,
    so.status,
    l.accounts_status,
    l.business_status,
    so.wo_status,
    l.product_code,
    l.product_name,
    l.product_remarks,
    COALESCE(so.advance_payment_required, false) AS advance_payment_required,
    COALESCE(so.advance_payment_received, false) AS advance_payment_received
   FROM ((public.sales_orders so
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
     LEFT JOIN public.sales_order_lines l ON (((l.so_id = so.id) AND (l.line_no = 1))));


--
-- Name: so_seq_m; Type: SEQUENCE; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE SEQUENCE public.so_seq_m
    START WITH 1
    INCREMENT BY 1
    NO MINVALUE
    NO MAXVALUE
    CACHE 1;


--
-- Name: so_seq_sl; Type: SEQUENCE; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE SEQUENCE public.so_seq_sl
    START WITH 1
    INCREMENT BY 1
    NO MINVALUE
    NO MAXVALUE
    CACHE 1;


--
-- Name: users; Type: TABLE; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE public.users (
    id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
    user_id text NOT NULL,
    password_hash text NOT NULL,
    display_name text,
    role text,
    active boolean DEFAULT true,
    created_at timestamp without time zone DEFAULT now(),
    role_id uuid
);


--
-- Name: v_artwork_jobs; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_artwork_jobs WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 SELECT a.id,
    so.so_number,
    so.so_date,
    so.created_at AS so_created_at,
    to_char(COALESCE(((so.created_at AT TIME ZONE 'UTC'::text) AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kolkata'::text), (so.so_date)::timestamp without time zone), 'HH24:MI'::text) AS so_time,
    so.sales_rep,
    sol.line_no,
    c.client_name,
    sol.product_code,
    sol.product_name,
    sol.category,
    sol.division,
    sol.qty,
    sol.unit,
    a.artwork_no,
    a.product_type,
    a.plate_status,
    a.die_status,
    a.plate_size,
    a.plate_count,
    a.has_hybrid_plate,
    a.hybrid_plate_size,
    a.hybrid_plate_count,
    a.die_count,
    a.sheet_length,
    a.sheet_width,
    a.sheet_ups,
    a.printing_colors,
    a.across_ups,
    a.along_ups,
    a.total_ups,
    a.across_width,
    a.teeth,
    a.across_gap_mm,
    a.along_gap_mm,
    a.status,
    a.artwork_at,
    a.approved_at,
    sol.accounts_status,
    sol.business_status,
    COALESCE(a.stock_qty_to_bill, (0)::numeric) AS stock_qty_to_bill,
    COALESCE(sol.job_type, ''::text) AS job_type
   FROM (((public.artworks a
     JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON (((sol.so_id = a.so_id) AND (sol.line_no = a.line_no))))
     JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = a.so_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)));


--
-- Name: v_artwork_groups; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_artwork_groups AS
 WITH base AS (
         SELECT v_artwork_jobs.id,
            v_artwork_jobs.so_number,
            v_artwork_jobs.so_date,
            v_artwork_jobs.so_created_at,
            v_artwork_jobs.so_time,
            v_artwork_jobs.sales_rep,
            v_artwork_jobs.line_no,
            v_artwork_jobs.client_name,
            v_artwork_jobs.product_code,
            v_artwork_jobs.product_name,
            v_artwork_jobs.category,
            v_artwork_jobs.division,
            v_artwork_jobs.qty,
            v_artwork_jobs.unit,
            v_artwork_jobs.artwork_no,
            v_artwork_jobs.product_type,
            v_artwork_jobs.plate_status,
            v_artwork_jobs.die_status,
            v_artwork_jobs.plate_size,
            v_artwork_jobs.plate_count,
            v_artwork_jobs.has_hybrid_plate,
            v_artwork_jobs.hybrid_plate_size,
            v_artwork_jobs.hybrid_plate_count,
            v_artwork_jobs.die_count,
            v_artwork_jobs.sheet_length,
            v_artwork_jobs.sheet_width,
            v_artwork_jobs.sheet_ups,
            v_artwork_jobs.printing_colors,
            v_artwork_jobs.across_ups,
            v_artwork_jobs.along_ups,
            v_artwork_jobs.total_ups,
            v_artwork_jobs.across_width,
            v_artwork_jobs.teeth,
            v_artwork_jobs.across_gap_mm,
            v_artwork_jobs.along_gap_mm,
            v_artwork_jobs.status,
            v_artwork_jobs.artwork_at,
            v_artwork_jobs.approved_at,
            v_artwork_jobs.accounts_status,
            v_artwork_jobs.business_status,
            v_artwork_jobs.stock_qty_to_bill,
            v_artwork_jobs.job_type
           FROM public.v_artwork_jobs
          WHERE (NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM v_artwork_jobs.artwork_no), ''::text) IS NOT NULL)
        ), rollup AS (
         SELECT b.artwork_no,
            min(b.so_date) AS so_date,
            max(NULLIF(b.product_type, ''::text)) AS product_type,
            max(NULLIF(b.plate_status, ''::text)) AS plate_status,
            max(NULLIF(b.die_status, ''::text)) AS die_status,
            max(NULLIF(b.plate_size, ''::text)) AS plate_size,
            max(b.plate_count) AS plate_count,
            bool_or(COALESCE(b.has_hybrid_plate, false)) AS has_hybrid_plate,
            max(NULLIF(b.hybrid_plate_size, ''::text)) AS hybrid_plate_size,
            max(b.hybrid_plate_count) AS hybrid_plate_count,
            max(b.die_count) AS die_count,
            max(b.sheet_length) AS sheet_length,
            max(b.sheet_width) AS sheet_width,
            max(NULLIF(b.printing_colors, ''::text)) AS printing_colors,
            max(b.across_ups) AS across_ups,
            max(b.along_ups) AS along_ups,
            max(b.total_ups) AS total_ups,
            max(b.across_width) AS across_width,
            max(b.teeth) AS teeth,
            max(b.across_gap_mm) AS across_gap_mm,
            max(b.along_gap_mm) AS along_gap_mm,
                CASE
                    WHEN bool_and((upper(COALESCE(b.status, 'NO_ART'::text)) = 'APPROVED'::text)) THEN 'APPROVED'::text
                    WHEN bool_or((upper(COALESCE(b.status, 'NO_ART'::text)) = 'PENDING_APPROVAL'::text)) THEN 'PENDING_APPROVAL'::text
                    WHEN bool_or((upper(COALESCE(b.status, 'NO_ART'::text)) = 'APPROVED'::text)) THEN 'PENDING_APPROVAL'::text
                    ELSE 'NO_ART'::text
                END AS status,
            min(b.artwork_at) AS artwork_at,
            max(b.approved_at) AS approved_at,
            count(*) AS job_count,
            COALESCE(sum(COALESCE(b.qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS total_qty,
            min(COALESCE(b.sheet_ups, (0)::numeric)) AS min_sheet_ups,
            array_remove(array_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(b.so_number, ''::text) ORDER BY NULLIF(b.so_number, ''::text)), NULL::text) AS so_list,
            array_remove(array_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(b.client_name, ''::text) ORDER BY NULLIF(b.client_name, ''::text)), NULL::text) AS client_list,
            array_remove(array_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(b.division, ''::text) ORDER BY NULLIF(b.division, ''::text)), NULL::text) AS division_list,
            array_remove(array_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(b.sales_rep, ''::text) ORDER BY NULLIF(b.sales_rep, ''::text)), NULL::text) AS sales_rep_list,
            array_remove(array_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(b.job_type, ''::text) ORDER BY NULLIF(b.job_type, ''::text)), NULL::text) AS job_type_list
           FROM base b
          GROUP BY b.artwork_no
        )
 SELECT artwork_no,
    so_date,
    product_type,
    plate_status,
    die_status,
    plate_size,
    plate_count,
    has_hybrid_plate,
    hybrid_plate_size,
    hybrid_plate_count,
    die_count,
    sheet_length,
    sheet_width,
    printing_colors,
    across_ups,
    along_ups,
    total_ups,
    across_width,
    teeth,
    across_gap_mm,
    along_gap_mm,
    status,
    artwork_at,
    approved_at,
    job_count,
    total_qty,
    so_list,
    client_list,
    division_list,
    sales_rep_list,
    job_type_list,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(product_type, ''::text)) = 'DIGITAL'::text) THEN ((product_type IS NOT NULL) AND (min_sheet_ups > (0)::numeric))
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(product_type, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) THEN ((product_type IS NOT NULL) AND (plate_status IS NOT NULL) AND (die_status IS NOT NULL) AND (printing_colors IS NOT NULL) AND (COALESCE(across_ups, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(along_ups, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(total_ups, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(across_width, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(teeth, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(across_gap_mm, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(along_gap_mm, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND ((upper(COALESCE(plate_status, ''::text)) <> 'NEW'::text) OR (COALESCE(plate_count, 0) > 0)) AND ((upper(COALESCE(die_status, ''::text)) <> 'NEW'::text) OR (COALESCE(die_count, 0) > 0)) AND (min_sheet_ups > (0)::numeric))
            ELSE ((product_type IS NOT NULL) AND (plate_status IS NOT NULL) AND (die_status IS NOT NULL) AND (printing_colors IS NOT NULL) AND (COALESCE(sheet_length, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(sheet_width, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND ((upper(COALESCE(plate_status, ''::text)) <> 'NEW'::text) OR ((plate_size IS NOT NULL) AND (COALESCE(plate_count, 0) > 0) AND ((NOT has_hybrid_plate) OR ((hybrid_plate_size IS NOT NULL) AND (COALESCE(hybrid_plate_count, 0) > 0))))) AND ((upper(COALESCE(die_status, ''::text)) <> 'NEW'::text) OR (COALESCE(die_count, 0) > 0)) AND (min_sheet_ups > (0)::numeric))
        END AS is_ready
   FROM rollup;


--
-- Name: v_artwork_jobs_active; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_artwork_jobs_active AS
 SELECT aj.id,
    aj.so_number,
    aj.so_date,
    aj.so_created_at,
    aj.so_time,
    aj.sales_rep,
    aj.line_no,
    aj.client_name,
    aj.product_code,
    aj.product_name,
    aj.category,
    aj.division,
    aj.qty,
    aj.unit,
    aj.artwork_no,
    aj.product_type,
    aj.plate_status,
    aj.die_status,
    aj.plate_size,
    aj.plate_count,
    aj.has_hybrid_plate,
    aj.hybrid_plate_size,
    aj.hybrid_plate_count,
    aj.die_count,
    aj.sheet_length,
    aj.sheet_width,
    aj.sheet_ups,
    aj.printing_colors,
    aj.across_ups,
    aj.along_ups,
    aj.total_ups,
    aj.across_width,
    aj.teeth,
    aj.across_gap_mm,
    aj.along_gap_mm,
    aj.status,
    aj.artwork_at,
    aj.approved_at,
    aj.accounts_status,
    aj.business_status,
    aj.stock_qty_to_bill,
    aj.job_type
   FROM ((public.v_artwork_jobs aj
     JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.so_number = aj.so_number)))
     JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON (((sol.so_id = so.id) AND ((sol.line_no)::text = (aj.line_no)::text))))
  WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['CLOSED'::text, 'CANCELLED'::text])));


--
-- Name: v_artwork_groups_active; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -

CREATE VIEW public.v_artwork_groups_active AS
 WITH base AS (
         SELECT v_artwork_jobs_active.id,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.so_number,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.so_date,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.so_created_at,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.so_time,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.sales_rep,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.line_no,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.client_name,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.product_code,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.product_name,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.category,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.division,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.qty,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.unit,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.artwork_no,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.product_type,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.plate_status,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.die_status,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.plate_size,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.plate_count,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.has_hybrid_plate,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.hybrid_plate_size,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.hybrid_plate_count,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.die_count,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.sheet_length,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.sheet_width,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.sheet_ups,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.printing_colors,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.across_ups,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.along_ups,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.total_ups,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.across_width,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.teeth,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.across_gap_mm,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.along_gap_mm,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.status,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.artwork_at,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.approved_at,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.accounts_status,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.business_status,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.stock_qty_to_bill,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.job_type
           FROM public.v_artwork_jobs_active
          WHERE (NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM v_artwork_jobs_active.artwork_no), ''::text) IS NOT NULL)
        ), rollup AS (
         SELECT b.artwork_no,
            min(b.so_date) AS so_date,
            max(NULLIF(b.product_type, ''::text)) AS product_type,
            max(NULLIF(b.plate_status, ''::text)) AS plate_status,
            max(NULLIF(b.die_status, ''::text)) AS die_status,
            max(NULLIF(b.plate_size, ''::text)) AS plate_size,
            max(b.plate_count) AS plate_count,
            bool_or(COALESCE(b.has_hybrid_plate, false)) AS has_hybrid_plate,
            max(NULLIF(b.hybrid_plate_size, ''::text)) AS hybrid_plate_size,
            max(b.hybrid_plate_count) AS hybrid_plate_count,
            max(b.die_count) AS die_count,
            max(b.sheet_length) AS sheet_length,
            max(b.sheet_width) AS sheet_width,
            max(NULLIF(b.printing_colors, ''::text)) AS printing_colors,
            max(b.across_ups) AS across_ups,
            max(b.along_ups) AS along_ups,
            max(b.total_ups) AS total_ups,
            max(b.across_width) AS across_width,
            max(b.teeth) AS teeth,
            max(b.across_gap_mm) AS across_gap_mm,
            max(b.along_gap_mm) AS along_gap_mm,
                CASE
                    WHEN bool_and((upper(COALESCE(b.status, 'NO_ART'::text)) = 'APPROVED'::text)) THEN 'APPROVED'::text
                    WHEN bool_or((upper(COALESCE(b.status, 'NO_ART'::text)) = 'PENDING_APPROVAL'::text)) THEN 'PENDING_APPROVAL'::text
                    WHEN bool_or((upper(COALESCE(b.status, 'NO_ART'::text)) = 'APPROVED'::text)) THEN 'PENDING_APPROVAL'::text
                    ELSE 'NO_ART'::text
                END AS status,
            min(b.artwork_at) AS artwork_at,
            max(b.approved_at) AS approved_at,
            count(*) AS job_count,
            COALESCE(sum(COALESCE(b.qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS total_qty,
            min(COALESCE(b.sheet_ups, (0)::numeric)) AS min_sheet_ups,
            array_remove(array_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(b.so_number, ''::text) ORDER BY NULLIF(b.so_number, ''::text)), NULL::text) AS so_list,
            array_remove(array_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(b.client_name, ''::text) ORDER BY NULLIF(b.client_name, ''::text)), NULL::text) AS client_list,
            array_remove(array_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(b.division, ''::text) ORDER BY NULLIF(b.division, ''::text)), NULL::text) AS division_list,
            array_remove(array_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(b.sales_rep, ''::text) ORDER BY NULLIF(b.sales_rep, ''::text)), NULL::text) AS sales_rep_list,
            array_remove(array_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(b.job_type, ''::text) ORDER BY NULLIF(b.job_type, ''::text)), NULL::text) AS job_type_list
           FROM base b
          GROUP BY b.artwork_no
        )
 SELECT artwork_no,
    so_date,
    product_type,
    plate_status,
    die_status,
    plate_size,
    plate_count,
    has_hybrid_plate,
    hybrid_plate_size,
    hybrid_plate_count,
    die_count,
    sheet_length,
    sheet_width,
    printing_colors,
    across_ups,
    along_ups,
    total_ups,
    across_width,
    teeth,
    across_gap_mm,
    along_gap_mm,
    status,
    artwork_at,
    approved_at,
    job_count,
    total_qty,
    so_list,
    client_list,
    division_list,
    sales_rep_list,
    job_type_list,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(product_type, ''::text)) = 'DIGITAL'::text) THEN ((product_type IS NOT NULL) AND (min_sheet_ups > (0)::numeric))
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(product_type, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) THEN ((product_type IS NOT NULL) AND (plate_status IS NOT NULL) AND (die_status IS NOT NULL) AND (printing_colors IS NOT NULL) AND (COALESCE(across_ups, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(along_ups, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(total_ups, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(across_width, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(teeth, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(across_gap_mm, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(along_gap_mm, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND ((upper(COALESCE(plate_status, ''::text)) <> 'NEW'::text) OR (COALESCE(plate_count, 0) > 0)) AND ((upper(COALESCE(die_status, ''::text)) <> 'NEW'::text) OR (COALESCE(die_count, 0) > 0)) AND (min_sheet_ups > (0)::numeric))
            ELSE ((product_type IS NOT NULL) AND (plate_status IS NOT NULL) AND (die_status IS NOT NULL) AND (printing_colors IS NOT NULL) AND (COALESCE(sheet_length, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(sheet_width, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND ((upper(COALESCE(plate_status, ''::text)) <> 'NEW'::text) OR ((plate_size IS NOT NULL) AND (COALESCE(plate_count, 0) > 0) AND ((NOT has_hybrid_plate) OR ((hybrid_plate_size IS NOT NULL) AND (COALESCE(hybrid_plate_count, 0) > 0))))) AND ((upper(COALESCE(die_status, ''::text)) <> 'NEW'::text) OR (COALESCE(die_count, 0) > 0)) AND (min_sheet_ups > (0)::numeric))
        END AS is_ready
   FROM rollup;


--
-- Name: v_artwork_reference; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_artwork_reference WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 WITH artwork_rollup AS (
         SELECT a.artwork_no,
            max(NULLIF(a.product_type, ''::text)) AS product_type,
            max(NULLIF(a.plate_status, ''::text)) AS plate_status,
            max(NULLIF(a.die_status, ''::text)) AS die_status,
            max(NULLIF(a.plate_size, ''::text)) AS plate_size,
            max(a.plate_count) AS plate_count,
            bool_or(COALESCE(a.has_hybrid_plate, false)) AS has_hybrid_plate,
            max(NULLIF(a.hybrid_plate_size, ''::text)) AS hybrid_plate_size,
            max(a.hybrid_plate_count) AS hybrid_plate_count,
            max(a.die_count) AS die_count,
            max(a.sheet_length) AS sheet_length,
            max(a.sheet_width) AS sheet_width,
            max(NULLIF(a.printing_colors, ''::text)) AS printing_colors,
            max(a.across_ups) AS across_ups,
            max(a.along_ups) AS along_ups,
            max(a.total_ups) AS total_ups,
            max(a.across_width) AS across_width,
            max(a.teeth) AS teeth,
            max(a.across_gap_mm) AS across_gap_mm,
            max(a.along_gap_mm) AS along_gap_mm,
                CASE
                    WHEN bool_and((upper(COALESCE(a.status, 'NO_ART'::text)) = 'APPROVED'::text)) THEN 'APPROVED'::text
                    WHEN bool_or((upper(COALESCE(a.status, 'NO_ART'::text)) = 'PENDING_APPROVAL'::text)) THEN 'PENDING_APPROVAL'::text
                    WHEN bool_or((upper(COALESCE(a.status, 'NO_ART'::text)) = 'APPROVED'::text)) THEN 'PENDING_APPROVAL'::text
                    ELSE 'NO_ART'::text
                END AS status,
            min(a.artwork_at) AS artwork_at,
            max(a.approved_at) AS approved_at,
            count(*) AS job_count,
            COALESCE(sum(COALESCE(a.stock_qty_to_bill, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS stock_qty_to_bill
           FROM public.artworks a
          WHERE (NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM a.artwork_no), ''::text) IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY a.artwork_no
        ), proc_rollup AS (
         SELECT COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM p.artwork_no), ''::text), NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM p.artwork_key), ''::text)) AS artwork_no,
            max(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(p.type, ''::text)) = 'PLATE'::text) THEN p.plate_size
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END) AS plate_size,
            max(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(p.type, ''::text)) = 'PLATE'::text) THEN p.plate_count
                    ELSE NULL::numeric
                END) AS plate_count,
            bool_or(
                CASE
                    WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(p.type, ''::text)) = 'PLATE'::text) AND ((p.hybrid_plate_count IS NOT NULL) OR (NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM p.hybrid_plate_size), ''::text) IS NOT NULL))) THEN true
                    ELSE false
                END) AS has_hybrid_plate,
            max(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(p.type, ''::text)) = 'PLATE'::text) THEN p.hybrid_plate_size
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END) AS hybrid_plate_size,
            max(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(p.type, ''::text)) = 'PLATE'::text) THEN p.hybrid_plate_count
                    ELSE NULL::numeric
                END) AS hybrid_plate_count,
            max(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(p.type, ''::text)) = 'DIE'::text) THEN p.die_count
                    ELSE NULL::numeric
                END) AS die_count
           FROM public.purchase_artwork_procurement p
          WHERE (COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM p.artwork_no), ''::text), NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM p.artwork_key), ''::text)) IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM p.artwork_no), ''::text), NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM p.artwork_key), ''::text))
        )
 SELECT ar.artwork_no,
    COALESCE(ar.product_type, ''::text) AS product_type,
    COALESCE(ar.plate_status, ''::text) AS plate_status,
    COALESCE(ar.die_status, ''::text) AS die_status,
    COALESCE(pr.plate_size, ar.plate_size, ''::text) AS plate_size,
    COALESCE(pr.plate_count, (ar.plate_count)::numeric, (0)::numeric) AS plate_count,
    COALESCE(pr.has_hybrid_plate, ar.has_hybrid_plate, false) AS has_hybrid_plate,
    COALESCE(pr.hybrid_plate_size, ar.hybrid_plate_size, '770X1030'::text) AS hybrid_plate_size,
    COALESCE(pr.hybrid_plate_count, (ar.hybrid_plate_count)::numeric, (0)::numeric) AS hybrid_plate_count,
    COALESCE(pr.die_count, (ar.die_count)::numeric, (0)::numeric) AS die_count,
    COALESCE(ar.sheet_length, (0)::numeric) AS sheet_length,
    COALESCE(ar.sheet_width, (0)::numeric) AS sheet_width,
    COALESCE(ar.printing_colors, ''::text) AS printing_colors,
    COALESCE(ar.across_ups, (0)::numeric) AS across_ups,
    COALESCE(ar.along_ups, (0)::numeric) AS along_ups,
    COALESCE(ar.total_ups, (0)::numeric) AS total_ups,
    COALESCE(ar.across_width, (0)::numeric) AS across_width,
    COALESCE(ar.teeth, (0)::numeric) AS teeth,
    COALESCE(ar.across_gap_mm, (0)::numeric) AS across_gap_mm,
    COALESCE(ar.along_gap_mm, (0)::numeric) AS along_gap_mm,
    COALESCE(ar.status, 'NO_ART'::text) AS status,
    ar.artwork_at,
    ar.approved_at,
    ar.job_count,
    COALESCE(ar.stock_qty_to_bill, (0)::numeric) AS stock_qty_to_bill
   FROM (artwork_rollup ar
     LEFT JOIN proc_rollup pr ON ((pr.artwork_no = ar.artwork_no)));


--
-- Name: v_fg_stock_available; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_fg_stock_available AS
 WITH invoice_usage AS (
         SELECT il.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS billed_qty
           FROM (public.invoice_lines il
             LEFT JOIN public.invoices i ON ((i.id = il.invoice_id)))
          WHERE (COALESCE(upper(i.status), ''::text) <> 'CANCELLED'::text)
          GROUP BY il.so_line_id
        ), fg_adjustments AS (
         SELECT fg_stock_adjustments.pack_id,
            fg_stock_adjustments.opening_id,
            fg_stock_adjustments.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(fg_stock_adjustments.adjustment_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS adjusted_qty,
            (array_agg(fg_stock_adjustments.reason ORDER BY fg_stock_adjustments.adjustment_date DESC NULLS LAST, fg_stock_adjustments.created_at DESC))[1] AS latest_adjustment_reason,
            (array_agg(fg_stock_adjustments.remarks ORDER BY fg_stock_adjustments.adjustment_date DESC NULLS LAST, fg_stock_adjustments.created_at DESC))[1] AS latest_adjustment_remarks,
            max(COALESCE(fg_stock_adjustments.adjustment_date, fg_stock_adjustments.created_at)) AS latest_adjustment_at
           FROM public.fg_stock_adjustments
          GROUP BY fg_stock_adjustments.pack_id, fg_stock_adjustments.opening_id, fg_stock_adjustments.so_line_id
        ), packed_fg AS (
         SELECT ('PACKED::'::text || (pr.id)::text) AS row_id,
            'PACKED'::text AS source_type,
            pr.id AS pack_id,
            NULL::uuid AS opening_id,
            pr.so_id,
            pr.so_line_id,
            pr.so_number,
            (pr.line_no)::text AS line_no,
            so.client_code,
            COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code) AS client_name,
            COALESCE(sol.product_code, pr.product_code) AS product_code,
            COALESCE(sol.product_name, pr.product_name) AS product_name,
            COALESCE(sol.unit, 'Pcs'::text) AS uom,
            (0)::numeric AS opening_qty,
            COALESCE(pr.packed_qty, (0)::numeric) AS packed_qty,
            COALESCE(iu.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) AS billed_qty,
            COALESCE(adj.adjusted_qty, (0)::numeric) AS adjusted_qty,
            GREATEST(((COALESCE(pr.packed_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(iu.billed_qty, (0)::numeric)) - COALESCE(adj.adjusted_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS available_qty,
            GREATEST(((COALESCE(pr.packed_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(iu.billed_qty, (0)::numeric)) - COALESCE(adj.adjusted_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS billable_qty,
            (pr.packed_at)::timestamp with time zone AS stock_date,
            adj.latest_adjustment_reason,
            adj.latest_adjustment_remarks,
            adj.latest_adjustment_at,
            sol.product_remarks AS remarks
           FROM (((((public.packing_records pr
             LEFT JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = pr.so_id)))
             LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
             LEFT JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON ((sol.id = pr.so_line_id)))
             LEFT JOIN invoice_usage iu ON ((iu.so_line_id = pr.so_line_id)))
             LEFT JOIN fg_adjustments adj ON ((adj.pack_id = pr.id)))
          WHERE (COALESCE(pr.packed_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric)
        ), opening_fg AS (
         SELECT ('OPENING::'::text || (os.id)::text) AS row_id,
            'OPENING'::text AS source_type,
            NULL::uuid AS pack_id,
            os.id AS opening_id,
            NULL::uuid AS so_id,
            NULL::uuid AS so_line_id,
            ''::text AS so_number,
            ''::text AS line_no,
            os.client_code,
            COALESCE(os.client_name, os.client_code) AS client_name,
            os.product_code,
            os.product_name,
            COALESCE(os.uom, 'Pcs'::text) AS uom,
            COALESCE(os.opening_qty, (0)::numeric) AS opening_qty,
            (0)::numeric AS packed_qty,
            (0)::numeric AS billed_qty,
            COALESCE(adj.adjusted_qty, (0)::numeric) AS adjusted_qty,
            GREATEST((COALESCE(os.opening_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(adj.adjusted_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS available_qty,
            (0)::numeric AS billable_qty,
            os.opening_date AS stock_date,
            adj.latest_adjustment_reason,
            adj.latest_adjustment_remarks,
            adj.latest_adjustment_at,
            os.remarks
           FROM (public.fg_opening_stock os
             LEFT JOIN fg_adjustments adj ON ((adj.opening_id = os.id)))
        )
 SELECT packed_fg.row_id,
    packed_fg.source_type,
    packed_fg.pack_id,
    packed_fg.opening_id,
    packed_fg.so_id,
    packed_fg.so_line_id,
    packed_fg.so_number,
    packed_fg.line_no,
    packed_fg.client_code,
    packed_fg.client_name,
    packed_fg.product_code,
    packed_fg.product_name,
    packed_fg.uom,
    packed_fg.opening_qty,
    packed_fg.packed_qty,
    packed_fg.billed_qty,
    packed_fg.adjusted_qty,
    packed_fg.available_qty,
    packed_fg.billable_qty,
    packed_fg.stock_date,
    packed_fg.latest_adjustment_reason,
    packed_fg.latest_adjustment_remarks,
    packed_fg.latest_adjustment_at,
    packed_fg.remarks
   FROM packed_fg
UNION ALL
 SELECT opening_fg.row_id,
    opening_fg.source_type,
    opening_fg.pack_id,
    opening_fg.opening_id,
    opening_fg.so_id,
    opening_fg.so_line_id,
    opening_fg.so_number,
    opening_fg.line_no,
    opening_fg.client_code,
    opening_fg.client_name,
    opening_fg.product_code,
    opening_fg.product_name,
    opening_fg.uom,
    opening_fg.opening_qty,
    opening_fg.packed_qty,
    opening_fg.billed_qty,
    opening_fg.adjusted_qty,
    opening_fg.available_qty,
    opening_fg.billable_qty,
    opening_fg.stock_date,
    opening_fg.latest_adjustment_reason,
    opening_fg.latest_adjustment_remarks,
    opening_fg.latest_adjustment_at,
    opening_fg.remarks
   FROM opening_fg;


--
-- Name: v_artwork_workbench_active; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_artwork_workbench_active WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 WITH stock_by_product AS (
         SELECT v_fg_stock_available.product_code,
            sum(COALESCE(v_fg_stock_available.available_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS fg_stock_qty
           FROM public.v_fg_stock_available
          WHERE (NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM v_fg_stock_available.product_code), ''::text) IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY v_fg_stock_available.product_code
        )
 SELECT aj.id,
    aj.so_number,
    aj.so_date,
    aj.so_created_at,
    aj.so_time,
    aj.sales_rep,
    aj.line_no,
    aj.client_name,
    aj.product_code,
    aj.product_name,
    aj.category,
    aj.division,
    aj.qty,
    aj.unit,
    aj.artwork_no,
    aj.product_type,
    aj.plate_status,
    aj.die_status,
    aj.plate_size,
    aj.plate_count,
    aj.has_hybrid_plate,
    aj.hybrid_plate_size,
    aj.hybrid_plate_count,
    aj.die_count,
    aj.sheet_length,
    aj.sheet_width,
    aj.sheet_ups,
    aj.printing_colors,
    aj.across_ups,
    aj.along_ups,
    aj.total_ups,
    aj.across_width,
    aj.teeth,
    aj.across_gap_mm,
    aj.along_gap_mm,
    aj.status,
    aj.artwork_at,
    aj.approved_at,
    aj.accounts_status,
    aj.business_status,
    aj.stock_qty_to_bill,
    aj.job_type,
    COALESCE(sp.fg_stock_qty, (0)::numeric) AS fg_stock_qty
   FROM (public.v_artwork_jobs_active aj
     LEFT JOIN stock_by_product sp ON ((sp.product_code = aj.product_code)));


--
-- Name: v_billing_dispatch_summary; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_billing_dispatch_summary WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 WITH dispatch_totals AS (
         SELECT dr.so_line_id,
            (COALESCE(sum(dr.dispatch_qty), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS dispatched_qty
           FROM public.dispatch_records dr
          WHERE (dr.so_line_id IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY dr.so_line_id
        ), dispatch_latest AS (
         SELECT DISTINCT ON (dr.so_line_id) dr.so_line_id,
            dr.dispatch_no AS latest_dispatch_no,
            COALESCE((dr.dispatch_date)::timestamp without time zone, dr.created_at) AS latest_dispatch_date,
            dr.transporter,
            dr.vehicle_no,
            dr.lr_no
           FROM public.dispatch_records dr
          WHERE (dr.so_line_id IS NOT NULL)
          ORDER BY dr.so_line_id, COALESCE((dr.dispatch_date)::timestamp without time zone, dr.created_at) DESC, dr.created_at DESC, dr.id DESC
        )
 SELECT t.so_line_id,
    t.dispatched_qty,
    l.latest_dispatch_no,
    l.latest_dispatch_date,
    l.transporter,
    l.vehicle_no,
    l.lr_no
   FROM (dispatch_totals t
     LEFT JOIN dispatch_latest l ON ((l.so_line_id = t.so_line_id)));


--
-- Name: v_billed_order_closure; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_billed_order_closure WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 WITH invoice_usage AS (
         SELECT il.so_line_id,
            (COALESCE(sum(COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS billed_qty,
            (COALESCE(sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(inv.status, 'DRAFT'::text)) = 'POSTED'::text) THEN COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric)
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS posted_qty,
            (COALESCE(sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(inv.status, 'DRAFT'::text)) <> 'POSTED'::text) THEN COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric)
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS draft_qty,
            (count(DISTINCT inv.id))::integer AS invoice_count,
            min(inv.invoice_date) AS first_invoice_date,
            max(inv.invoice_date) AS last_invoice_date,
            string_agg(DISTINCT inv.invoice_no, ' || '::text ORDER BY inv.invoice_no) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(inv.invoice_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS invoice_nos,
            string_agg(DISTINCT (((inv.invoice_no || ' ['::text) || upper(COALESCE(inv.status, 'DRAFT'::text))) || ']'::text), ' || '::text ORDER BY (((inv.invoice_no || ' ['::text) || upper(COALESCE(inv.status, 'DRAFT'::text))) || ']'::text)) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(inv.invoice_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS invoice_refs
           FROM (public.invoice_lines il
             JOIN public.invoices inv ON ((inv.id = il.invoice_id)))
          WHERE ((il.so_line_id IS NOT NULL) AND ((il.so_line_id)::text !~~ 'MANUAL-%'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(inv.status, 'DRAFT'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['CANCELLED'::text, 'VOID'::text, 'DELETED'::text])) AND (upper(COALESCE(inv.document_type, 'INVOICE'::text)) <> 'CHALLAN'::text) AND (COALESCE(inv.invoice_no, ''::text) !~~ 'DC-H/%'::text))
          GROUP BY il.so_line_id
        )
 SELECT so.id AS so_id,
    sol.id AS so_line_id,
    COALESCE(so.so_number, ''::text) AS so_number,
    so.so_date,
    COALESCE(sol.line_no, 0) AS line_no,
    COALESCE(so.po_number, ''::text) AS po_number,
    so.po_date,
    COALESCE(so.sales_rep, ''::text) AS sales_rep,
    COALESCE(so.client_code, ''::text) AS client_code,
    COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code, ''::text) AS client_name,
    COALESCE(sol.division, ''::text) AS division,
    COALESCE(sol.product_code, ''::text) AS product_code,
    COALESCE(sol.product_name, ''::text) AS product_name,
    COALESCE(sol.category, ''::text) AS category,
    COALESCE(sol.unit, 'Pcs'::text) AS unit,
    (COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS order_qty,
    (COALESCE(iu.billed_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS dispatched_qty,
    (GREATEST((COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(iu.billed_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS dispatch_balance_qty,
    (COALESCE(iu.billed_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS billed_qty,
    (GREATEST((COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(iu.billed_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS billing_gap_qty,
    (COALESCE(iu.posted_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS posted_qty,
    (COALESCE(iu.draft_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS draft_qty,
    COALESCE(iu.invoice_count, 0) AS invoice_count,
    COALESCE(iu.invoice_nos, ''::text) AS invoice_nos,
    COALESCE(iu.invoice_refs, ''::text) AS invoice_refs,
    iu.first_invoice_date,
    iu.last_invoice_date,
    ds.latest_dispatch_no,
    ds.latest_dispatch_date,
    COALESCE(ds.transporter, ''::text) AS transporter,
    COALESCE(ds.vehicle_no, ''::text) AS vehicle_no,
    COALESCE(ds.lr_no, ''::text) AS lr_no,
    sol.expected_delivery,
    sol.final_delivery,
    upper(COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text)) AS raw_line_status,
    sol.closed_at,
    COALESCE(sol.closed_by, ''::text) AS closed_by,
    sol.status_updated_at,
    ((COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(iu.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) >= COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric))) AS auto_closed_by_dispatch,
        CASE
            WHEN ((COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(iu.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) >= COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric))) THEN 'CLOSED'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(sol.closed_by, ''::text)) ~~ '%SHORT CLOSE%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(sol.closed_by, ''::text)) ~~ '%SHORT_CLOSE%'::text)) THEN 'SHORT_CLOSED'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = 'PARTIAL_DISPATCHED'::text) THEN 'PARTIAL_DISPATCHED'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = 'CLOSED'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(sol.closed_by, ''::text)) ~~ '%MANUAL%'::text)) THEN 'CLOSED'::text
            ELSE 'OPEN'::text
        END AS closure_status,
        CASE
            WHEN ((COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(iu.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) >= COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric))) THEN 'Fully billed'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(sol.closed_by, ''::text)) ~~ '%SHORT CLOSE%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(sol.closed_by, ''::text)) ~~ '%SHORT_CLOSE%'::text)) THEN COALESCE(NULLIF(sol.closed_by, ''::text), 'Manual short close'::text)
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = 'CLOSED'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(sol.closed_by, ''::text)) ~~ '%MANUAL%'::text)) THEN COALESCE(NULLIF(sol.closed_by, ''::text), 'Manual close'::text)
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = 'PARTIAL_DISPATCHED'::text) THEN 'Marked for partial dispatch'::text
            ELSE 'Billed but not fully closed'::text
        END AS closure_reason,
    lower(concat_ws(' '::text, COALESCE(so.so_number, ''::text), COALESCE((sol.line_no)::text, ''::text), COALESCE(so.po_number, ''::text), COALESCE(so.sales_rep, ''::text), COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code, ''::text), COALESCE(sol.division, ''::text), COALESCE(sol.product_code, ''::text), COALESCE(sol.product_name, ''::text), COALESCE(sol.category, ''::text), COALESCE(iu.invoice_nos, ''::text), COALESCE(iu.invoice_refs, ''::text), COALESCE(sol.closed_by, ''::text))) AS match_text
   FROM ((((public.sales_order_lines sol
     JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = sol.so_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
     JOIN invoice_usage iu ON ((iu.so_line_id = sol.id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.v_billing_dispatch_summary ds ON ((ds.so_line_id = sol.id)))
  WHERE (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text);


--
-- Name: v_billing_division_needs_fix; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_billing_division_needs_fix AS
 SELECT inv.invoice_no,
    inv.invoice_date,
    COALESCE(il.so_number, so.so_number, ''::text) AS so_number,
    COALESCE(il.line_no, sol.line_no) AS line_no,
    COALESCE(il.product_code, sol.product_code, ''::text) AS product_code,
    COALESCE(il.product_name, sol.product_name, ''::text) AS product_name,
    COALESCE(inv.client_name, c.client_name, inv.client_code, so.client_code, ''::text) AS client_name,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(btrim(il.division), ''::text), NULLIF(btrim(sol.division), ''::text), ''::text) AS current_division,
    COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric) AS billed_qty,
    COALESCE(il.taxable_amount, il.line_amount, (COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(il.rate, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS billed_value
   FROM ((((public.invoice_lines il
     JOIN public.invoices inv ON ((inv.id = il.invoice_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON ((sol.id = il.so_line_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = COALESCE(il.so_id, sol.so_id))))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = COALESCE(inv.client_code, so.client_code))))
  WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(inv.status, 'POSTED'::text)) = 'POSTED'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(inv.document_type, 'INVOICE'::text)) <> 'CHALLAN'::text) AND (COALESCE(inv.invoice_no, ''::text) !~~ 'DC-H/%'::text) AND ((COALESCE(NULLIF(btrim(il.division), ''::text), NULLIF(btrim(sol.division), ''::text), ''::text) = ''::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(btrim(il.division), ''::text), NULLIF(btrim(sol.division), ''::text), ''::text)) = 'MANUAL'::text)));


--
-- Name: v_billing_document_register; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_billing_document_register AS
 WITH base AS (
         SELECT (inv.id)::text AS id,
            inv.invoice_no,
            inv.invoice_date,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(inv.document_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['INVOICE'::text, 'CHALLAN'::text])) THEN upper(inv.document_type)
                    WHEN (COALESCE(inv.invoice_no, ''::text) ~~ 'DC-H/%'::text) THEN 'CHALLAN'::text
                    ELSE 'INVOICE'::text
                END AS document_type,
            COALESCE(inv.client_code, 'MANUAL'::text) AS client_code,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(inv.bill_to_name, ''::text), NULLIF(inv.client_name, ''::text), c.client_name, inv.client_code, 'MANUAL'::text) AS client_name,
            COALESCE(inv.status, 'DRAFT'::text) AS status,
            COALESCE(inv.billing_mode, ''::text) AS billing_mode,
            COALESCE(inv.remarks, ''::text) AS remarks,
            COALESCE(inv.created_by, ''::text) AS created_by,
            (COALESCE(inv.subtotal, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS subtotal,
            (COALESCE(inv.tax_total, GREATEST(((COALESCE(inv.grand_total, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(inv.subtotal, (0)::numeric)) - COALESCE(inv.freight, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric)))::numeric(18,2) AS tax_total,
            (COALESCE(inv.grand_total, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS grand_total,
            inv.created_at,
            inv.posted_at
           FROM (public.invoices inv
             LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = inv.client_code)))
        ), line_meta AS (
         SELECT (il.invoice_id)::text AS id,
            (count(*))::integer AS line_count,
            (COALESCE(sum(il.qty), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS total_qty,
            min(upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(il.source_mode, ''::text), 'UNKNOWN'::text))) AS derived_billing_mode
           FROM public.invoice_lines il
          GROUP BY il.invoice_id
        ), line_preview_raw AS (
         SELECT s.id,
            s.product_label,
            row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY s.id ORDER BY s.product_label) AS rn
           FROM ( SELECT DISTINCT (il.invoice_id)::text AS id,
                    COALESCE(NULLIF(il.product_name, ''::text), NULLIF(il.product_code, ''::text), 'Item'::text) AS product_label
                   FROM public.invoice_lines il) s
        ), line_preview AS (
         SELECT line_preview_raw.id,
            string_agg(line_preview_raw.product_label, ', '::text ORDER BY line_preview_raw.product_label) AS product_preview
           FROM line_preview_raw
          WHERE (line_preview_raw.rn <= 2)
          GROUP BY line_preview_raw.id
        )
 SELECT b.id,
    b.invoice_no,
    b.invoice_date,
    b.document_type,
    b.client_code,
    b.client_name,
    b.status,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(b.billing_mode, ''::text), lm.derived_billing_mode, 'UNKNOWN'::text) AS billing_mode,
    (COALESCE(lm.total_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS total_qty,
    COALESCE(lm.line_count, 0) AS line_count,
    COALESCE(lp.product_preview, ''::text) AS product_preview,
    b.remarks,
    b.subtotal,
    b.tax_total,
    b.grand_total,
    b.created_at,
    b.posted_at,
    COALESCE(b.posted_at, (b.created_at)::timestamp with time zone, ((b.invoice_date)::timestamp without time zone)::timestamp with time zone) AS sort_ts,
    COALESCE(b.created_by, ''::text) AS created_by
   FROM ((base b
     LEFT JOIN line_meta lm ON ((lm.id = b.id)))
     LEFT JOIN line_preview lp ON ((lp.id = b.id)));


--
-- Name: v_billing_invoice_usage_summary; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_billing_invoice_usage_summary WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 WITH usage_base AS (
         SELECT il.so_line_id,
            il.qty,
            COALESCE(inv.invoice_no, ''::text) AS invoice_no,
            COALESCE(inv.status, 'DRAFT'::text) AS invoice_status
           FROM (public.invoice_lines il
             LEFT JOIN public.invoices inv ON ((inv.id = il.invoice_id)))
          WHERE (il.so_line_id IS NOT NULL)
        ), usage_totals AS (
         SELECT ub.so_line_id,
            (COALESCE(sum(ub.qty), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS billed_qty,
            (COALESCE(sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(ub.invoice_status) = 'POSTED'::text) THEN ub.qty
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS posted_qty,
            (COALESCE(sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(ub.invoice_status) <> 'POSTED'::text) THEN ub.qty
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS draft_qty
           FROM usage_base ub
          GROUP BY ub.so_line_id
        ), usage_refs AS (
         SELECT x.so_line_id,
            string_agg(x.invoice_ref, ' || '::text ORDER BY x.invoice_ref) AS invoice_refs
           FROM ( SELECT DISTINCT ub.so_line_id,
                    (((ub.invoice_no || ' ['::text) || ub.invoice_status) || ']'::text) AS invoice_ref
                   FROM usage_base ub
                  WHERE (ub.invoice_no <> ''::text)) x
          GROUP BY x.so_line_id
        )
 SELECT t.so_line_id,
    t.billed_qty,
    t.posted_qty,
    t.draft_qty,
    r.invoice_refs
   FROM (usage_totals t
     LEFT JOIN usage_refs r ON ((r.so_line_id = t.so_line_id)));


--
-- Name: v_billing_packing_summary; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_billing_packing_summary WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 SELECT so_line_id,
    (COALESCE(sum(packed_qty), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS packed_qty,
    max(packed_at) AS latest_packed_at
   FROM public.packing_records pr
  WHERE (so_line_id IS NOT NULL)
  GROUP BY so_line_id;


--
-- Name: v_billing_line_read_model; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_billing_line_read_model AS
 WITH fg_adjustments AS (
         SELECT fa.so_line_id,
            (COALESCE(sum(fa.adjustment_qty), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS adjusted_qty
           FROM public.fg_stock_adjustments fa
          WHERE (fa.so_line_id IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY fa.so_line_id
        )
 SELECT so.id AS so_id,
    sol.id AS so_line_id,
    so.so_number,
    so.so_date,
    COALESCE(so.client_code, ''::text) AS client_code,
    COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code, ''::text) AS client_name,
    COALESCE(c.state, ''::text) AS client_state,
    COALESCE(c.gstin, ''::text) AS client_gstin,
    TRIM(BOTH ', '::text FROM concat_ws(', '::text, NULLIF(c.bill_to_address, ''::text), NULLIF(c.bill_to_city, ''::text), NULLIF(c.bill_to_state, ''::text), NULLIF(c.bill_to_pincode, ''::text))) AS client_address,
    COALESCE(so.po_number, ''::text) AS po_number,
    so.po_date,
    COALESCE(so.currency, 'INR'::text) AS currency,
    COALESCE(so.remarks, ''::text) AS sales_remarks,
    COALESCE(so.mode_of_transport, ''::text) AS mode_of_transport,
    COALESCE(so.transport_preference, ''::text) AS transport_preference,
    COALESCE(so.transport_payment, ''::text) AS transport_payment,
    COALESCE(so.billing_remarks, ''::text) AS billing_remarks,
    COALESCE(so.sales_rep, ''::text) AS sales_rep,
    COALESCE(sol.line_no, 0) AS line_no,
    COALESCE(sol.product_code, ''::text) AS product_code,
    COALESCE(sol.product_name, ''::text) AS product_name,
    COALESCE(sol.category, ''::text) AS category,
    COALESCE(sol.unit, 'Pcs'::text) AS unit,
    (COALESCE(sol.rate, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS rate,
    (COALESCE(sol.gst_pct, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS gst_pct,
    COALESCE(sol.hsn_group, ''::text) AS hsn_group,
    (COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS order_qty,
    (COALESCE(pk.packed_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS packed_qty,
    (COALESCE(ds.dispatched_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS dispatched_qty,
    (COALESCE(fg.adjusted_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS fg_adjusted_qty,
    (COALESCE(usg.billed_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS billed_qty,
    (COALESCE(usg.posted_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS posted_qty,
    (COALESCE(usg.draft_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS draft_qty,
    (GREATEST((COALESCE(ds.dispatched_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(usg.billed_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS dispatch_billable_qty,
    (GREATEST(((COALESCE(pk.packed_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(usg.billed_qty, (0)::numeric)) - COALESCE(fg.adjusted_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS fg_billable_qty,
    (GREATEST((COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(usg.billed_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS direct_billable_qty,
    pk.latest_packed_at,
    ds.latest_dispatch_no AS dispatch_no,
    ds.latest_dispatch_date AS dispatch_date,
    COALESCE(ds.transporter, ''::text) AS transporter,
    COALESCE(ds.vehicle_no, ''::text) AS vehicle_no,
    COALESCE(ds.lr_no, ''::text) AS lr_no,
    COALESCE(sol.job_reference, ''::text) AS job_reference,
    COALESCE(sol.division, ''::text) AS division,
    COALESCE(sol.quote_no, ''::text) AS quote_no,
    COALESCE(sol.pm_code, ''::text) AS pm_code,
    sol.expected_delivery,
    sol.final_delivery,
    COALESCE(sol.prepress_remarks, ''::text) AS prepress_remarks,
    COALESCE(sol.product_remarks, ''::text) AS product_remarks,
    COALESCE(usg.invoice_refs, ''::text) AS invoice_refs,
    lower(concat_ws(' '::text, COALESCE(so.so_number, ''::text), COALESCE((sol.line_no)::text, ''::text), COALESCE(sol.product_code, ''::text), COALESCE(sol.product_name, ''::text), COALESCE(sol.job_reference, ''::text), COALESCE(c.client_name, ''::text), COALESCE(so.po_number, ''::text),
        CASE
            WHEN COALESCE(so.advance_payment_required, false) THEN 'advance payment'::text
            ELSE ''::text
        END,
        CASE
            WHEN COALESCE(so.advance_payment_received, false) THEN 'payment received'::text
            ELSE 'payment pending'::text
        END)) AS match_text,
    COALESCE(so.advance_payment_required, false) AS advance_payment_required,
    COALESCE(so.advance_payment_received, false) AS advance_payment_received,
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(so.advance_payment_required, false) AND COALESCE(so.advance_payment_received, false)) THEN 'PAYMENT_RECEIVED'::text
            WHEN COALESCE(so.advance_payment_required, false) THEN 'PAYMENT_PENDING'::text
            ELSE 'NOT_REQUIRED'::text
        END AS advance_payment_status
   FROM ((((((public.sales_order_lines sol
     JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = sol.so_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
     LEFT JOIN public.v_billing_dispatch_summary ds ON ((ds.so_line_id = sol.id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.v_billing_packing_summary pk ON ((pk.so_line_id = sol.id)))
     LEFT JOIN fg_adjustments fg ON ((fg.so_line_id = sol.id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.v_billing_invoice_usage_summary usg ON ((usg.so_line_id = sol.id)))
  WHERE (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text);


--
-- Name: v_client_master_register; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_client_master_register WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 SELECT c.id,
    c.client_code,
    c.client_name,
    c.state,
    c.gstin,
    c.pan_no,
    c.credit_days,
    c.category,
    c.payment_terms,
    c.active,
    count(cp.id) FILTER (WHERE (cp.address_type = 'BILL_TO'::text)) AS bill_to_count,
    count(cp.id) FILTER (WHERE (cp.address_type = 'SHIP_TO'::text)) AS ship_to_count,
    COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', cp.id, 'party_name', cp.party_name, 'address_type', cp.address_type, 'label', cp.label, 'address_line1', cp.address_line1, 'address_line2', cp.address_line2, 'city', cp.city, 'state', cp.state, 'pincode', cp.pincode, 'gstin', cp.gstin, 'pan_no', cp.pan_no, 'payment_terms', cp.payment_terms, 'contact_person', cp.contact_person, 'contact_phone', cp.contact_phone, 'is_default', cp.is_default, 'active', cp.active) ORDER BY cp.address_type, cp.is_default DESC, cp.label, cp.party_name) FILTER (WHERE (cp.id IS NOT NULL)), '[]'::jsonb) AS parties
   FROM (public.clients c
     LEFT JOIN public.client_parties cp ON ((cp.client_id = c.id)))
  GROUP BY c.id, c.client_code, c.client_name, c.state, c.gstin, c.pan_no, c.credit_days, c.category, c.payment_terms, c.active;


--
-- Name: v_dispatch_board; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_dispatch_board WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 SELECT pr.id AS pack_id,
    pr.so_id,
    pr.so_line_id,
    pr.so_number,
    pr.line_no,
    pr.product_code,
    pr.product_name,
    pr.packed_qty,
    COALESCE(d.total_dispatched, (0)::numeric) AS dispatched_qty,
    (pr.packed_qty - COALESCE(d.total_dispatched, (0)::numeric)) AS balance_qty
   FROM (public.packing_records pr
     LEFT JOIN ( SELECT dispatch_records.so_line_id,
            sum(dispatch_records.dispatch_qty) AS total_dispatched
           FROM public.dispatch_records
          GROUP BY dispatch_records.so_line_id) d ON ((d.so_line_id = pr.so_line_id)))
  WHERE (pr.ready_to_dispatch = true);


--
-- Name: work_order_jobs; Type: TABLE; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE public.work_order_jobs (
    id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
    wo_id uuid,
    so_number text,
    line_no integer,
    product_name text,
    qty numeric,
    unit text,
    ups numeric,
    artwork_no text,
    client_name text,
    expected_delivery date,
    po_number text,
    job_priority text,
    product_remarks text,
    so_remarks text,
    job_reference text,
    prepress_remark text,
    category text,
    sales_rep text,
    so_date date,
    group_ups numeric
);


--
-- Name: work_order_routing; Type: TABLE; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE public.work_order_routing (
    id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
    wo_id uuid,
    so_number text,
    line_no text,
    process_name text,
    sequence_no integer,
    department text,
    planned_machine text,
    planned_qty numeric DEFAULT 0,
    completed_qty numeric DEFAULT 0,
    status text DEFAULT 'PENDING'::text,
    created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now()
);


--
-- Name: v_packing_queue_fast; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_packing_queue_fast AS
 WITH artwork_category AS (
         SELECT a.artwork_no,
            max(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'FLEXO'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'CORRUGATION'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'OFFSET'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END) AS department_category
           FROM public.artworks a
          WHERE (COALESCE(a.artwork_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)
          GROUP BY a.artwork_no
        ), job_base AS (
         SELECT woj.wo_id,
            wo_1.wo_number,
            wo_1.wo_date,
            woj.so_number,
            woj.line_no,
            max(NULLIF(woj.artwork_no, ''::text)) AS artwork_no,
            max(NULLIF(woj.client_name, ''::text)) AS client_name,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(max(COALESCE(woj.ups, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric), NULLIF(max(COALESCE(woj.group_ups, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric) AS job_ups,
            string_agg(DISTINCT woj.product_name, ' / '::text ORDER BY woj.product_name) AS product_name,
            COALESCE(max(ac.department_category),
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'FLEXO'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'CORRUGATION'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'OFFSET'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                    ELSE max(woj.category)
                END) AS department_category
           FROM ((public.work_order_jobs woj
             JOIN public.work_orders wo_1 ON ((wo_1.id = woj.wo_id)))
             LEFT JOIN artwork_category ac ON ((ac.artwork_no = woj.artwork_no)))
          GROUP BY woj.wo_id, wo_1.wo_number, wo_1.wo_date, woj.so_number, woj.line_no
        ), final_production_routing AS (
         SELECT r.wo_id,
            r.id AS routing_id,
            COALESCE(r.process_name, r.department, ''::text) AS process_name,
            row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY r.wo_id ORDER BY r.sequence_no DESC, r.id DESC) AS rn
           FROM public.work_order_routing r
          WHERE (upper(TRIM(BOTH FROM COALESCE(r.process_name, r.department, ''::text))) <> ALL (ARRAY['PACKING'::text, 'DISPATCH'::text, 'CUTTING PRE'::text, 'CUTTING POST'::text, 'QC'::text]))
        ), production_line_totals AS (
         SELECT pe.routing_id,
            pe.so_number,
            pe.line_no,
            sum(COALESCE(pe.ok_qty, GREATEST((COALESCE(pe.produced_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pe.rejected_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))) AS produced_qty
           FROM public.production_entries pe
          WHERE ((COALESCE(pe.so_number, ''::text) <> ''::text) AND (COALESCE(pe.line_no, ''::text) <> ''::text))
          GROUP BY pe.routing_id, pe.so_number, pe.line_no
        ), production_totals AS (
         SELECT pe.routing_id,
            sum(COALESCE(pe.ok_qty, GREATEST((COALESCE(pe.produced_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pe.rejected_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))) AS produced_qty
           FROM public.production_entries pe
          GROUP BY pe.routing_id
        ), packing_totals AS (
         SELECT pr.so_line_id,
            pr.so_number,
            pr.line_no,
            (array_agg(pr.id ORDER BY pr.packed_at DESC NULLS LAST, pr.id))[1] AS pack_id,
            sum(COALESCE(pr.packed_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS packed_qty,
            sum(COALESCE(pr.packed_weight_kg, (0)::numeric)) AS packed_weight_kg,
            bool_or((pr.ready_to_dispatch = true)) AS ready_to_dispatch
           FROM public.packing_records pr
          GROUP BY pr.so_line_id, pr.so_number, pr.line_no
        )
 SELECT pt.pack_id,
    so.id AS so_id,
    sol.id AS so_line_id,
    jb.so_number,
    jb.line_no,
    sol.product_code,
    COALESCE(sol.product_name, jb.product_name) AS product_name,
    jb.client_name,
    COALESCE(sol.category, jb.department_category) AS category,
    jb.department_category,
    COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) AS order_qty,
        CASE
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(fr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(fr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text)) THEN (COALESCE(plt.produced_qty, prod.produced_qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(NULLIF(jb.job_ups, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric))
            ELSE COALESCE(plt.produced_qty, prod.produced_qty, (0)::numeric)
        END AS produced_qty,
    COALESCE(pt.packed_qty, (0)::numeric) AS packed_qty,
    pt.ready_to_dispatch,
    sol.expected_delivery,
    sol.final_delivery,
    sol.division,
    sol.quote_no,
    sol.pm_code,
    sol.product_remarks,
    sol.prepress_remarks,
    so.remarks AS so_remarks,
    jb.artwork_no,
    jb.wo_number,
    jb.wo_date,
    COALESCE((wo.snapshot_json ->> 'modeOfTransport'::text), ''::text) AS transport_mode,
    COALESCE(pt.packed_weight_kg, (0)::numeric) AS packed_weight_kg
   FROM (((((((job_base jb
     JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.id = jb.wo_id)))
     LEFT JOIN final_production_routing fr ON (((fr.wo_id = jb.wo_id) AND (fr.rn = 1))))
     LEFT JOIN production_line_totals plt ON (((plt.routing_id = fr.routing_id) AND (plt.so_number = jb.so_number) AND (plt.line_no = (jb.line_no)::text))))
     LEFT JOIN production_totals prod ON ((prod.routing_id = fr.routing_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.so_number = jb.so_number)))
     LEFT JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON (((sol.so_id = so.id) AND (sol.line_no = jb.line_no))))
     LEFT JOIN packing_totals pt ON ((pt.so_line_id = sol.id)))
  WHERE (
        CASE
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(fr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(fr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text)) THEN (COALESCE(plt.produced_qty, prod.produced_qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(NULLIF(jb.job_ups, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric))
            ELSE COALESCE(plt.produced_qty, prod.produced_qty, (0)::numeric)
        END > (0)::numeric);


--
-- Name: v_dispatch_queue_fast; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_dispatch_queue_fast AS
 WITH dispatch_totals AS (
         SELECT dr.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS dispatched_qty
           FROM public.dispatch_records dr
          GROUP BY dr.so_line_id
        )
 SELECT pq.pack_id,
    pq.so_id,
    pq.so_line_id,
    pq.so_number,
    pq.line_no,
    pq.product_code,
    pq.product_name,
    pq.client_name,
    pq.category,
    pq.department_category,
    pq.order_qty,
    pq.produced_qty,
    pq.packed_qty,
    COALESCE(dt.dispatched_qty, (0)::numeric) AS dispatched_qty,
    pq.ready_to_dispatch,
    pq.expected_delivery,
    pq.final_delivery,
    pq.division,
    pq.quote_no,
    pq.pm_code,
    pq.product_remarks,
    pq.prepress_remarks,
    pq.so_remarks,
    pq.artwork_no,
    pq.wo_number,
    pq.wo_date,
    pq.transport_mode,
    pq.packed_weight_kg
   FROM (public.v_packing_queue_fast pq
     LEFT JOIN dispatch_totals dt ON ((dt.so_line_id = pq.so_line_id)))
  WHERE (pq.ready_to_dispatch = true);


--
-- Name: v_flexo_work_order_jobs; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_flexo_work_order_jobs AS
 WITH latest_artwork AS (
         SELECT DISTINCT ON (a.so_id, (a.line_no)::text) a.id,
            a.so_id,
            a.line_no,
            a.artwork_no,
            a.product_type,
            a.sheet_length,
            a.sheet_width,
            a.sheet_ups,
            a.printing_colors,
            a.across_ups,
            a.along_ups,
            a.total_ups,
            a.across_width,
            a.teeth,
            a.across_gap_mm,
            a.along_gap_mm,
            COALESCE(a.stock_qty_to_bill, (0)::numeric) AS stock_qty_to_bill,
            a.status,
            a.artwork_at,
            a.approved_at
           FROM public.artworks a
          ORDER BY a.so_id, (a.line_no)::text,
                CASE
                    WHEN (NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(a.artwork_no, ''::text)), ''::text) IS NOT NULL) THEN 0
                    ELSE 1
                END, a.approved_at DESC NULLS LAST, a.artwork_at DESC NULLS LAST, a.id DESC
        ), processed_flexo_jobs AS (
         SELECT woj.wo_id,
            woj.so_number,
            (woj.line_no)::text AS line_no_key,
            woj.qty,
            wo.wo_number,
            wo.wo_date,
            COALESCE(wo.snapshot_json, '{}'::jsonb) AS snapshot_json
           FROM (public.work_order_jobs woj
             JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.id = woj.wo_id)))
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{jobDetails,type}'::text[]), ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) OR (COALESCE(wo.snapshot_json, '{}'::jsonb) ? 'flexoDetails'::text))
        ), processed_agg AS (
         SELECT pfj.so_number,
            pfj.line_no_key,
            sum(COALESCE(pfj.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS processed_qty,
            string_agg(DISTINCT pfj.wo_number, ', '::text ORDER BY pfj.wo_number) AS wo_list,
            min(pfj.wo_number) AS wo_no,
            max(pfj.wo_date) AS wo_date,
            COALESCE(to_char((max(pfj.wo_date))::timestamp without time zone, 'HH24:MI'::text), ''::text) AS wo_time,
            COALESCE((max(pfj.wo_date))::text, ''::text) AS wo_date_time,
            max(NULLIF((pfj.snapshot_json #>> '{flexoDetails,cylinderTeeth}'::text[]), ''::text)) AS processed_teeth,
            max(NULLIF((pfj.snapshot_json #>> '{flexoDetails,totalUps}'::text[]), ''::text)) AS processed_ups,
