            max(NULLIF(btrim(concat_ws(' - '::text, NULLIF((pfj.snapshot_json #>> '{flexoDetails,itemCode}'::text[]), ''::text), NULLIF((pfj.snapshot_json #>> '{flexoDetails,itemName}'::text[]), ''::text))), ''::text)) AS processed_item_details
           FROM processed_flexo_jobs pfj
          GROUP BY pfj.so_number, pfj.line_no_key
        ), base_rows AS (
         SELECT so.id AS so_id,
            so.so_number,
            so.so_date,
            COALESCE(to_char((so.created_at AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kolkata'::text), 'HH24:MI'::text), ''::text) AS so_time,
            btrim(concat_ws(' '::text, COALESCE((so.so_date)::text, ''::text), COALESCE(to_char((so.created_at AT TIME ZONE 'Asia/Kolkata'::text), 'HH24:MI'::text), ''::text))) AS so_date_time,
            so.sales_rep,
            so.po_number,
            so.remarks AS so_remarks,
            upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) AS header_status,
            sol.id AS so_line_id,
            sol.line_no,
            sol.product_code,
            sol.product_name,
            sol.category,
            COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) AS order_qty,
            COALESCE(sol.unit, ''::text) AS unit,
            COALESCE(sol.job_reference, ''::text) AS job_reference,
            COALESCE(sol.job_reference, ''::text) AS job_card_no,
            COALESCE(sol.division, ''::text) AS division,
            sol.expected_delivery,
            COALESCE(sol.job_priority, ''::text) AS job_priority,
            COALESCE(sol.product_remarks, ''::text) AS product_remarks,
            COALESCE(sol.prepress_remarks, ''::text) AS prepress_remarks,
            COALESCE(sol.prepress_remarks, ''::text) AS prepress_remark,
            upper(COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text)) AS line_status,
            upper(COALESCE(sol.accounts_status, 'PENDING'::text)) AS accounts_status,
            upper(COALESCE(sol.business_status, 'PENDING'::text)) AS business_status,
            COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code, ''::text) AS client_name,
            a.artwork_no,
            a.product_type AS artwork_product_type,
            a.sheet_length,
            a.sheet_width,
            a.sheet_ups,
            a.printing_colors,
            a.across_ups,
            a.along_ups,
            a.total_ups,
            a.across_width,
            a.teeth,
            a.across_gap_mm,
            a.along_gap_mm,
            COALESCE(a.stock_qty_to_bill, (0)::numeric) AS stock_qty_to_bill,
            upper(COALESCE(a.status, 'NO_ART'::text)) AS artwork_status,
            a.approved_at,
            COALESCE(pa.processed_qty, (0)::numeric) AS processed_qty,
            COALESCE(pa.wo_list, ''::text) AS wo_list,
            COALESCE(pa.wo_no, ''::text) AS wo_no,
            pa.wo_date,
            COALESCE(pa.wo_time, ''::text) AS wo_time,
            COALESCE(pa.wo_date_time, ''::text) AS wo_date_time,
            COALESCE(pa.processed_teeth, ''::text) AS processed_teeth,
            COALESCE(pa.processed_ups, ''::text) AS processed_ups,
            COALESCE(pa.processed_item_details, ''::text) AS processed_item_details
           FROM ((((public.sales_order_lines sol
             JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = sol.so_id)))
             LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
             LEFT JOIN latest_artwork a ON (((a.so_id = sol.so_id) AND ((a.line_no)::text = (sol.line_no)::text))))
             LEFT JOIN processed_agg pa ON (((pa.so_number = so.so_number) AND (pa.line_no_key = (sol.line_no)::text))))
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text))
        ), classified AS (
         SELECT b.so_id,
            b.so_number,
            b.so_date,
            b.so_time,
            b.so_date_time,
            b.sales_rep,
            b.po_number,
            b.so_remarks,
            b.header_status,
            b.so_line_id,
            b.line_no,
            b.product_code,
            b.product_name,
            b.category,
            b.order_qty,
            b.unit,
            b.job_reference,
            b.job_card_no,
            b.division,
            b.expected_delivery,
            b.job_priority,
            b.product_remarks,
            b.prepress_remarks,
            b.prepress_remark,
            b.line_status,
            b.accounts_status,
            b.business_status,
            b.client_name,
            b.artwork_no,
            b.artwork_product_type,
            b.sheet_length,
            b.sheet_width,
            b.sheet_ups,
            b.printing_colors,
            b.across_ups,
            b.along_ups,
            b.total_ups,
            b.across_width,
            b.teeth,
            b.across_gap_mm,
            b.along_gap_mm,
            b.stock_qty_to_bill,
            b.artwork_status,
            b.approved_at,
            b.processed_qty,
            b.wo_list,
            b.wo_no,
            b.wo_date,
            b.wo_time,
            b.wo_date_time,
            b.processed_teeth,
            b.processed_ups,
            b.processed_item_details,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.artwork_product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'Flexo'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.artwork_product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'Corrugation'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.artwork_product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'Offset'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.artwork_product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'Digital'::text
                    ELSE NULLIF(b.artwork_product_type, ''::text)
                END AS normalized_artwork_product_type,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.division, ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'Flexo'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.division, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'Corrugation'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.division, ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'Offset'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.division, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'Digital'::text
                    ELSE NULLIF(b.division, ''::text)
                END AS normalized_division,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'Flexo'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'Corrugation'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'Offset'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(b.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'Digital'::text
                    ELSE NULLIF(b.category, ''::text)
                END AS normalized_category
           FROM base_rows b
        ), eligible AS (
         SELECT c.so_id,
            c.so_number,
            c.so_date,
            c.so_time,
            c.so_date_time,
            c.sales_rep,
            c.po_number,
            c.so_remarks,
            c.header_status,
            c.so_line_id,
            c.line_no,
            c.product_code,
            c.product_name,
            c.category,
            c.order_qty,
            c.unit,
            c.job_reference,
            c.job_card_no,
            c.division,
            c.expected_delivery,
            c.job_priority,
            c.product_remarks,
            c.prepress_remarks,
            c.prepress_remark,
            c.line_status,
            c.accounts_status,
            c.business_status,
            c.client_name,
            c.artwork_no,
            c.artwork_product_type,
            c.sheet_length,
            c.sheet_width,
            c.sheet_ups,
            c.printing_colors,
            c.across_ups,
            c.along_ups,
            c.total_ups,
            c.across_width,
            c.teeth,
            c.across_gap_mm,
            c.along_gap_mm,
            c.stock_qty_to_bill,
            c.artwork_status,
            c.approved_at,
            c.processed_qty,
            c.wo_list,
            c.wo_no,
            c.wo_date,
            c.wo_time,
            c.wo_date_time,
            c.processed_teeth,
            c.processed_ups,
            c.processed_item_details,
            c.normalized_artwork_product_type,
            c.normalized_division,
            c.normalized_category,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(c.normalized_artwork_product_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXO'::text, 'OFFSET'::text, 'DIGITAL'::text, 'CORRUGATION'::text])) THEN c.normalized_artwork_product_type
                    ELSE COALESCE(c.normalized_division, c.normalized_category, c.normalized_artwork_product_type, ''::text)
                END AS product_type,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(c.normalized_artwork_product_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXO'::text, 'OFFSET'::text, 'DIGITAL'::text, 'CORRUGATION'::text])) THEN 'Artwork Product Type'::text
                    ELSE 'Sales Order Division'::text
                END AS classification_source,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(c.normalized_artwork_product_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXO'::text, 'OFFSET'::text, 'DIGITAL'::text, 'CORRUGATION'::text])) THEN (upper(c.normalized_artwork_product_type) = 'FLEXO'::text)
                    ELSE ((upper(COALESCE(c.normalized_division, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) OR ((NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(c.artwork_no, ''::text)), ''::text) IS NULL) AND (upper(COALESCE(c.normalized_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text)))
                END AS is_flexo_wo_candidate,
            (upper(COALESCE(c.line_status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['CLOSED'::text, 'CANCELLED'::text])) AS is_line_active
           FROM classified c
        )
 SELECT so_number,
    so_date,
    so_time,
    so_date_time,
    sales_rep,
    line_no,
    job_card_no,
    division,
    classification_source,
    product_code,
    client_name,
    product_name,
    category,
    product_type,
    GREATEST((order_qty - stock_qty_to_bill), (0)::numeric) AS qty,
    order_qty,
    stock_qty_to_bill,
        CASE
            WHEN is_line_active THEN GREATEST(((order_qty - stock_qty_to_bill) - processed_qty), (0)::numeric)
            ELSE (0)::numeric
        END AS remaining_qty,
    processed_qty,
    unit,
    artwork_no,
    sheet_length,
    sheet_width,
    sheet_ups,
    printing_colors,
    across_ups,
    along_ups,
    total_ups,
    across_width,
    across_gap_mm,
    along_gap_mm,
    teeth,
    artwork_status AS status,
    artwork_status,
    approved_at,
    accounts_status,
    business_status,
    ((accounts_status = 'APPROVED'::text) AND (business_status = 'APPROVED'::text) AND (artwork_status = 'APPROVED'::text)) AS can_create_wo,
        CASE
            WHEN ((accounts_status = 'REJECTED'::text) OR (business_status = 'REJECTED'::text) OR (artwork_status = 'REJECTED'::text)) THEN ('Rejected at '::text || concat_ws(' / '::text,
            CASE
                WHEN (accounts_status = 'REJECTED'::text) THEN 'Accounts'::text
                ELSE NULL::text
            END,
            CASE
                WHEN (business_status = 'REJECTED'::text) THEN 'Business'::text
                ELSE NULL::text
            END,
            CASE
                WHEN (artwork_status = 'REJECTED'::text) THEN 'Artwork'::text
                ELSE NULL::text
            END))
            WHEN ((accounts_status <> 'APPROVED'::text) OR (business_status <> 'APPROVED'::text) OR (artwork_status <> 'APPROVED'::text)) THEN ('Pending at '::text || concat_ws(' / '::text,
            CASE
                WHEN (accounts_status <> 'APPROVED'::text) THEN 'Accounts'::text
                ELSE NULL::text
            END,
            CASE
                WHEN (business_status <> 'APPROVED'::text) THEN 'Business'::text
                ELSE NULL::text
            END,
            CASE
                WHEN (artwork_status <> 'APPROVED'::text) THEN 'Artwork'::text
                ELSE NULL::text
            END))
            ELSE 'Approved'::text
        END AS approval_stage,
    wo_list,
    wo_no,
    processed_teeth,
    processed_ups,
    processed_item_details,
    po_number,
    expected_delivery,
    job_priority,
    so_remarks,
    product_remarks,
    prepress_remark,
    prepress_remarks,
    job_reference,
    wo_date,
    wo_time,
    wo_date_time,
    line_status,
    is_line_active
   FROM eligible e
  WHERE ((is_flexo_wo_candidate = true) AND ((is_line_active = true) OR (processed_qty > (0)::numeric)));


--
-- Name: VIEW v_flexo_work_order_jobs; Type: COMMENT; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

COMMENT ON VIEW public.v_flexo_work_order_jobs IS 'Fast Flexo Work Order queue: pending, approval status, remaining qty, and processed WO metadata in one view for GAS.';


--
-- Name: v_packing_board; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_packing_board WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 WITH prod AS (
         SELECT pe.wo_id,
            pe.routing_id,
            pe.produced_qty,
            row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY pe.wo_id ORDER BY pe.created_at DESC) AS rn
           FROM public.production_entries pe
        ), pack AS (
         SELECT packing_records.so_number,
            packing_records.line_no,
            sum(packing_records.packed_qty) AS packed_qty
           FROM public.packing_records
          GROUP BY packing_records.so_number, packing_records.line_no
        )
 SELECT woj.wo_id,
    woj.so_number,
    woj.line_no,
    woj.product_name,
    woj.client_name,
    woj.artwork_no,
    woj.qty AS order_qty,
    COALESCE(prod.produced_qty, (0)::numeric) AS produced_qty,
    COALESCE(pack.packed_qty, (0)::numeric) AS packed_qty,
    (COALESCE(prod.produced_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pack.packed_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS balance_qty
   FROM ((public.work_order_jobs woj
     LEFT JOIN prod ON (((prod.wo_id = woj.wo_id) AND (prod.rn = 1))))
     LEFT JOIN pack ON (((pack.so_number = woj.so_number) AND (pack.line_no = woj.line_no))))
  WHERE (COALESCE(prod.produced_qty, (0)::numeric) > COALESCE(pack.packed_qty, (0)::numeric));


--
-- Name: v_pr_po_receipt_cycle; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_pr_po_receipt_cycle WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 WITH receipt_by_line AS (
         SELECT r_1.po_line_id,
            (count(*))::integer AS receipt_count,
            (sum(COALESCE(r_1.qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS line_received_qty,
            max(r_1.receipt_date) AS latest_receipt_date,
            jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('receiptId', (r_1.id)::text, 'poId', COALESCE((r_1.po_id)::text, ''::text), 'poNo', COALESCE(r_1.po_no, ''::text), 'poLineId', COALESCE((r_1.po_line_id)::text, ''::text), 'lineNo', COALESCE(r_1.line_no, 0), 'sourceType', COALESCE(r_1.source_type, ''::text), 'receiptDate', r_1.receipt_date, 'challanNo', COALESCE(r_1.challan_no, ''::text), 'qty', COALESCE(r_1.qty, (0)::numeric), 'rate', COALESCE(r_1.rate, (0)::numeric), 'remarks', COALESCE(r_1.remarks, ''::text), 'createdAt', r_1.created_at) ORDER BY r_1.receipt_date DESC, r_1.created_at DESC, r_1.id DESC) AS receipt_entries
           FROM public.purchase_po_receipts r_1
          GROUP BY r_1.po_line_id
        ), po_by_pr AS (
         SELECT pol_1.source_ref AS pr_no,
            (count(DISTINCT pol_1.po_id))::integer AS po_count,
            (count(*))::integer AS po_line_count,
            string_agg(DISTINCT pol_1.po_no, ', '::text ORDER BY pol_1.po_no) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(pol_1.po_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS po_refs,
            (sum(COALESCE(pol_1.qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS total_ordered_qty,
            (sum(COALESCE(rbl_1.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS total_po_received_qty,
            max(rbl_1.latest_receipt_date) AS latest_po_receipt_date
           FROM (public.purchase_order_lines pol_1
             LEFT JOIN receipt_by_line rbl_1 ON ((rbl_1.po_line_id = pol_1.id)))
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(pol_1.source_type, ''::text)) = 'INVENTORY_PR'::text) AND (COALESCE(pol_1.source_ref, ''::text) <> ''::text))
          GROUP BY pol_1.source_ref
        ), direct_pr_receipts AS (
         SELECT l.ref_no AS pr_no,
            (count(*))::integer AS direct_receipt_count,
            (sum(COALESCE(l.qty_in, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS direct_received_qty,
            max(l.created_at) AS latest_direct_receipt_at
           FROM public.inv_ledger l
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(l.ref_type, ''::text)) = 'PR-RECEIPT'::text) AND (COALESCE(l.ref_no, ''::text) <> ''::text) AND (COALESCE(l.qty_in, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric))
          GROUP BY l.ref_no
        )
 SELECT (pr.id)::text AS pr_id,
    pr.pr_no,
    pr.created_at AS pr_created_at,
    pr.closed_at AS pr_closed_at,
    COALESCE(pr.status, 'OPEN'::text) AS pr_status,
    COALESCE(pr.item_code, i.item_code, ''::text) AS pr_item_code,
    COALESCE(pr.item_name, i.item_name, ''::text) AS pr_item_name,
    COALESCE(i.category, ''::text) AS item_category,
    COALESCE(i.uom, ''::text) AS uom,
    COALESCE(pr.department, ''::text) AS pr_department,
    COALESCE(pr.job_ref, ''::text) AS pr_job_ref,
    COALESCE(pr.remarks, ''::text) AS pr_remarks,
    (COALESCE(pr.requested_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS pr_requested_qty,
    (COALESCE(pr.received_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS pr_received_qty,
    (GREATEST((COALESCE(pr.requested_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pr.received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS pr_pending_receipt_qty,
    (COALESCE(pbp.total_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS pr_ordered_qty,
    (GREATEST((COALESCE(pr.requested_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pbp.total_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS pr_pending_to_order_qty,
    (COALESCE(pbp.total_po_received_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS pr_po_received_qty,
    (GREATEST((COALESCE(pbp.total_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pbp.total_po_received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS pr_open_po_qty,
    (COALESCE(dpr.direct_received_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS pr_direct_received_qty,
    COALESCE(pbp.po_count, 0) AS pr_po_count,
    COALESCE(pbp.po_line_count, 0) AS pr_po_line_count,
    COALESCE(dpr.direct_receipt_count, 0) AS pr_direct_receipt_count,
    COALESCE(pbp.po_refs, ''::text) AS pr_po_refs,
    pbp.latest_po_receipt_date AS pr_latest_po_receipt_date,
    dpr.latest_direct_receipt_at AS pr_latest_direct_receipt_at,
    (po.id)::text AS po_id,
    po.po_no,
    po.order_date AS po_order_date,
    COALESCE(po.status, ''::text) AS po_status,
    (po.vendor_id)::text AS vendor_id,
    COALESCE(po.vendor_name, v.vendor_name, ''::text) AS vendor_name,
    COALESCE(v.vendor_code, ''::text) AS vendor_code,
    COALESCE(v.gstin, ''::text) AS vendor_gstin,
    COALESCE(po.payment_terms, ''::text) AS payment_terms,
    COALESCE(po.delivery_terms, ''::text) AS delivery_terms,
    COALESCE(po.freight_terms, ''::text) AS freight_terms,
    (COALESCE(po.freight_value, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS freight_value,
    COALESCE(po.created_by, ''::text) AS po_created_by,
    po.created_at AS po_created_at,
    po.updated_at AS po_updated_at,
    (pol.id)::text AS po_line_id,
    pol.line_no AS po_line_no,
    COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text) AS po_source_type,
    COALESCE(pol.source_ref, ''::text) AS po_source_ref,
    COALESCE(pol.item_code, ''::text) AS po_item_code,
    COALESCE(pol.item_name, ''::text) AS po_item_name,
    COALESCE(pol.department, ''::text) AS po_line_department,
    COALESCE(pol.job_ref, ''::text) AS po_line_job_ref,
    COALESCE(pol.remarks, ''::text) AS po_line_remarks,
    (COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS po_line_ordered_qty,
    (COALESCE(pol.rate, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS po_line_rate,
    (COALESCE(pol.tax_pct, (0)::numeric))::numeric(8,2) AS po_line_tax_pct,
    (COALESCE(pol.amount, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS po_line_amount,
    (COALESCE(pol.tax_amount, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS po_line_tax_amount,
    (COALESCE(pol.total_amount, pol.amount, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS po_line_total_amount,
    (COALESCE(rbl.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS po_line_received_qty,
    (GREATEST((COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(rbl.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS po_line_pending_qty,
    (GREATEST((COALESCE(rbl.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS po_line_over_received_qty,
    COALESCE(rbl.receipt_count, 0) AS po_line_receipt_count,
    rbl.latest_receipt_date AS po_line_latest_receipt_date,
    COALESCE(rbl.receipt_entries, '[]'::jsonb) AS po_line_receipt_entries,
    (r.id)::text AS receipt_id,
    r.receipt_date,
    r.created_at AS receipt_created_at,
    COALESCE(r.challan_no, ''::text) AS receipt_challan_no,
    (COALESCE(r.qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS receipt_qty,
    (COALESCE(r.rate, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS receipt_rate,
    ((COALESCE(r.qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(r.rate, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(18,2) AS receipt_value,
    COALESCE(r.remarks, ''::text) AS receipt_remarks,
        CASE
            WHEN (pol.id IS NULL) THEN 'PR_PENDING_PO'::text
            WHEN (COALESCE(rbl.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN 'PO_PENDING_RECEIPT'::text
            WHEN (COALESCE(rbl.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric) < COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric)) THEN 'PO_PARTIAL_RECEIPT'::text
            WHEN (COALESCE(rbl.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric) = COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric)) THEN 'PO_RECEIVED'::text
            WHEN (COALESCE(rbl.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric) > COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric)) THEN 'PO_OVER_RECEIVED'::text
            ELSE 'UNKNOWN'::text
        END AS cycle_status,
        CASE
            WHEN (pol.id IS NULL) THEN 1
            WHEN (COALESCE(rbl.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN 2
            WHEN (COALESCE(rbl.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric) < COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric)) THEN 3
            WHEN (COALESCE(rbl.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric) = COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric)) THEN 4
            WHEN (COALESCE(rbl.line_received_qty, (0)::numeric) > COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric)) THEN 5
            ELSE 9
        END AS cycle_status_rank
   FROM ((((((((public.inv_purchase_requests pr
     LEFT JOIN public.inv_items i ON ((i.id = pr.item_id)))
     LEFT JOIN po_by_pr pbp ON ((pbp.pr_no = pr.pr_no)))
     LEFT JOIN direct_pr_receipts dpr ON ((dpr.pr_no = pr.pr_no)))
     LEFT JOIN public.purchase_order_lines pol ON (((upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = 'INVENTORY_PR'::text) AND (pol.source_ref = pr.pr_no))))
     LEFT JOIN public.purchase_orders po ON ((po.id = pol.po_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.purchase_vendors v ON ((v.id = po.vendor_id)))
     LEFT JOIN receipt_by_line rbl ON ((rbl.po_line_id = pol.id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.purchase_po_receipts r ON ((r.po_line_id = pol.id)));


--
-- Name: VIEW v_pr_po_receipt_cycle; Type: COMMENT; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

COMMENT ON VIEW public.v_pr_po_receipt_cycle IS 'End-to-end inventory purchase traceability from PR to PO line to PO receipt, preserving PRs without PO and PO lines without receipt.';


--
-- Name: v_production_jobcard_lookup_fast; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_production_jobcard_lookup_fast WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 WITH artwork_category AS (
         SELECT a.artwork_no,
            max(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'FLEXO'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'CORRUGATION'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'OFFSET'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END) AS department_category
           FROM public.artworks a
          WHERE (COALESCE(a.artwork_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)
          GROUP BY a.artwork_no
        )
 SELECT woj.wo_id,
    wo.wo_number,
    wo.wo_date,
    string_agg(DISTINCT woj.artwork_no, ', '::text ORDER BY woj.artwork_no) AS artwork_nos,
    max(NULLIF(woj.client_name, ''::text)) AS client_name,
    string_agg(DISTINCT woj.product_name, ' / '::text ORDER BY woj.product_name) AS product_names,
    string_agg(DISTINCT woj.so_number, ', '::text ORDER BY woj.so_number) AS so_numbers,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'jobDetails'::text) ->> 'type'::text), ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'FLEXO'::text
            ELSE COALESCE(max(ac.department_category),
            CASE
                WHEN (upper(COALESCE(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'jobDetails'::text) ->> 'type'::text), ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'CORRUGATION'::text
                WHEN (upper(COALESCE(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'jobDetails'::text) ->> 'type'::text), ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'OFFSET'::text
                WHEN (upper(COALESCE(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'jobDetails'::text) ->> 'type'::text), ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'FLEXO'::text
                WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'CORRUGATION'::text
                WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'OFFSET'::text
                WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                ELSE NULL::text
            END)
        END AS department_category
   FROM ((public.work_order_jobs woj
     JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.id = woj.wo_id)))
     LEFT JOIN artwork_category ac ON ((ac.artwork_no = woj.artwork_no)))
  GROUP BY woj.wo_id, wo.wo_number, wo.wo_date, wo.snapshot_json;


--
-- Name: v_production_stage_queue_fast; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_production_stage_queue_fast AS
 WITH RECURSIVE artwork_category AS (
         SELECT a.artwork_no,
            max(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'FLEXO'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'CORRUGATION'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'OFFSET'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(a.product_type, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END) AS department_category
           FROM public.artworks a
          WHERE (COALESCE(a.artwork_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)
          GROUP BY a.artwork_no
        ), snapshot_metrics AS (
         SELECT wo.id AS wo_id,
            COALESCE(( SELECT sum(COALESCE((NULLIF((p.value ->> 'requiredQty'::text), ''::text))::numeric, (NULLIF((p.value ->> 'sheetsWithWaste'::text), ''::text))::numeric, (0)::numeric)) AS sum
                   FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE((wo.snapshot_json -> 'papers'::text), '[]'::jsonb)) p(value)), (0)::numeric) AS paper_sheets_with_waste,
            COALESCE(( SELECT sum(COALESCE((NULLIF((p.value ->> 'coreSheets'::text), ''::text))::numeric, (NULLIF((p.value ->> 'sheets'::text), ''::text))::numeric, (0)::numeric)) AS sum
                   FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE((wo.snapshot_json -> 'papers'::text), '[]'::jsonb)) p(value)), (0)::numeric) AS paper_core_sheets,
            COALESCE(( SELECT sum(COALESCE((NULLIF((j.value ->> 'coreSheets'::text), ''::text))::numeric, (0)::numeric)) AS sum
                   FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE((wo.snapshot_json -> 'jobs'::text), '[]'::jsonb)) j(value)), (0)::numeric) AS job_core_sheets,
            COALESCE((NULLIF(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'flexoDetails'::text) ->> 'totalRunningMeter'::text), ''::text))::numeric, (NULLIF(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'flexoDetails'::text) ->> 'baseRunningMeter'::text), ''::text))::numeric, (0)::numeric) AS flexo_running_meter,
            COALESCE((NULLIF(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'flexoDetails'::text) ->> 'requiredKg'::text), ''::text))::numeric, (0)::numeric) AS flexo_required_kg,
            COALESCE((NULLIF(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'wastage'::text) ->> 'processSheets'::text), ''::text))::numeric, (0)::numeric) AS process_waste_sheets
           FROM public.work_orders wo
        ), stores_issue_plan AS (
         SELECT wo.id AS wo_id,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(split_part(l.remarks, '|'::text, 5), ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['SHEET'::text, 'SHEETS'::text])) THEN COALESCE(l.qty_out, (0)::numeric)
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS issued_sheet_qty,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(split_part(l.remarks, '|'::text, 5), ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['RM'::text, 'R.M.'::text, 'RUNNING METER'::text, 'RUNNING METERS'::text])) THEN COALESCE(l.qty_out, (0)::numeric)
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS issued_rm_qty,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(split_part(l.remarks, '|'::text, 5), ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['KG'::text, 'KGS'::text, 'KILOGRAM'::text, 'KILOGRAMS'::text])) THEN COALESCE(l.qty_out, (0)::numeric)
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS issued_kg_qty
           FROM ((public.work_orders wo
             JOIN public.inv_ledger l ON (((l.ref_type = 'ISSUE'::text) AND (l.ref_no = wo.wo_number))))
             LEFT JOIN public.inv_ledger rev ON (((rev.ref_no = (l.id)::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(rev.ref_type, ''::text)) ~~ 'REV-%'::text))))
          WHERE (rev.id IS NULL)
          GROUP BY wo.id
        ), stores_issue_plan_normalized AS (
         SELECT sip.wo_id,
            sip.issued_sheet_qty,
            sip.issued_rm_qty,
            sip.issued_kg_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((sm.flexo_required_kg > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric) > (sm.flexo_required_kg * (20)::numeric)) AND ((COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric) / (1000)::numeric) <= (sm.flexo_required_kg * 2.5))) THEN (COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric) / (1000)::numeric)
                    ELSE COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric)
                END AS effective_issued_kg_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((sm.flexo_running_meter > (0)::numeric) AND (sm.flexo_required_kg > (0)::numeric)) THEN
                    CASE
                        WHEN (
                        CASE
                            WHEN ((sm.flexo_required_kg > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric) > (sm.flexo_required_kg * (20)::numeric)) AND ((COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric) / (1000)::numeric) <= (sm.flexo_required_kg * 2.5))) THEN (((COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric) / (1000)::numeric) * sm.flexo_running_meter) / sm.flexo_required_kg)
                            ELSE ((COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric) * sm.flexo_running_meter) / sm.flexo_required_kg)
                        END > (sm.flexo_running_meter * (5)::numeric)) THEN sm.flexo_running_meter
                        ELSE
                        CASE
                            WHEN ((sm.flexo_required_kg > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric) > (sm.flexo_required_kg * (20)::numeric)) AND ((COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric) / (1000)::numeric) <= (sm.flexo_required_kg * 2.5))) THEN (((COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric) / (1000)::numeric) * sm.flexo_running_meter) / sm.flexo_required_kg)
                            ELSE ((COALESCE(sip.issued_kg_qty, (0)::numeric) * sm.flexo_running_meter) / sm.flexo_required_kg)
                        END
                    END
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END AS issued_kg_equivalent_rm
           FROM (stores_issue_plan sip
             JOIN snapshot_metrics sm ON ((sm.wo_id = sip.wo_id)))
        ), initial_routing AS (
         SELECT DISTINCT ON (r.wo_id) r.wo_id,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(r.process_name, ''::text), NULLIF(r.department, ''::text), 'PROCESS'::text) AS first_process_name
           FROM public.work_order_routing r
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(r.process_name, ''::text), NULLIF(r.department, ''::text), 'PROCESS'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['PACKING'::text, 'DISPATCH'::text, 'CUTTING PRE'::text, 'CUTTING POST'::text, 'QC'::text]))
          ORDER BY r.wo_id, r.sequence_no, r.id
        ), job_meta AS (
         SELECT woj.wo_id,
            wo.wo_number,
            wo.wo_date,
            string_agg(DISTINCT woj.so_number, ', '::text ORDER BY woj.so_number) AS so_numbers,
            string_agg(DISTINCT (woj.line_no)::text, ', '::text ORDER BY (woj.line_no)::text) AS line_nos,
            string_agg(DISTINCT woj.artwork_no, ', '::text ORDER BY woj.artwork_no) AS artwork_nos,
            max(NULLIF(woj.client_name, ''::text)) AS client_name,
            string_agg(DISTINCT woj.product_name, ' / '::text ORDER BY woj.product_name) AS product_names,
            sum(COALESCE(woj.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS order_qty,
            max(sm.flexo_running_meter) AS flexo_running_meter,
            max(NULLIF(woj.job_priority, ''::text)) AS job_priority,
            max(woj.expected_delivery) AS expected_delivery,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(sum(COALESCE(woj.ups, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric),
                CASE
                    WHEN (COALESCE(NULLIF(max(sm.paper_core_sheets), (0)::numeric), NULLIF(max(sm.job_core_sheets), (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN (sum(COALESCE(woj.qty, (0)::numeric)) / COALESCE(NULLIF(max(sm.paper_core_sheets), (0)::numeric), NULLIF(max(sm.job_core_sheets), (0)::numeric)))
                    ELSE (1)::numeric
                END) AS ups_factor,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'jobDetails'::text) ->> 'type'::text), ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'FLEXO'::text
                    ELSE COALESCE(max(ac.department_category),
                    CASE
                        WHEN (upper(COALESCE(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'jobDetails'::text) ->> 'type'::text), ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'CORRUGATION'::text
                        WHEN (upper(COALESCE(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'jobDetails'::text) ->> 'type'::text), ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'OFFSET'::text
                        WHEN (upper(COALESCE(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'jobDetails'::text) ->> 'type'::text), ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                        WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'FLEXO'::text
                        WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'CORRUGATION'::text
                        WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'OFFSET'::text
                        WHEN (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                        ELSE NULL::text
                    END)
                END AS department_category,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(((wo.snapshot_json -> 'jobDetails'::text) ->> 'type'::text), ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(max(ir.first_process_name), ''::text)) ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(max(ir.first_process_name), ''::text)) ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(max(ac.department_category), ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(max(woj.category), ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text)) THEN GREATEST((COALESCE(max(sipn.issued_rm_qty), (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(max(sipn.issued_kg_equivalent_rm), (0)::numeric)), COALESCE(max(sm.flexo_running_meter), (0)::numeric))
                    ELSE COALESCE(NULLIF(max(sipn.issued_sheet_qty), (0)::numeric), NULLIF(max(sm.paper_sheets_with_waste), (0)::numeric), NULLIF((max(sm.paper_core_sheets) + max(sm.process_waste_sheets)), (0)::numeric), (max(sm.job_core_sheets) + max(sm.process_waste_sheets)))
                END AS stage1_plan_qty
           FROM (((((public.work_order_jobs woj
             JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.id = woj.wo_id)))
             JOIN snapshot_metrics sm ON ((sm.wo_id = wo.id)))
             LEFT JOIN stores_issue_plan_normalized sipn ON ((sipn.wo_id = wo.id)))
             LEFT JOIN initial_routing ir ON ((ir.wo_id = wo.id)))
             LEFT JOIN artwork_category ac ON ((ac.artwork_no = woj.artwork_no)))
          GROUP BY woj.wo_id, wo.wo_number, wo.wo_date, wo.snapshot_json
        ), production_totals AS (
         SELECT pe.routing_id,
            sum(GREATEST(COALESCE(pe.produced_qty, (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric)) AS gross_produced_qty,
            sum(COALESCE(pe.ok_qty, GREATEST((COALESCE(pe.produced_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pe.rejected_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))) AS ok_produced_qty
           FROM public.production_entries pe
          GROUP BY pe.routing_id
        ), routing_base AS (
         SELECT r.id AS routing_id,
            r.wo_id,
            jm.wo_number,
            jm.wo_date,
            jm.so_numbers,
            jm.line_nos,
            jm.artwork_nos,
            jm.client_name,
            jm.product_names,
            jm.order_qty,
            jm.flexo_running_meter,
            jm.job_priority,
            jm.expected_delivery,
            jm.ups_factor,
            jm.department_category,
            r.sequence_no,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(r.process_name, ''::text), NULLIF(r.department, ''::text), 'PROCESS'::text) AS process_name,
            r.department,
            r.planned_machine,
            upper(COALESCE(r.status, ''::text)) AS routing_status,
            COALESCE(pt.gross_produced_qty, (0)::numeric) AS gross_produced_qty,
            COALESCE(pt.ok_produced_qty, (0)::numeric) AS ok_produced_qty,
            jm.stage1_plan_qty,
            row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY r.wo_id ORDER BY r.sequence_no, r.id) AS rn
           FROM ((public.work_order_routing r
             JOIN job_meta jm ON ((jm.wo_id = r.wo_id)))
             LEFT JOIN production_totals pt ON ((pt.routing_id = r.id)))
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(r.process_name, ''::text), NULLIF(r.department, ''::text), 'PROCESS'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['PACKING'::text, 'DISPATCH'::text, 'CUTTING PRE'::text, 'CUTTING POST'::text, 'QC'::text]))
        ), stage_calc AS (
         SELECT rb.routing_id,
            rb.wo_id,
            rb.wo_number,
            rb.wo_date,
            rb.so_numbers,
            rb.line_nos,
            rb.artwork_nos,
            rb.client_name,
            rb.product_names,
            rb.order_qty,
            rb.flexo_running_meter,
            rb.job_priority,
            rb.expected_delivery,
            rb.ups_factor,
            rb.department_category,
            rb.sequence_no,
            rb.process_name,
            rb.department,
            rb.planned_machine,
            rb.routing_status,
            rb.gross_produced_qty AS produced_qty,
            rb.ok_produced_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text)) AND (upper(rb.process_name) !~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN GREATEST(rb.stage1_plan_qty, rb.gross_produced_qty)
                    WHEN (rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) THEN GREATEST(rb.stage1_plan_qty, rb.gross_produced_qty)
                    ELSE rb.stage1_plan_qty
                END AS planned_qty,
            GREATEST((
                CASE
                    WHEN ((rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text)) AND (upper(rb.process_name) !~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN GREATEST(rb.stage1_plan_qty, rb.gross_produced_qty)
                    WHEN (rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) THEN GREATEST(rb.stage1_plan_qty, rb.gross_produced_qty)
                    ELSE rb.stage1_plan_qty
                END - rb.gross_produced_qty), (0)::numeric) AS balance_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) AND (upper(rb.process_name) !~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) AND (upper(rb.process_name) !~~ '%DIECUT%'::text) AND (upper(rb.process_name) !~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN rb.ok_produced_qty
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END AS rm_carry_qty,
            rb.rn
           FROM routing_base rb
          WHERE (rb.rn = 1)
        UNION ALL
         SELECT rb.routing_id,
            rb.wo_id,
            rb.wo_number,
            rb.wo_date,
            rb.so_numbers,
            rb.line_nos,
            rb.artwork_nos,
            rb.client_name,
            rb.product_names,
            rb.order_qty,
            rb.flexo_running_meter,
            rb.job_priority,
            rb.expected_delivery,
            rb.ups_factor,
            rb.department_category,
            rb.sequence_no,
            rb.process_name,
            rb.department,
            rb.planned_machine,
            rb.routing_status,
            rb.gross_produced_qty AS produced_qty,
            rb.ok_produced_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN (rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%PRINT%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%LAMINAT%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%COAT%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN ((rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) OR (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text))) THEN sc_1.rm_carry_qty
                    WHEN ((rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text)) AND (upper(rb.process_name) !~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN sc_1.rm_carry_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%2 PLY%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%TWO PLY%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%PLY MAKING%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%CORRUGATION SHEET PASTING%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%SHEET PASTING%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(sc_1.process_name) ~~ '%PRINT%'::text) THEN round((sc_1.ok_produced_qty * COALESCE(NULLIF(rb.ups_factor, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric)))
                    WHEN (upper(sc_1.process_name) ~~ '%LAMINAT%'::text) THEN round((sc_1.ok_produced_qty * COALESCE(NULLIF(rb.ups_factor, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric)))
                    WHEN (upper(sc_1.process_name) ~~ '%COAT%'::text) THEN round((sc_1.ok_produced_qty * COALESCE(NULLIF(rb.ups_factor, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric)))
                    WHEN (upper(sc_1.process_name) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) THEN round((sc_1.ok_produced_qty * COALESCE(NULLIF(rb.ups_factor, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric)))
                    WHEN (upper(sc_1.process_name) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text) THEN round((sc_1.ok_produced_qty * COALESCE(NULLIF(rb.ups_factor, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric)))
                    ELSE sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                END AS planned_qty,
            GREATEST((
                CASE
                    WHEN (rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%PRINT%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%LAMINAT%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%COAT%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN ((rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) OR (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text))) THEN sc_1.rm_carry_qty
                    WHEN ((rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text)) AND (upper(rb.process_name) !~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN sc_1.rm_carry_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%2 PLY%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%TWO PLY%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%PLY MAKING%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%CORRUGATION SHEET PASTING%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%SHEET PASTING%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(rb.process_name) ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text) THEN sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    WHEN (upper(sc_1.process_name) ~~ '%PRINT%'::text) THEN round((sc_1.ok_produced_qty * COALESCE(NULLIF(rb.ups_factor, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric)))
                    WHEN (upper(sc_1.process_name) ~~ '%LAMINAT%'::text) THEN round((sc_1.ok_produced_qty * COALESCE(NULLIF(rb.ups_factor, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric)))
                    WHEN (upper(sc_1.process_name) ~~ '%COAT%'::text) THEN round((sc_1.ok_produced_qty * COALESCE(NULLIF(rb.ups_factor, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric)))
                    WHEN (upper(sc_1.process_name) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) THEN round((sc_1.ok_produced_qty * COALESCE(NULLIF(rb.ups_factor, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric)))
                    WHEN (upper(sc_1.process_name) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text) THEN round((sc_1.ok_produced_qty * COALESCE(NULLIF(rb.ups_factor, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric)))
                    ELSE sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                END - rb.gross_produced_qty), (0)::numeric) AS balance_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN (rb.department_category = 'FLEXO'::text) THEN
                    CASE
                        WHEN (rb.ok_produced_qty > (0)::numeric) THEN rb.ok_produced_qty
                        ELSE sc_1.ok_produced_qty
                    END
                    ELSE sc_1.rm_carry_qty
                END AS rm_carry_qty,
            rb.rn
           FROM (routing_base rb
             JOIN stage_calc sc_1 ON (((sc_1.wo_id = rb.wo_id) AND (rb.rn = (sc_1.rn + 1)))))
        )
 SELECT routing_id,
    wo_id,
    wo_number,
    wo_date,
    so_numbers,
    line_nos,
    artwork_nos,
    client_name,
    product_names,
    department_category,
    process_name,
    department,
    planned_machine,
    sequence_no,
    expected_delivery,
    job_priority,
    planned_qty,
    produced_qty,
    balance_qty,
        CASE
            WHEN (routing_status = 'SHORT_CLOSED'::text) THEN 'SHORT_CLOSED'::text
            WHEN (routing_status = 'HOLD'::text) THEN 'HOLD'::text
            WHEN ((planned_qty <= (0)::numeric) AND (produced_qty <= (0)::numeric)) THEN 'PENDING'::text
            WHEN (balance_qty <= (0)::numeric) THEN 'COMPLETED'::text
            WHEN (produced_qty > (0)::numeric) THEN 'IN_PROGRESS'::text
            ELSE 'PENDING'::text
        END AS status
   FROM stage_calc sc;


--
-- Name: v_production_stage_rows_fast; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_production_stage_rows_fast AS
 WITH RECURSIVE split_marker AS (
         SELECT q.wo_id,
            min(q.sequence_no) AS split_seq
           FROM public.v_production_stage_queue_fast q
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(q.department_category, ''::text)) <> 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text)))
          GROUP BY q.wo_id
        ), queue_meta AS (
         SELECT DISTINCT q.wo_id,
            q.wo_date,
            q.wo_number,
            q.so_numbers,
            q.line_nos,
            q.artwork_nos,
            q.client_name,
            q.product_names,
            q.department_category,
            q.expected_delivery,
            q.job_priority
           FROM public.v_production_stage_queue_fast q
        ), combined_rows AS (
         SELECT (q.routing_id)::text AS row_key,
            0 AS row_sort,
            ''::text AS job_sort,
            'COMBINED'::text AS row_kind,
            'Combined Sheet Flow'::text AS row_description,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(q.department_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text)) AND (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) !~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN 'RM'::text
                    WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN 'SHEETS'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(q.department_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) THEN 'RM'::text
                    ELSE 'SHEETS'::text
                END AS plan_unit,
            q.wo_id,
            q.wo_date,
            q.routing_id,
            q.wo_number,
            q.so_numbers,
            q.so_numbers AS so_number_display,
            NULL::text AS so_number,
            NULL::text AS line_no,
            NULL::text AS job_reference,
            NULL::numeric AS job_ups,
            q.client_name,
            q.product_names AS product_name,
            q.product_names,
            q.artwork_nos AS artwork_no,
            q.artwork_nos,
            q.process_name,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(q.department_category, ''::text)) = 'DIGITAL'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text)) THEN 'Printing'::text
                    WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(q.department_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text))) THEN 'Inspection/Slitting'::text
                    WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(q.department_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN 'Flexo Die Sheeting'::text
                    WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(q.department_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO PRINT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%ONLINE GOLD FOIL%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%ONLINE SILVER FOIL%'::text))) THEN 'Flexo Printing'::text
                    WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(q.department_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text))) THEN 'Flexo Die Cutting Offline'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) = 'CORRUGATION SHEET PASTING'::text) THEN 'Sheet Pasting'::text
                    ELSE q.process_name
                END AS process_display_name,
            q.department,
            q.planned_machine,
            q.sequence_no,
            q.expected_delivery,
            q.job_priority,
            q.department_category,
            q.planned_qty,
            q.produced_qty,
            q.balance_qty,
            q.status
           FROM (public.v_production_stage_queue_fast q
             LEFT JOIN split_marker sm ON ((sm.wo_id = q.wo_id)))
          WHERE (((sm.split_seq IS NULL) OR (q.sequence_no <= sm.split_seq)) AND (upper(COALESCE(q.process_name, ''::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['PACKING'::text, 'DISPATCH'::text, 'CUTTING PRE'::text, 'CUTTING POST'::text, 'QC'::text])))
        ), job_dimension AS (
         SELECT woj.wo_id,
            woj.so_number,
            (woj.line_no)::text AS line_no,
            woj.job_reference,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(woj.job_reference, ''::text), ((COALESCE(woj.so_number, ''::text) || '||'::text) || COALESCE((woj.line_no)::text, ''::text))) AS job_key,
            max(NULLIF(woj.client_name, ''::text)) AS client_name,
            max(NULLIF(woj.product_name, ''::text)) AS product_name,
            max(NULLIF(woj.artwork_no, ''::text)) AS artwork_no,
            max(NULLIF(woj.job_priority, ''::text)) AS job_priority,
            max(woj.expected_delivery) AS expected_delivery,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(max(COALESCE(woj.ups, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric), NULLIF(max(COALESCE(woj.group_ups, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric) AS job_ups
           FROM public.work_order_jobs woj
          GROUP BY woj.wo_id, woj.so_number, (woj.line_no)::text, woj.job_reference, COALESCE(NULLIF(woj.job_reference, ''::text), ((COALESCE(woj.so_number, ''::text) || '||'::text) || COALESCE((woj.line_no)::text, ''::text)))
        ), routing_entry_totals AS (
         SELECT pe.routing_id,
            sum(GREATEST(COALESCE(pe.produced_qty, (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric)) AS gross_produced_qty,
            sum(COALESCE(pe.ok_qty, GREATEST((COALESCE(pe.produced_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pe.rejected_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))) AS ok_produced_qty
           FROM public.production_entries pe
          GROUP BY pe.routing_id
        ), job_entry_totals AS (
         SELECT pe.routing_id,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(pe.so_number, ''::text), NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM "substring"(COALESCE(pe.downtime_reason, ''::text), 'SO:([^|]+)'::text)), ''::text)) AS so_number,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(pe.line_no, ''::text), NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM "substring"(COALESCE(pe.downtime_reason, ''::text), 'LINE:([^|]+)'::text)), ''::text)) AS line_no,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(pe.job_reference, ''::text), NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM "substring"(COALESCE(pe.downtime_reason, ''::text), 'JOBREF:([^|]+)'::text)), ''::text)) AS job_reference,
            sum(GREATEST(COALESCE(pe.produced_qty, (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric)) AS gross_produced_qty,
            sum(COALESCE(pe.ok_qty, GREATEST((COALESCE(pe.produced_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pe.rejected_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))) AS ok_produced_qty
           FROM public.production_entries pe
          GROUP BY pe.routing_id, COALESCE(NULLIF(pe.so_number, ''::text), NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM "substring"(COALESCE(pe.downtime_reason, ''::text), 'SO:([^|]+)'::text)), ''::text)), COALESCE(NULLIF(pe.line_no, ''::text), NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM "substring"(COALESCE(pe.downtime_reason, ''::text), 'LINE:([^|]+)'::text)), ''::text)), COALESCE(NULLIF(pe.job_reference, ''::text), NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM "substring"(COALESCE(pe.downtime_reason, ''::text), 'JOBREF:([^|]+)'::text)), ''::text))
        ), job_totals AS (
         SELECT jet.routing_id,
            jet.so_number,
            jet.line_no,
            jet.job_reference,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(jet.job_reference, ''::text), ((COALESCE(jet.so_number, ''::text) || '||'::text) || COALESCE(jet.line_no, ''::text))) AS job_key,
            jet.gross_produced_qty,
            jet.ok_produced_qty
           FROM job_entry_totals jet
        ), post_split_routing AS (
         SELECT r.id AS routing_id,
            r.wo_id,
            qm.wo_date,
            qm.wo_number,
            qm.so_numbers,
            qm.line_nos,
            qm.artwork_nos,
            qm.client_name,
            qm.product_names,
            qm.department_category,
            qm.expected_delivery,
            qm.job_priority,
            r.sequence_no,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(r.process_name, ''::text), NULLIF(r.department, ''::text), 'PROCESS'::text) AS process_name,
            r.department,
            r.planned_machine,
            upper(COALESCE(r.status, ''::text)) AS routing_status,
            sm.split_seq,
            row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY r.wo_id ORDER BY r.sequence_no, r.id) AS post_rn
           FROM ((public.work_order_routing r
             JOIN split_marker sm ON ((sm.wo_id = r.wo_id)))
             JOIN queue_meta qm ON ((qm.wo_id = r.wo_id)))
          WHERE ((r.sequence_no > sm.split_seq) AND (upper(COALESCE(r.process_name, ''::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['PACKING'::text, 'DISPATCH'::text, 'CUTTING PRE'::text, 'CUTTING POST'::text, 'QC'::text])))
        ), split_seed AS (
         SELECT psr.wo_id,
            psr.sequence_no,
            psr.post_rn,
            psr.routing_id,
            jd.job_key,
            jd.so_number,
            jd.line_no,
            jd.job_reference,
            jd.client_name,
            jd.product_name,
            jd.artwork_no,
            jd.job_priority,
            jd.expected_delivery,
            jd.job_ups,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%2 PLY%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%TWO PLY%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%PLY MAKING%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORRUGATION SHEET PASTING%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%SHEET PASTING%'::text) OR ((upper(COALESCE(psr.department_category, ''::text)) <> 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text))) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN COALESCE(ret.ok_produced_qty, (0)::numeric)
                    ELSE (COALESCE(ret.ok_produced_qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(NULLIF(jd.job_ups, (0)::numeric), (1)::numeric))
                END AS planned_qty,
            COALESCE(jt.gross_produced_qty, (0)::numeric) AS produced_qty,
            COALESCE(jt.ok_produced_qty, (0)::numeric) AS ok_produced_qty
           FROM (((((post_split_routing psr
             JOIN split_marker sm ON (((sm.wo_id = psr.wo_id) AND (psr.post_rn = 1))))
             JOIN job_dimension jd ON ((jd.wo_id = psr.wo_id)))
             LEFT JOIN public.v_production_stage_queue_fast dc ON (((dc.wo_id = psr.wo_id) AND (dc.sequence_no = sm.split_seq))))
             LEFT JOIN routing_entry_totals ret ON ((ret.routing_id = dc.routing_id)))
             LEFT JOIN job_totals jt ON (((jt.routing_id = psr.routing_id) AND (jt.job_key = jd.job_key))))
        ), job_stage_calc AS (
         SELECT ss.wo_id,
            ss.sequence_no,
            ss.post_rn,
            ss.routing_id,
            ss.job_key,
            ss.so_number,
            ss.line_no,
            ss.job_reference,
            ss.client_name,
            ss.product_name,
            ss.artwork_no,
            ss.job_priority,
            ss.expected_delivery,
            ss.job_ups,
            ss.planned_qty,
            ss.produced_qty,
            ss.ok_produced_qty
           FROM split_seed ss
        UNION ALL
         SELECT nxt.wo_id,
            nxt.sequence_no,
            nxt.post_rn,
            nxt.routing_id,
            prev.job_key,
            prev.so_number,
            prev.line_no,
            prev.job_reference,
            prev.client_name,
            prev.product_name,
            prev.artwork_no,
            prev.job_priority,
            prev.expected_delivery,
            prev.job_ups,
            prev.ok_produced_qty AS planned_qty,
            COALESCE(jt.gross_produced_qty, (0)::numeric) AS produced_qty,
            COALESCE(jt.ok_produced_qty, (0)::numeric) AS ok_produced_qty
           FROM ((job_stage_calc prev
             JOIN post_split_routing nxt ON (((nxt.wo_id = prev.wo_id) AND (nxt.post_rn = (prev.post_rn + 1)))))
             LEFT JOIN job_totals jt ON (((jt.routing_id = nxt.routing_id) AND (jt.job_key = prev.job_key))))
        )
 SELECT cr.row_key,
    cr.row_sort,
    cr.job_sort,
    cr.row_kind,
    cr.row_description,
    cr.plan_unit,
    cr.wo_id,
    cr.wo_date,
    cr.routing_id,
    cr.wo_number,
    cr.so_numbers,
    cr.so_number_display,
    cr.so_number,
    cr.line_no,
    cr.job_reference,
    cr.job_ups,
    cr.client_name,
    cr.product_name,
    cr.product_names,
    cr.artwork_no,
    cr.artwork_nos,
    cr.process_name,
    cr.process_display_name,
    cr.department,
    cr.planned_machine,
    cr.sequence_no,
    cr.expected_delivery,
    cr.job_priority,
    cr.department_category,
    cr.planned_qty,
    cr.produced_qty,
    cr.balance_qty,
    cr.status
   FROM combined_rows cr
UNION ALL
 SELECT (((jsc.routing_id)::text || '||'::text) || jsc.job_key) AS row_key,
    1 AS row_sort,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(jsc.job_reference, ''::text), ((COALESCE(jsc.so_number, ''::text) || '-'::text) || COALESCE(jsc.line_no, ''::text))) AS job_sort,
    'JOB'::text AS row_kind,
    'Separated Job'::text AS row_description,
        CASE
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text)) THEN 'RM'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(psr.department_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text)) AND (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) !~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN 'RM'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%2 PLY%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%TWO PLY%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%PLY MAKING%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORRUGATION SHEET PASTING%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%SHEET PASTING%'::text) OR ((upper(COALESCE(psr.department_category, ''::text)) <> 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text))) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN 'SHEETS'::text
            ELSE 'UNITS'::text
        END AS plan_unit,
    psr.wo_id,
    psr.wo_date,
    psr.routing_id,
    psr.wo_number,
    psr.so_numbers,
    COALESCE(jsc.so_number, ''::text) AS so_number_display,
    jsc.so_number,
    jsc.line_no,
    jsc.job_reference,
    jsc.job_ups,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(jsc.client_name, ''::text), psr.client_name) AS client_name,
    jsc.product_name,
    psr.product_names,
    jsc.artwork_no,
    psr.artwork_nos,
    psr.process_name,
        CASE
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(psr.department_category, ''::text)) = 'DIGITAL'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text)) THEN 'Printing'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(psr.department_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text))) THEN 'Inspection/Slitting'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(psr.department_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE SHEET%'::text)) THEN 'Flexo Die Sheeting'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(psr.department_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO PRINT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%ONLINE GOLD FOIL%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%ONLINE SILVER FOIL%'::text))) THEN 'Flexo Printing'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(psr.department_category, ''::text)) = 'FLEXO'::text) AND ((upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE CUT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIECUT%'::text))) THEN 'Flexo Die Cutting Offline'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(psr.process_name, ''::text)) = 'CORRUGATION SHEET PASTING'::text) THEN 'Sheet Pasting'::text
            ELSE psr.process_name
        END AS process_display_name,
    psr.department,
    psr.planned_machine,
    psr.sequence_no,
    COALESCE(jsc.expected_delivery, psr.expected_delivery) AS expected_delivery,
    COALESCE(jsc.job_priority, psr.job_priority) AS job_priority,
    psr.department_category,
    jsc.planned_qty,
    jsc.produced_qty,
    GREATEST((jsc.planned_qty - jsc.produced_qty), (0)::numeric) AS balance_qty,
        CASE
            WHEN (psr.routing_status = 'SHORT_CLOSED'::text) THEN 'SHORT_CLOSED'::text
            WHEN (psr.routing_status = 'HOLD'::text) THEN 'HOLD'::text
            WHEN ((jsc.planned_qty <= (0)::numeric) AND (jsc.produced_qty <= (0)::numeric)) THEN 'PENDING'::text
            WHEN (GREATEST((jsc.planned_qty - jsc.produced_qty), (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN 'COMPLETED'::text
            WHEN (jsc.produced_qty > (0)::numeric) THEN 'IN_PROGRESS'::text
            ELSE 'PENDING'::text
        END AS status
   FROM (job_stage_calc jsc
     JOIN post_split_routing psr ON ((psr.routing_id = jsc.routing_id)));


--
-- Name: v_purchase_order_summary; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_purchase_order_summary AS
 WITH receipt_by_line AS (
         SELECT r.po_line_id,
            (sum(COALESCE(r.qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS received_qty
           FROM public.purchase_po_receipts r
          GROUP BY r.po_line_id
        ), line_stats AS (
         SELECT (pol.po_id)::text AS po_id,
            (count(*))::integer AS line_count,
            (sum(COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS total_qty,
            (sum(COALESCE(rb.received_qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS received_qty,
            (sum(GREATEST((COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(rb.received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS pending_qty,
            bool_or((upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['ARTWORK_PLATE'::text, 'ARTWORK_DIE'::text, 'DIRECT_PLATE'::text, 'DIRECT_DIE'::text]))) AS has_artwork,
            bool_or((upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['ARTWORK_PLATE'::text, 'DIRECT_PLATE'::text]))) AS has_plate,
            bool_or((upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['ARTWORK_DIE'::text, 'DIRECT_DIE'::text]))) AS has_die
           FROM (public.purchase_order_lines pol
             LEFT JOIN receipt_by_line rb ON ((rb.po_line_id = pol.id)))
          GROUP BY (pol.po_id)::text
        )
 SELECT (po.id)::text AS po_id,
    po.po_no,
    po.order_date,
    (po.vendor_id)::text AS vendor_id,
    COALESCE(po.vendor_name, ''::text) AS vendor_name,
    COALESCE(v.gstin, ''::text) AS vendor_gstin,
    COALESCE(po.status, 'OPEN'::text) AS status,
    COALESCE(ls.line_count, 0) AS line_count,
    (COALESCE(ls.total_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS total_qty,
    (COALESCE(ls.received_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS received_qty,
    (COALESCE(ls.pending_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS pending_qty,
    (COALESCE(po.basic_total, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS basic_total,
    (COALESCE(po.tax_total, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS tax_total,
    (COALESCE(po.total_value, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS total_value,
    COALESCE(po.created_by, ''::text) AS created_by,
    COALESCE(po.payment_terms, ''::text) AS payment_terms,
    COALESCE(po.freight_terms, ''::text) AS freight_terms,
    (COALESCE(po.freight_value, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS freight_value,
    COALESCE(po.delivery_terms, ''::text) AS delivery_terms,
    COALESCE(po.notes, ''::text) AS notes,
    po.created_at,
    po.updated_at,
        CASE
            WHEN COALESCE(ls.has_artwork, false) THEN 'ARTWORK_PROCUREMENT'::text
            ELSE 'INVENTORY_PROCUREMENT'::text
        END AS workflow,
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(ls.has_plate, false) AND COALESCE(ls.has_die, false)) THEN 'MIXED'::text
            WHEN COALESCE(ls.has_die, false) THEN 'DIE'::text
            WHEN COALESCE(ls.has_plate, false) THEN 'PLATE'::text
            ELSE 'INVENTORY'::text
        END AS artwork_type
   FROM ((public.purchase_orders po
     LEFT JOIN public.purchase_vendors v ON ((v.id = po.vendor_id)))
     LEFT JOIN line_stats ls ON ((ls.po_id = (po.id)::text)));


--
-- Name: v_purchase_requests_open; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_purchase_requests_open AS
 WITH po_alloc_base AS (
         SELECT pol.source_ref AS pr_no,
            (sum(COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS ordered_qty,
            (sum(GREATEST((COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(r.received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS open_po_qty,
            (max(pol.rate))::numeric(18,2) AS po_rate,
            string_agg(DISTINCT pol.po_no, ', '::text ORDER BY pol.po_no) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(pol.po_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS po_refs
           FROM (public.purchase_order_lines pol
             LEFT JOIN ( SELECT purchase_po_receipts.po_line_id,
                    (sum(COALESCE(purchase_po_receipts.qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS received_qty
                   FROM public.purchase_po_receipts
                  GROUP BY purchase_po_receipts.po_line_id) r ON ((r.po_line_id = pol.id)))
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = 'INVENTORY_PR'::text)
          GROUP BY pol.source_ref
        )
 SELECT pr.pr_no,
    pr.created_at,
    COALESCE(pr.item_code, ''::text) AS item_code,
    COALESCE(pr.item_name, ''::text) AS item_name,
    (COALESCE(pr.requested_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS requested_qty,
    (COALESCE(pr.received_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS received_qty,
    COALESCE(pr.department, ''::text) AS department,
    COALESCE(pr.job_ref, ''::text) AS job_ref,
    COALESCE(pr.remarks, ''::text) AS remarks,
    COALESCE(pr.status, 'OPEN'::text) AS status,
    COALESCE(po.ordered_qty, (0)::numeric) AS pending_qty,
    (COALESCE(po.open_po_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS open_po_qty,
    (COALESCE(po.po_rate, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS po_rate,
    (0)::numeric(18,2) AS tax_pct,
    (GREATEST(((COALESCE(pr.requested_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pr.received_qty, (0)::numeric)) - COALESCE(po.open_po_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS available_to_order_qty,
    COALESCE(po.po_refs, ''::text) AS po_refs
   FROM (public.inv_purchase_requests pr
     LEFT JOIN po_alloc_base po ON ((po.pr_no = pr.pr_no)))
  WHERE (upper(COALESCE(pr.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CLOSED'::text);


--
-- Name: v_purchase_dashboard_core_metrics; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_purchase_dashboard_core_metrics AS
 SELECT (COALESCE(( SELECT count(*) AS count
           FROM public.v_purchase_requests_open r
          WHERE (COALESCE(r.available_to_order_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric)), (0)::bigint))::integer AS pending_req_count,
    (COALESCE(( SELECT sum(COALESCE(r.available_to_order_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS sum
           FROM public.v_purchase_requests_open r
          WHERE (COALESCE(r.available_to_order_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS pending_req_qty,
    (COALESCE(( SELECT count(*) AS count
           FROM public.v_purchase_order_summary s
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(s.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['OPEN'::text, 'PARTIAL'::text]))), (0)::bigint))::integer AS open_po_count,
    (COALESCE(( SELECT sum(COALESCE(s.pending_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS sum
           FROM public.v_purchase_order_summary s
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(s.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['OPEN'::text, 'PARTIAL'::text]))), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS open_po_pending_qty,
    (COALESCE(( SELECT sum(COALESCE(s.total_value, (0)::numeric)) AS sum
           FROM public.v_purchase_order_summary s), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS total_po_value,
    (COALESCE(( SELECT sum(COALESCE(s.total_value, (0)::numeric)) AS sum
           FROM public.v_purchase_order_summary s
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(s.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['OPEN'::text, 'PARTIAL'::text]))), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS open_po_value,
    (COALESCE(( SELECT count(*) AS count
           FROM public.purchase_vendors v
          WHERE COALESCE(v.is_active, true)), (0)::bigint))::integer AS active_vendors;


--
-- Name: v_purchase_lead_time_items; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_purchase_lead_time_items AS
 SELECT (id)::text AS id,
    COALESCE(item_code, ''::text) AS item_code,
    COALESCE(item_name, ''::text) AS item_name,
    COALESCE(category, ''::text) AS category,
    COALESCE(department, ''::text) AS department,
    COALESCE(uom, ''::text) AS uom,
    COALESCE(active, true) AS active,
    lead_time_days,
    moq_qty,
    lead_time_updated_at,
    COALESCE(lead_time_updated_by, ''::text) AS lead_time_updated_by
   FROM public.inv_items i;


--
-- Name: v_purchase_orders_read_model; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_purchase_orders_read_model AS
 WITH receipt_by_line AS (
         SELECT r.po_line_id,
            (sum(COALESCE(r.qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS received_qty,
            max(r.receipt_date) AS latest_receipt_date,
            jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', (r.id)::text, 'poId', COALESCE((r.po_id)::text, ''::text), 'poNo', COALESCE(r.po_no, ''::text), 'poLineId', COALESCE((r.po_line_id)::text, ''::text), 'lineNo', COALESCE(r.line_no, 0), 'sourceType', COALESCE(r.source_type, ''::text), 'receiptDate', r.receipt_date, 'challanNo', COALESCE(r.challan_no, ''::text), 'qty', COALESCE(r.qty, (0)::numeric), 'rate', COALESCE(r.rate, (0)::numeric), 'remarks', COALESCE(r.remarks, ''::text), 'createdAt', r.created_at) ORDER BY r.receipt_date DESC, r.created_at DESC, r.id DESC) AS receipt_entries
           FROM public.purchase_po_receipts r
          GROUP BY r.po_line_id
        )
 SELECT (po.id)::text AS po_id,
    po.po_no,
    po.order_date,
    (po.vendor_id)::text AS vendor_id,
    po.vendor_name,
    COALESCE(v.gstin, ''::text) AS vendor_gstin,
    po.vendor_email,
    po.vendor_phone,
    po.vendor_address,
    po.payment_terms,
    po.freight_terms,
    (COALESCE(po.freight_value, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS freight_value,
    po.delivery_terms,
    po.notes,
    po.status,
    (COALESCE(po.total_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS total_qty,
    (COALESCE(po.basic_total, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS basic_total,
    (COALESCE(po.tax_total, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS tax_total,
    (COALESCE(po.total_value, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS total_value,
    po.created_by,
    po.created_at,
    po.updated_at,
    (pol.id)::text AS line_id,
    pol.line_no,
    pol.source_type,
    pol.source_ref,
    pol.item_code,
    pol.item_name,
    (COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS qty,
    (COALESCE(pol.rate, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS rate,
    (COALESCE(pol.tax_pct, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS tax_pct,
    (COALESCE(pol.amount, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS amount,
    (COALESCE(pol.tax_amount, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS tax_amount,
    (COALESCE(pol.total_amount, pol.amount, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS total_amount,
    pol.department,
    pol.job_ref,
    pol.remarks,
    pol.created_at AS line_created_at,
    (COALESCE(rb.received_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS received_qty,
    (GREATEST((COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(rb.received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS pending_qty,
    (GREATEST((COALESCE(rb.received_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS over_received_qty,
    COALESCE(rb.latest_receipt_date, NULL::date) AS latest_receipt_date,
    COALESCE(rb.receipt_entries, '[]'::jsonb) AS receipt_entries
   FROM (((public.purchase_orders po
     JOIN public.purchase_order_lines pol ON ((pol.po_id = po.id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.purchase_vendors v ON ((v.id = po.vendor_id)))
     LEFT JOIN receipt_by_line rb ON ((rb.po_line_id = pol.id)));


--
-- Name: v_purchase_plate_die_jobs; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_purchase_plate_die_jobs WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 SELECT so.id AS so_id,
    so.so_number,
    so.so_date,
    so.client_code,
    a.line_no,
    a.artwork_no,
    a.plate_status,
    a.die_status
   FROM (public.artworks a
     JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = a.so_id)))
  WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(a.plate_status, ''::text)) = 'NEW'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(a.die_status, ''::text)) = 'NEW'::text));


--
-- Name: v_purchase_po_print_lines; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_purchase_po_print_lines AS
 SELECT (po.id)::text AS po_id,
    po.po_no,
    po.order_date,
    COALESCE(po.status, 'OPEN'::text) AS status,
    COALESCE(po.created_by, ''::text) AS created_by,
    COALESCE(po.payment_terms, ''::text) AS payment_terms,
    COALESCE(po.freight_terms, ''::text) AS freight_terms,
    (COALESCE(po.freight_value, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS freight_value,
    COALESCE(po.delivery_terms, ''::text) AS delivery_terms,
    COALESCE(po.notes, ''::text) AS notes,
    (v.id)::text AS vendor_id,
    COALESCE(v.vendor_code, ''::text) AS vendor_code,
    COALESCE(v.vendor_name, po.vendor_name, ''::text) AS vendor_name,
    COALESCE(v.gstin, ''::text) AS vendor_gstin,
