    COALESCE(v.phone, po.vendor_phone, ''::text) AS vendor_phone,
    COALESCE(v.email, po.vendor_email, ''::text) AS vendor_email,
    COALESCE(v.address, po.vendor_address, ''::text) AS vendor_address,
    COALESCE(v.city, ''::text) AS vendor_city,
    COALESCE(v.state, ''::text) AS vendor_state,
    (pol.id)::text AS line_id,
    pol.line_no,
    COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text) AS source_type,
    COALESCE(pol.source_ref, ''::text) AS source_ref,
    COALESCE(pol.item_code, ''::text) AS item_code,
    COALESCE(pol.item_name, ''::text) AS item_name,
    (COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS qty,
    (COALESCE(pol.rate, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS rate,
    (COALESCE(pol.tax_pct, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS tax_pct,
    (COALESCE(pol.amount, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS amount,
    (COALESCE(pol.tax_amount, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS tax_amount,
    (COALESCE(pol.total_amount, pol.amount, (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,2) AS total_amount,
    COALESCE(pol.department, ''::text) AS department,
    COALESCE(pol.job_ref, ''::text) AS job_ref,
    COALESCE(pol.remarks, ''::text) AS remarks,
    ''::text AS hsn_code,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['ARTWORK_PLATE'::text, 'ARTWORK_DIE'::text, 'DIRECT_PLATE'::text, 'DIRECT_DIE'::text])) THEN 'PCS'::text
            ELSE COALESCE(NULLIF(i.uom, ''::text), ''::text)
        END AS stock_unit
   FROM (((public.purchase_orders po
     JOIN public.purchase_order_lines pol ON ((pol.po_id = po.id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.purchase_vendors v ON ((v.id = po.vendor_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.inv_items i ON ((i.item_code = pol.item_code)));


--
-- Name: v_report_planning_tooling_status; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_planning_tooling_status AS
 WITH artwork_ranked AS (
         SELECT so.so_number,
            a.so_id,
            a.line_no,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(a.artwork_no, ''::text), (((a.so_id)::text || '||'::text) || (a.line_no)::text)) AS artwork_key,
            a.artwork_no,
            a.plate_status,
            a.die_status,
            row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY a.so_id, a.line_no ORDER BY a.approved_at DESC NULLS LAST, a.artwork_at DESC NULLS LAST, a.id DESC) AS rn
           FROM (public.artworks a
             JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = a.so_id)))
        ), artwork_base AS (
         SELECT artwork_ranked.so_number,
            artwork_ranked.so_id,
            artwork_ranked.line_no,
            artwork_ranked.artwork_key,
            artwork_ranked.artwork_no,
            artwork_ranked.plate_status,
            artwork_ranked.die_status,
            artwork_ranked.rn
           FROM artwork_ranked
          WHERE (artwork_ranked.rn = 1)
        ), manual_proc AS (
         SELECT purchase_artwork_procurement.artwork_key,
            upper(COALESCE(purchase_artwork_procurement.type, ''::text)) AS tooling_type,
            max(purchase_artwork_procurement.vendor) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(purchase_artwork_procurement.vendor, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS vendor,
            min(purchase_artwork_procurement.ordered_on) AS ordered_on,
            max(purchase_artwork_procurement.received_on) AS received_on,
            string_agg(DISTINCT purchase_artwork_procurement.challan_no, ', '::text ORDER BY purchase_artwork_procurement.challan_no) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(purchase_artwork_procurement.challan_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS challan_nos,
            sum(COALESCE(purchase_artwork_procurement.count, (0)::numeric)) AS manual_qty
           FROM public.purchase_artwork_procurement
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(purchase_artwork_procurement.status, 'ACTIVE'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text)
          GROUP BY purchase_artwork_procurement.artwork_key, (upper(COALESCE(purchase_artwork_procurement.type, ''::text)))
        ), receipt_by_line AS (
         SELECT purchase_po_receipts.po_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(purchase_po_receipts.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS received_qty,
            max(purchase_po_receipts.receipt_date) AS last_receipt_date,
            string_agg(DISTINCT purchase_po_receipts.challan_no, ', '::text ORDER BY purchase_po_receipts.challan_no) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(purchase_po_receipts.challan_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS challan_nos
           FROM public.purchase_po_receipts
          GROUP BY purchase_po_receipts.po_line_id
        ), po_tooling_lines AS (
         SELECT pol.source_ref AS artwork_key,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['ARTWORK_DIE'::text, 'DIRECT_DIE'::text])) THEN 'DIE'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['ARTWORK_PLATE'::text, 'DIRECT_PLATE'::text])) THEN 'PLATE'::text
                    ELSE ''::text
                END AS tooling_type,
            pol.po_no,
            po.order_date,
            COALESCE(po.vendor_name, ''::text) AS vendor_name,
            COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric) AS ordered_qty,
            COALESCE(r.received_qty, (0)::numeric) AS received_qty,
            GREATEST((COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(r.received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS pending_qty,
            r.last_receipt_date,
            r.challan_nos
           FROM ((public.purchase_order_lines pol
             LEFT JOIN public.purchase_orders po ON ((po.po_no = pol.po_no)))
             LEFT JOIN receipt_by_line r ON ((r.po_line_id = pol.id)))
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['ARTWORK_PLATE'::text, 'ARTWORK_DIE'::text, 'DIRECT_PLATE'::text, 'DIRECT_DIE'::text]))
        ), po_tooling AS (
         SELECT po_tooling_lines.artwork_key,
            po_tooling_lines.tooling_type,
            sum(po_tooling_lines.ordered_qty) AS ordered_qty,
            sum(po_tooling_lines.received_qty) AS received_qty,
            sum(po_tooling_lines.pending_qty) AS pending_qty,
            min(po_tooling_lines.order_date) AS first_order_date,
            max(po_tooling_lines.last_receipt_date) AS last_receipt_date,
            string_agg(DISTINCT po_tooling_lines.po_no, ', '::text ORDER BY po_tooling_lines.po_no) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(po_tooling_lines.po_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS po_nos,
            string_agg(DISTINCT po_tooling_lines.vendor_name, ', '::text ORDER BY po_tooling_lines.vendor_name) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(po_tooling_lines.vendor_name, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS vendors,
            string_agg(DISTINCT po_tooling_lines.challan_nos, ', '::text ORDER BY po_tooling_lines.challan_nos) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(po_tooling_lines.challan_nos, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS challan_nos
           FROM po_tooling_lines
          WHERE (po_tooling_lines.tooling_type <> ''::text)
          GROUP BY po_tooling_lines.artwork_key, po_tooling_lines.tooling_type
        )
 SELECT ab.so_number,
    ab.line_no,
    ab.artwork_key,
        CASE
            WHEN ((COALESCE(pt.pending_qty, (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) AND ((COALESCE(pt.received_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) OR (mp.received_on IS NOT NULL))) THEN 'RECEIVED'::text
            WHEN (COALESCE(pt.received_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN 'PARTIAL'::text
            WHEN ((COALESCE(pt.ordered_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) OR (mp.ordered_on IS NOT NULL)) THEN 'ORDERED'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(ab.plate_status, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['NEW'::text, 'YES'::text, 'REQUIRED'::text, 'SELECTED'::text])) THEN 'PENDING_ORDER'::text
            ELSE COALESCE(NULLIF(ab.plate_status, ''::text), 'NA'::text)
        END AS plate_procurement_status,
    COALESCE(pt.po_nos, ''::text) AS plate_po_nos,
    COALESCE(pt.first_order_date, mp.ordered_on) AS plate_ordered_on,
    COALESCE(pt.last_receipt_date, mp.received_on) AS plate_received_on,
    COALESCE(pt.ordered_qty, mp.manual_qty, (0)::numeric) AS plate_ordered_qty,
    COALESCE(pt.received_qty,
        CASE
            WHEN (mp.received_on IS NOT NULL) THEN mp.manual_qty
            ELSE (0)::numeric
        END, (0)::numeric) AS plate_received_qty,
    COALESCE(pt.pending_qty, GREATEST((COALESCE(mp.manual_qty, (0)::numeric) -
        CASE
            WHEN (mp.received_on IS NOT NULL) THEN COALESCE(mp.manual_qty, (0)::numeric)
            ELSE (0)::numeric
        END), (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric) AS plate_pending_qty,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(pt.vendors, ''::text), mp.vendor, ''::text) AS plate_vendors,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(pt.challan_nos, ''::text), mp.challan_nos, ''::text) AS plate_receipt_refs,
        CASE
            WHEN ((COALESCE(dt.pending_qty, (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) AND ((COALESCE(dt.received_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) OR (md.received_on IS NOT NULL))) THEN 'RECEIVED'::text
            WHEN (COALESCE(dt.received_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN 'PARTIAL'::text
            WHEN ((COALESCE(dt.ordered_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) OR (md.ordered_on IS NOT NULL)) THEN 'ORDERED'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(ab.die_status, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['NEW'::text, 'YES'::text, 'REQUIRED'::text, 'SELECTED'::text])) THEN 'PENDING_ORDER'::text
            ELSE COALESCE(NULLIF(ab.die_status, ''::text), 'NA'::text)
        END AS die_procurement_status,
    COALESCE(dt.po_nos, ''::text) AS die_po_nos,
    COALESCE(dt.first_order_date, md.ordered_on) AS die_ordered_on,
    COALESCE(dt.last_receipt_date, md.received_on) AS die_received_on,
    COALESCE(dt.ordered_qty, md.manual_qty, (0)::numeric) AS die_ordered_qty,
    COALESCE(dt.received_qty,
        CASE
            WHEN (md.received_on IS NOT NULL) THEN md.manual_qty
            ELSE (0)::numeric
        END, (0)::numeric) AS die_received_qty,
    COALESCE(dt.pending_qty, GREATEST((COALESCE(md.manual_qty, (0)::numeric) -
        CASE
            WHEN (md.received_on IS NOT NULL) THEN COALESCE(md.manual_qty, (0)::numeric)
            ELSE (0)::numeric
        END), (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric) AS die_pending_qty,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(dt.vendors, ''::text), md.vendor, ''::text) AS die_vendors,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(dt.challan_nos, ''::text), md.challan_nos, ''::text) AS die_receipt_refs
   FROM ((((artwork_base ab
     LEFT JOIN po_tooling pt ON (((pt.artwork_key = ab.artwork_key) AND (pt.tooling_type = 'PLATE'::text))))
     LEFT JOIN po_tooling dt ON (((dt.artwork_key = ab.artwork_key) AND (dt.tooling_type = 'DIE'::text))))
     LEFT JOIN manual_proc mp ON (((mp.artwork_key = ab.artwork_key) AND (mp.tooling_type = 'PLATE'::text))))
     LEFT JOIN manual_proc md ON (((md.artwork_key = ab.artwork_key) AND (md.tooling_type = 'DIE'::text))));


--
-- Name: v_purchase_tooling_pending_summary; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_purchase_tooling_pending_summary AS
 WITH active_jobs AS (
         SELECT COALESCE(NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(v_artwork_jobs_active.artwork_no, ''::text)), ''::text), ((COALESCE((v_artwork_jobs_active.id)::text, ''::text) || '||'::text) || COALESCE((v_artwork_jobs_active.line_no)::text, ''::text))) AS artwork_key,
            v_artwork_jobs_active.so_number,
            (v_artwork_jobs_active.line_no)::text AS line_no,
            upper(COALESCE(v_artwork_jobs_active.plate_status, ''::text)) AS plate_status,
            upper(COALESCE(v_artwork_jobs_active.die_status, ''::text)) AS die_status
           FROM public.v_artwork_jobs_active
        ), tooling_state AS (
         SELECT a.artwork_key,
                CASE
                    WHEN (NOT ((a.plate_status = 'NEW'::text) OR (a.plate_status = 'YES'::text) OR (a.plate_status = 'REQUIRED'::text) OR (a.plate_status = 'SELECTED'::text) OR (a.plate_status ~~ '%NEW%'::text))) THEN false
                    ELSE COALESCE((NULLIF(upper(COALESCE(t.plate_procurement_status, ''::text)), ''::text) <> ALL (ARRAY['RECEIVED'::text, 'NA'::text])),
                    CASE
                        WHEN (COALESCE(t.plate_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN true
                        WHEN (GREATEST((COALESCE(t.plate_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(t.plate_received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN false
                        ELSE true
                    END)
                END AS plate_pending,
                CASE
                    WHEN (NOT ((a.die_status = 'NEW'::text) OR (a.die_status = 'YES'::text) OR (a.die_status = 'REQUIRED'::text) OR (a.die_status = 'SELECTED'::text) OR (a.die_status ~~ '%NEW%'::text))) THEN false
                    ELSE COALESCE((NULLIF(upper(COALESCE(t.die_procurement_status, ''::text)), ''::text) <> ALL (ARRAY['RECEIVED'::text, 'NA'::text])),
                    CASE
                        WHEN (COALESCE(t.die_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN true
                        WHEN (GREATEST((COALESCE(t.die_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(t.die_received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN false
                        ELSE true
                    END)
                END AS die_pending
           FROM (active_jobs a
             LEFT JOIN public.v_report_planning_tooling_status t ON (((t.so_number = a.so_number) AND ((t.line_no)::text = a.line_no))))
        )
 SELECT 'PLATE'::text AS type,
    count(DISTINCT tooling_state.artwork_key) FILTER (WHERE tooling_state.plate_pending) AS pending_count
   FROM tooling_state
UNION ALL
 SELECT 'DIE'::text AS type,
    count(DISTINCT tooling_state.artwork_key) FILTER (WHERE tooling_state.die_pending) AS pending_count
   FROM tooling_state;


--
-- Name: v_purchase_tooling_workbench; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_purchase_tooling_workbench AS
 WITH base AS (
         SELECT COALESCE(NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(a.artwork_no, ''::text)), ''::text), ((COALESCE((a.id)::text, ''::text) || '||'::text) || COALESCE((a.line_no)::text, ''::text))) AS artwork_key,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(btrim(COALESCE(a.artwork_no, ''::text)), ''::text), 'UNASSIGNED'::text) AS artwork_no,
            (a.id)::text AS so_id,
            COALESCE(a.so_number, ''::text) AS so_number,
            a.so_date,
            (a.line_no)::text AS line_no,
            COALESCE(a.client_name, ''::text) AS client_name,
            COALESCE(a.product_name, ''::text) AS product_name,
            (COALESCE(a.qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS qty,
            COALESCE(a.unit, ''::text) AS unit,
            (COALESCE(a.sheet_ups, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS sheet_ups,
            COALESCE(a.plate_status, ''::text) AS plate_status,
            COALESCE(a.die_status, ''::text) AS die_status,
            COALESCE(a.plate_size, ''::text) AS plate_size,
            (COALESCE(a.plate_count, 0))::numeric(14,4) AS plate_count,
            COALESCE(a.hybrid_plate_size, ''::text) AS hybrid_plate_size,
            (COALESCE(a.hybrid_plate_count, 0))::numeric(14,4) AS hybrid_plate_count,
            (COALESCE(a.die_count, 0))::numeric(14,4) AS die_count,
            COALESCE(t.plate_vendors, ''::text) AS plate_vendors,
            t.plate_ordered_on,
            t.plate_received_on,
            COALESCE(t.plate_receipt_refs, ''::text) AS plate_receipt_refs,
            (COALESCE(t.plate_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS plate_ordered_qty,
            (COALESCE(t.plate_received_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS plate_received_qty,
            COALESCE(t.plate_po_nos, ''::text) AS plate_po_nos,
            COALESCE(t.plate_procurement_status, ''::text) AS plate_procurement_status,
            COALESCE(t.die_vendors, ''::text) AS die_vendors,
            t.die_ordered_on,
            t.die_received_on,
            COALESCE(t.die_receipt_refs, ''::text) AS die_receipt_refs,
            (COALESCE(t.die_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS die_ordered_qty,
            (COALESCE(t.die_received_qty, (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS die_received_qty,
            COALESCE(t.die_po_nos, ''::text) AS die_po_nos,
            COALESCE(t.die_procurement_status, ''::text) AS die_procurement_status
           FROM (public.v_artwork_jobs_active a
             LEFT JOIN public.v_report_planning_tooling_status t ON (((t.so_number = a.so_number) AND ((t.line_no)::text = (a.line_no)::text))))
        )
 SELECT artwork_key AS id,
    artwork_key,
    max(artwork_no) AS artwork_no,
    min(so_date) AS so_date,
    COALESCE(string_agg(DISTINCT so_number, ', '::text ORDER BY so_number), ''::text) AS so,
    COALESCE(string_agg(DISTINCT line_no, ', '::text ORDER BY b.line_no), ''::text) AS line_no,
    ''::text AS wo_nos,
    COALESCE(string_agg(DISTINCT client_name, ', '::text ORDER BY client_name), ''::text) AS client,
    COALESCE(string_agg(DISTINCT product_name, ' / '::text ORDER BY b.product_name), ''::text) AS product_name,
    (sum(COALESCE(qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS qty,
    max(COALESCE(unit, ''::text)) AS unit,
    (max(COALESCE(sheet_ups, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS artwork_ups,
    jsonb_agg(DISTINCT jsonb_build_object('soId', so_id, 'soNo', so_number, 'lineNo', line_no, 'artworkNo', artwork_no, 'sheetUps', sheet_ups)) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(so_id, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS member_refs,
    max(
        CASE
            WHEN ((upper(plate_status) = ANY (ARRAY['NEW'::text, 'YES'::text, 'REQUIRED'::text, 'SELECTED'::text])) OR (upper(plate_status) ~~ '%NEW%'::text)) THEN plate_status
            ELSE ''::text
        END) AS plate_status_raw,
    max(
        CASE
            WHEN ((upper(die_status) = ANY (ARRAY['NEW'::text, 'YES'::text, 'REQUIRED'::text, 'SELECTED'::text])) OR (upper(die_status) ~~ '%NEW%'::text)) THEN die_status
            ELSE ''::text
        END) AS die_status_raw,
    bool_or(((upper(plate_status) = ANY (ARRAY['NEW'::text, 'YES'::text, 'REQUIRED'::text, 'SELECTED'::text])) OR (upper(plate_status) ~~ '%NEW%'::text))) AS plate_is_new,
    bool_or(((upper(die_status) = ANY (ARRAY['NEW'::text, 'YES'::text, 'REQUIRED'::text, 'SELECTED'::text])) OR (upper(die_status) ~~ '%NEW%'::text))) AS die_is_new,
    COALESCE(string_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(btrim(plate_vendors), ''::text), ', '::text ORDER BY NULLIF(btrim(plate_vendors), ''::text)), ''::text) AS plate_vendor,
    max(plate_ordered_on) AS plate_ordered_on,
    max(plate_received_on) AS plate_received_on,
    COALESCE(string_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(btrim(plate_receipt_refs), ''::text), ', '::text ORDER BY NULLIF(btrim(plate_receipt_refs), ''::text)), ''::text) AS plate_challan_no,
    max(NULLIF(btrim(plate_size), ''::text)) AS plate_size,
    (max(COALESCE(plate_count, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS plate_count,
    (max(COALESCE(hybrid_plate_count, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS hybrid_plate_count,
    max(NULLIF(btrim(hybrid_plate_size), ''::text)) AS hybrid_plate_size,
    ''::text AS plate_cost,
    (sum(COALESCE(plate_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS plate_ordered_qty,
    (sum(COALESCE(plate_received_qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS plate_received_qty,
    (GREATEST((sum(COALESCE(plate_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric)) - sum(COALESCE(plate_received_qty, (0)::numeric))), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS plate_pending_qty,
    COALESCE(string_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(btrim(plate_po_nos), ''::text), ', '::text ORDER BY NULLIF(btrim(plate_po_nos), ''::text)), ''::text) AS plate_po_refs,
    COALESCE(max(NULLIF(btrim(upper(plate_procurement_status)), ''::text)),
        CASE
            WHEN (sum(COALESCE(plate_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric)) <= (0)::numeric) THEN 'PENDING'::text
            WHEN (GREATEST((sum(COALESCE(plate_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric)) - sum(COALESCE(plate_received_qty, (0)::numeric))), (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN 'RECEIVED'::text
            WHEN (sum(COALESCE(plate_received_qty, (0)::numeric)) > (0)::numeric) THEN 'PARTIAL'::text
            ELSE 'ORDERED'::text
        END) AS status_plate,
    COALESCE(string_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(btrim(die_vendors), ''::text), ', '::text ORDER BY NULLIF(btrim(die_vendors), ''::text)), ''::text) AS die_vendor,
    max(die_ordered_on) AS die_ordered_on,
    max(die_received_on) AS die_received_on,
    COALESCE(string_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(btrim(die_receipt_refs), ''::text), ', '::text ORDER BY NULLIF(btrim(die_receipt_refs), ''::text)), ''::text) AS die_challan_no,
    (max(COALESCE(die_count, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS die_count,
    ''::text AS die_cost,
    (sum(COALESCE(die_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS die_ordered_qty,
    (sum(COALESCE(die_received_qty, (0)::numeric)))::numeric(14,4) AS die_received_qty,
    (GREATEST((sum(COALESCE(die_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric)) - sum(COALESCE(die_received_qty, (0)::numeric))), (0)::numeric))::numeric(14,4) AS die_pending_qty,
    COALESCE(string_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(btrim(die_po_nos), ''::text), ', '::text ORDER BY NULLIF(btrim(die_po_nos), ''::text)), ''::text) AS die_po_refs,
    COALESCE(max(NULLIF(btrim(upper(die_procurement_status)), ''::text)),
        CASE
            WHEN (sum(COALESCE(die_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric)) <= (0)::numeric) THEN 'PENDING'::text
            WHEN (GREATEST((sum(COALESCE(die_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric)) - sum(COALESCE(die_received_qty, (0)::numeric))), (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN 'RECEIVED'::text
            WHEN (sum(COALESCE(die_received_qty, (0)::numeric)) > (0)::numeric) THEN 'PARTIAL'::text
            ELSE 'ORDERED'::text
        END) AS status_die,
    array_agg(DISTINCT so_number) FILTER (WHERE (so_number <> ''::text)) AS so_list,
    array_agg(DISTINCT line_no) FILTER (WHERE (line_no <> ''::text)) AS line_list,
    ARRAY[]::text[] AS wo_list,
    array_agg(DISTINCT client_name) FILTER (WHERE (client_name <> ''::text)) AS client_list,
    array_agg(DISTINCT product_name) FILTER (WHERE (product_name <> ''::text)) AS product_list,
    (count(DISTINCT so_number))::integer AS so_count
   FROM base b
  GROUP BY artwork_key;


--
-- Name: v_report_billing_register; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_billing_register AS
 SELECT il.id AS invoice_line_id,
    il.invoice_id,
    inv.invoice_no,
    inv.invoice_date,
    inv.created_at,
    inv.posted_at,
    upper(COALESCE(inv.status, 'POSTED'::text)) AS invoice_status,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(inv.document_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['INVOICE'::text, 'CHALLAN'::text])) THEN upper(COALESCE(inv.document_type, ''::text))
            ELSE 'INVOICE'::text
        END AS document_type,
    COALESCE(inv.billing_mode, ''::text) AS billing_mode,
    COALESCE(inv.client_code, so.client_code, ''::text) AS client_code,
    COALESCE(inv.client_name, c.client_name, inv.client_code, so.client_code, ''::text) AS client_name,
    il.so_id,
    il.so_line_id,
    COALESCE(il.so_number, so.so_number) AS so_number,
    so.so_date,
    COALESCE(il.line_no, sol.line_no) AS line_no,
    COALESCE(so.sales_rep, ''::text) AS sales_rep,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(btrim(il.division), ''::text), NULLIF(btrim(sol.division), ''::text), ''::text) AS division,
    COALESCE(sol.product_code, il.product_code, ''::text) AS product_code,
    COALESCE(sol.product_name, il.product_name, ''::text) AS product_name,
    COALESCE(sol.category, ''::text) AS category,
    COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) AS order_qty,
    COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric) AS billed_qty,
    COALESCE(il.rate, (0)::numeric) AS rate,
    COALESCE(il.taxable_amount, il.line_amount, (COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(il.rate, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS billed_value,
    COALESCE(il.line_total, (((COALESCE(il.taxable_amount, il.line_amount, (COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(il.rate, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(il.cgst_amt, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(il.sgst_amt, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(il.igst_amt, (0)::numeric))) AS line_total,
    sol.expected_delivery,
    sol.final_delivery
   FROM ((((public.invoice_lines il
     JOIN public.invoices inv ON ((inv.id = il.invoice_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON ((sol.id = il.so_line_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = COALESCE(il.so_id, sol.so_id))))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = COALESCE(inv.client_code, so.client_code))))
  WHERE ((il.so_line_id IS NOT NULL) AND (upper(COALESCE(inv.status, 'POSTED'::text)) = 'POSTED'::text));


--
-- Name: v_report_delivery_performance; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_delivery_performance AS
 WITH dispatch_base AS (
         SELECT dr.so_line_id,
            max(dr.dispatch_date) AS last_dispatch_date,
            sum(COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS dispatched_qty
           FROM public.dispatch_records dr
          GROUP BY dr.so_line_id
        )
 SELECT so.so_number,
    sol.line_no,
    COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code) AS client_name,
    sol.product_name,
    COALESCE(sol.final_delivery, sol.expected_delivery) AS delivery_date,
    db.last_dispatch_date,
    COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) AS order_qty,
    COALESCE(db.dispatched_qty, (0)::numeric) AS dispatched_qty,
        CASE
            WHEN (db.last_dispatch_date IS NULL) THEN 'PENDING'::text
            WHEN (COALESCE(sol.final_delivery, sol.expected_delivery) IS NULL) THEN 'DISPATCHED'::text
            WHEN (db.last_dispatch_date <= COALESCE(sol.final_delivery, sol.expected_delivery)) THEN 'ON_TIME'::text
            ELSE 'DELAYED'::text
        END AS delivery_status,
        CASE
            WHEN ((db.last_dispatch_date IS NULL) OR (COALESCE(sol.final_delivery, sol.expected_delivery) IS NULL)) THEN 0
            ELSE GREATEST((db.last_dispatch_date - COALESCE(sol.final_delivery, sol.expected_delivery)), 0)
        END AS delay_days
   FROM (((public.sales_orders so
     JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON ((sol.so_id = so.id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
     LEFT JOIN dispatch_base db ON ((db.so_line_id = sol.id)))
  WHERE (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text);


--
-- Name: v_report_dispatch_register; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_dispatch_register AS
 WITH dispatch_line_base AS (
         SELECT dr_1.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(dr_1.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS line_dispatched_qty
           FROM public.dispatch_records dr_1
          WHERE (dr_1.so_line_id IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY dr_1.so_line_id
        ), invoice_base AS (
         SELECT il.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS billed_qty
           FROM public.invoice_lines il
          GROUP BY il.so_line_id
        )
 SELECT dr.id AS dispatch_id,
    dr.dispatch_no,
    dr.dispatch_date,
    dr.created_at,
    dr.so_id,
    dr.so_line_id,
    COALESCE(dr.so_number, so.so_number) AS so_number,
    so.so_date,
    COALESCE(dr.line_no, sol.line_no) AS line_no,
    so.client_code,
    COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code) AS client_name,
    so.sales_rep,
    COALESCE(sol.division, ''::text) AS division,
    COALESCE(sol.product_code, dr.product_code, ''::text) AS product_code,
    COALESCE(sol.product_name, dr.product_name, ''::text) AS product_name,
    COALESCE(sol.category, ''::text) AS category,
    COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) AS order_qty,
    COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric) AS dispatch_qty,
    COALESCE(dlb.line_dispatched_qty, COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS line_dispatched_qty,
    COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) AS billed_qty,
    GREATEST((COALESCE(dlb.line_dispatched_qty, COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric)) - COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS unbilled_qty,
        CASE
            WHEN ((COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(dlb.line_dispatched_qty, COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric)) >= COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric))) THEN 'FULLY_DISPATCHED'::text
            WHEN (COALESCE(dlb.line_dispatched_qty, COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric)) > (0)::numeric) THEN 'PARTIAL_DISPATCHED'::text
            ELSE 'PENDING'::text
        END AS dispatch_status,
        CASE
            WHEN ((COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) >= COALESCE(dlb.line_dispatched_qty, COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric))) AND (COALESCE(dlb.line_dispatched_qty, COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric)) > (0)::numeric)) THEN 'BILLED'::text
            WHEN (COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN 'PARTIAL_BILLED'::text
            ELSE 'UNBILLED'::text
        END AS billing_status,
    sol.expected_delivery,
    sol.final_delivery,
    dr.transporter,
    dr.vehicle_no,
    dr.lr_no
   FROM (((((public.dispatch_records dr
     LEFT JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON ((sol.id = dr.so_line_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = COALESCE(dr.so_id, sol.so_id))))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
     LEFT JOIN dispatch_line_base dlb ON ((dlb.so_line_id = dr.so_line_id)))
     LEFT JOIN invoice_base ib ON ((ib.so_line_id = dr.so_line_id)))
  WHERE ((COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text));


--
-- Name: v_report_job_profitability_phase1; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_job_profitability_phase1 AS
 WITH artwork_ranked AS (
         SELECT a.so_id,
            a.line_no,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(a.artwork_no, ''::text), (((a.so_id)::text || '||'::text) || (a.line_no)::text)) AS artwork_key,
            NULLIF(a.artwork_no, ''::text) AS artwork_no,
            row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY a.so_id, a.line_no ORDER BY a.approved_at DESC NULLS LAST, a.artwork_at DESC NULLS LAST, a.id DESC) AS rn
           FROM public.artworks a
        ), artwork_base AS (
         SELECT artwork_ranked.so_id,
            artwork_ranked.line_no,
            artwork_ranked.artwork_key,
            artwork_ranked.artwork_no,
            artwork_ranked.rn
           FROM artwork_ranked
          WHERE (artwork_ranked.rn = 1)
        ), wo_artwork_fallback AS (
         SELECT woj.so_number,
            woj.line_no,
            max(NULLIF(woj.artwork_no, ''::text)) AS artwork_no
           FROM public.work_order_jobs woj
          GROUP BY woj.so_number, woj.line_no
        ), line_base AS (
         SELECT so.id AS so_id,
            sol.id AS so_line_id,
            so.so_number,
            COALESCE(so.so_date, (so.created_at)::date) AS so_date,
            so.client_code,
            COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code) AS client_name,
            so.sales_rep,
            sol.line_no,
            sol.division,
            sol.product_code,
            sol.product_name,
            sol.category,
            COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) AS order_qty,
            COALESCE(sol.unit, ''::text) AS unit,
            COALESCE(sol.rate, (0)::numeric) AS rate,
            COALESCE(sol.net_amount, sol.amount, (COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(sol.rate, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS order_value,
            COALESCE(sol.job_type, ''::text) AS job_type,
            COALESCE(sol.job_reference, ''::text) AS sales_job_reference,
            COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text) AS so_line_status,
            (((so.id)::text || '||'::text) || (sol.line_no)::text) AS fallback_artwork_key,
            COALESCE(ab.artwork_no, waf.artwork_no, ''::text) AS artwork_no
           FROM ((((public.sales_orders so
             JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON ((sol.so_id = so.id)))
             LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
             LEFT JOIN artwork_base ab ON (((ab.so_id = so.id) AND (ab.line_no = sol.line_no))))
             LEFT JOIN wo_artwork_fallback waf ON (((waf.so_number = so.so_number) AND (waf.line_no = sol.line_no))))
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text))
        ), job_rows AS (
         SELECT lb.so_id,
            lb.so_line_id,
            lb.so_number,
            lb.so_date,
            lb.client_code,
            lb.client_name,
            lb.sales_rep,
            lb.line_no,
            lb.division,
            lb.product_code,
            lb.product_name,
            lb.category,
            lb.order_qty,
            lb.unit,
            lb.rate,
            lb.order_value,
            lb.job_type,
            lb.so_line_status,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(woj.job_reference, ''::text), NULLIF(lb.sales_job_reference, ''::text), ((lb.so_number || '||'::text) || (lb.line_no)::text)) AS job_key,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(woj.job_reference, ''::text), NULLIF(lb.sales_job_reference, ''::text), ''::text) AS job_reference,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(woj.artwork_no, ''::text), NULLIF(lb.artwork_no, ''::text), ''::text) AS artwork_no,
            wo.id AS wo_id,
            wo.wo_number,
            GREATEST(COALESCE(woj.qty, (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric) AS job_qty
           FROM ((line_base lb
             JOIN public.work_order_jobs woj ON (((woj.so_number = lb.so_number) AND (woj.line_no = lb.line_no))))
             LEFT JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.id = woj.wo_id)))
        ), job_fallback AS (
         SELECT lb.so_id,
            lb.so_line_id,
            lb.so_number,
            lb.so_date,
            lb.client_code,
            lb.client_name,
            lb.sales_rep,
            lb.line_no,
            lb.division,
            lb.product_code,
            lb.product_name,
            lb.category,
            lb.order_qty,
            lb.unit,
            lb.rate,
            lb.order_value,
            lb.job_type,
            lb.so_line_status,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(lb.sales_job_reference, ''::text), ((lb.so_number || '||'::text) || (lb.line_no)::text)) AS job_key,
            NULLIF(lb.sales_job_reference, ''::text) AS job_reference,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(lb.artwork_no, ''::text), ''::text) AS artwork_no,
            NULL::uuid AS wo_id,
            ''::text AS wo_number,
            GREATEST(lb.order_qty, (0)::numeric) AS job_qty
           FROM line_base lb
          WHERE (NOT (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
                   FROM public.work_order_jobs woj
                  WHERE ((woj.so_number = lb.so_number) AND (woj.line_no = lb.line_no)))))
        ), job_union AS (
         SELECT job_rows.so_id,
            job_rows.so_line_id,
            job_rows.so_number,
            job_rows.so_date,
            job_rows.client_code,
            job_rows.client_name,
            job_rows.sales_rep,
            job_rows.line_no,
            job_rows.division,
            job_rows.product_code,
            job_rows.product_name,
            job_rows.category,
            job_rows.order_qty,
            job_rows.unit,
            job_rows.rate,
            job_rows.order_value,
            job_rows.job_type,
            job_rows.so_line_status,
            job_rows.job_key,
            job_rows.job_reference,
            job_rows.artwork_no,
            job_rows.wo_id,
            job_rows.wo_number,
            job_rows.job_qty
           FROM job_rows
        UNION ALL
         SELECT job_fallback.so_id,
            job_fallback.so_line_id,
            job_fallback.so_number,
            job_fallback.so_date,
            job_fallback.client_code,
            job_fallback.client_name,
            job_fallback.sales_rep,
            job_fallback.line_no,
            job_fallback.division,
            job_fallback.product_code,
            job_fallback.product_name,
            job_fallback.category,
            job_fallback.order_qty,
            job_fallback.unit,
            job_fallback.rate,
            job_fallback.order_value,
            job_fallback.job_type,
            job_fallback.so_line_status,
            job_fallback.job_key,
            job_fallback.job_reference,
            job_fallback.artwork_no,
            job_fallback.wo_id,
            job_fallback.wo_number,
            job_fallback.job_qty
           FROM job_fallback
        ), line_jobs AS (
         SELECT ju.so_id,
            ju.so_line_id,
            ju.so_number,
            ju.so_date,
            ju.client_code,
            ju.client_name,
            ju.sales_rep,
            ju.line_no,
            ju.division,
            ju.product_code,
            ju.product_name,
            ju.category,
            ju.order_qty,
            ju.unit,
            ju.rate,
            ju.order_value,
            ju.job_type,
            ju.so_line_status,
            ju.job_key,
            max(COALESCE(ju.job_reference, ''::text)) AS job_reference,
            max(COALESCE(ju.artwork_no, ''::text)) AS artwork_no,
            sum(COALESCE(ju.job_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS job_qty,
            count(DISTINCT ju.wo_id) FILTER (WHERE (ju.wo_id IS NOT NULL)) AS wo_count,
            string_agg(DISTINCT ju.wo_number, ', '::text ORDER BY ju.wo_number) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(ju.wo_number, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS wo_numbers
           FROM job_union ju
          GROUP BY ju.so_id, ju.so_line_id, ju.so_number, ju.so_date, ju.client_code, ju.client_name, ju.sales_rep, ju.line_no, ju.division, ju.product_code, ju.product_name, ju.category, ju.order_qty, ju.unit, ju.rate, ju.order_value, ju.job_type, ju.so_line_status, ju.job_key
        ), line_job_share AS (
         SELECT lj.so_id,
            lj.so_line_id,
            lj.so_number,
            lj.so_date,
            lj.client_code,
            lj.client_name,
            lj.sales_rep,
            lj.line_no,
            lj.division,
            lj.product_code,
            lj.product_name,
            lj.category,
            lj.order_qty,
            lj.unit,
            lj.rate,
            lj.order_value,
            lj.job_type,
            lj.so_line_status,
            lj.job_key,
            lj.job_reference,
            lj.artwork_no,
            lj.job_qty,
            lj.wo_count,
            lj.wo_numbers,
            sum(lj.job_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY lj.so_line_id) AS total_job_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN (sum(lj.job_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY lj.so_line_id) > (0)::numeric) THEN (lj.job_qty / NULLIF(sum(lj.job_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY lj.so_line_id), (0)::numeric))
                    ELSE ((1)::numeric / (NULLIF(count(*) OVER (PARTITION BY lj.so_line_id), 0))::numeric)
                END AS job_share
           FROM line_jobs lj
        ), dispatch_base AS (
         SELECT dr.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS dispatched_qty,
            max(dr.dispatch_date) AS last_dispatch_date
           FROM public.dispatch_records dr
          WHERE (dr.so_line_id IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY dr.so_line_id
        ), invoice_base AS (
         SELECT il.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS billed_qty,
            sum(COALESCE(il.taxable_amount, il.line_amount, (COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(il.rate, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric)) AS billed_value,
            min(inv.invoice_date) AS first_invoice_date,
            max(inv.invoice_date) AS last_invoice_date,
            string_agg(DISTINCT inv.invoice_no, ', '::text ORDER BY inv.invoice_no) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(inv.invoice_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS invoice_nos
           FROM (public.invoice_lines il
             JOIN public.invoices inv ON ((inv.id = il.invoice_id)))
          WHERE ((il.so_line_id IS NOT NULL) AND (upper(COALESCE(inv.status, 'POSTED'::text)) = 'POSTED'::text))
          GROUP BY il.so_line_id
        ), wo_job_base AS (
         SELECT lb.so_line_id,
            woj.wo_id,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(woj.job_reference, ''::text), NULLIF(lb.sales_job_reference, ''::text), ((lb.so_number || '||'::text) || (lb.line_no)::text)) AS job_key,
            GREATEST(COALESCE(woj.qty, (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric) AS job_qty
           FROM (line_base lb
             JOIN public.work_order_jobs woj ON (((woj.so_number = lb.so_number) AND (woj.line_no = lb.line_no))))
        ), wo_job_share AS (
         SELECT wjb.so_line_id,
            wjb.wo_id,
            wjb.job_key,
            sum(wjb.job_qty) AS job_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN (sum(sum(wjb.job_qty)) OVER (PARTITION BY wjb.wo_id) > (0)::numeric) THEN (sum(wjb.job_qty) / NULLIF(sum(sum(wjb.job_qty)) OVER (PARTITION BY wjb.wo_id), (0)::numeric))
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END AS wo_share
           FROM wo_job_base wjb
          GROUP BY wjb.so_line_id, wjb.wo_id, wjb.job_key
        ), issue_base AS (
         SELECT wo.id AS wo_id,
            sum(COALESCE(il.qty_out, (0)::numeric)) AS issued_qty,
            sum(COALESCE(il.value, (COALESCE(il.qty_out, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(il.rate, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric)) AS issued_value
           FROM (public.inv_ledger il
             JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.wo_number = il.ref_no)))
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(il.ref_type, ''::text)) = 'ISSUE'::text) AND (COALESCE(il.qty_out, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (NOT (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
                   FROM public.inv_ledger rev
                  WHERE ((rev.ref_no = (il.id)::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(rev.ref_type, ''::text)) ~~ 'REV-%'::text))))))
          GROUP BY wo.id
        ), job_rm_cost AS (
         SELECT wjs.so_line_id,
            wjs.job_key,
            sum((COALESCE(ib.issued_qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(wjs.wo_share, (0)::numeric))) AS rm_issued_qty,
            sum((COALESCE(ib.issued_value, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(wjs.wo_share, (0)::numeric))) AS rm_issued_value
           FROM (wo_job_share wjs
             LEFT JOIN issue_base ib ON ((ib.wo_id = wjs.wo_id)))
          GROUP BY wjs.so_line_id, wjs.job_key
        ), po_tooling_base AS (
         SELECT
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['ARTWORK_DIE'::text, 'DIRECT_DIE'::text])) THEN 'DIE'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['ARTWORK_PLATE'::text, 'DIRECT_PLATE'::text])) THEN 'PLATE'::text
                    ELSE ''::text
                END AS tooling_type,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(pol.source_ref, ''::text), ''::text) AS source_ref,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(pol.job_ref, ''::text), ''::text) AS job_ref,
            COALESCE(po.order_date, (pol.created_at)::date) AS event_date,
            COALESCE(pol.amount, (0)::numeric) AS basic_amount
           FROM (public.purchase_order_lines pol
             LEFT JOIN public.purchase_orders po ON ((po.po_no = pol.po_no)))
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['ARTWORK_PLATE'::text, 'ARTWORK_DIE'::text, 'DIRECT_PLATE'::text, 'DIRECT_DIE'::text])) AND (upper(COALESCE(po.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text))
        ), manual_tooling_base AS (
         SELECT upper(COALESCE(purchase_artwork_procurement.type, ''::text)) AS tooling_type,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(purchase_artwork_procurement.artwork_key, ''::text), ''::text) AS artwork_key,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(purchase_artwork_procurement.artwork_no, ''::text), ''::text) AS artwork_no,
            COALESCE(purchase_artwork_procurement.ordered_on, purchase_artwork_procurement.received_on, (purchase_artwork_procurement.created_at)::date) AS event_date,
            COALESCE(purchase_artwork_procurement.cost, (0)::numeric) AS basic_amount
           FROM public.purchase_artwork_procurement
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(purchase_artwork_procurement.status, 'ACTIVE'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text)
        ), line_tooling_direct AS (
         SELECT lb.so_line_id,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (pt.tooling_type = 'PLATE'::text) THEN pt.basic_amount
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS plate_direct_cost,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (pt.tooling_type = 'DIE'::text) THEN pt.basic_amount
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS die_direct_cost
           FROM (line_base lb
             LEFT JOIN po_tooling_base pt ON ((pt.source_ref = lb.fallback_artwork_key)))
          GROUP BY lb.so_line_id
        ), line_tooling_manual_direct AS (
         SELECT lb.so_line_id,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (mt.tooling_type = 'PLATE'::text) THEN mt.basic_amount
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS plate_manual_direct_cost,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (mt.tooling_type = 'DIE'::text) THEN mt.basic_amount
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS die_manual_direct_cost
           FROM (line_base lb
             LEFT JOIN manual_tooling_base mt ON ((mt.artwork_key = lb.fallback_artwork_key)))
          GROUP BY lb.so_line_id
        ), line_tooling_shared_po AS (
         SELECT lb.so_line_id,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (pt.tooling_type = 'PLATE'::text) THEN pt.basic_amount
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS plate_shared_po_cost,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (pt.tooling_type = 'DIE'::text) THEN pt.basic_amount
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS die_shared_po_cost
           FROM (line_base lb
             LEFT JOIN po_tooling_base pt ON (((lb.artwork_no <> ''::text) AND (pt.source_ref = lb.artwork_no) AND ((pt.job_ref = ''::text) OR (pt.job_ref ~~* (((('%'::text || lb.so_number) || '/'::text) || (lb.line_no)::text) || '%'::text))) AND ((pt.event_date >= (lb.so_date - '15 days'::interval)) AND (pt.event_date <= (lb.so_date + '120 days'::interval))))))
          GROUP BY lb.so_line_id
        ), line_tooling_shared_manual AS (
         SELECT lb.so_line_id,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (mt.tooling_type = 'PLATE'::text) THEN mt.basic_amount
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS plate_shared_manual_cost,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (mt.tooling_type = 'DIE'::text) THEN mt.basic_amount
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END) AS die_shared_manual_cost
           FROM (line_base lb
             LEFT JOIN manual_tooling_base mt ON (((lb.artwork_no <> ''::text) AND ((mt.artwork_key = lb.artwork_no) OR (mt.artwork_no = lb.artwork_no)) AND ((mt.event_date >= (lb.so_date - '15 days'::interval)) AND (mt.event_date <= (lb.so_date + '120 days'::interval))))))
          GROUP BY lb.so_line_id
        ), line_tooling AS (
         SELECT lb.so_line_id,
            (((COALESCE(ld.plate_direct_cost, (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(md.plate_manual_direct_cost, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(sp.plate_shared_po_cost, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(sm.plate_shared_manual_cost, (0)::numeric)) AS plate_cost,
            (((COALESCE(ld.die_direct_cost, (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(md.die_manual_direct_cost, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(sp.die_shared_po_cost, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(sm.die_shared_manual_cost, (0)::numeric)) AS die_cost
           FROM ((((line_base lb
             LEFT JOIN line_tooling_direct ld ON ((ld.so_line_id = lb.so_line_id)))
             LEFT JOIN line_tooling_manual_direct md ON ((md.so_line_id = lb.so_line_id)))
             LEFT JOIN line_tooling_shared_po sp ON ((sp.so_line_id = lb.so_line_id)))
             LEFT JOIN line_tooling_shared_manual sm ON ((sm.so_line_id = lb.so_line_id)))
        ), final_base AS (
         SELECT ljs.so_id,
            ljs.so_line_id,
            ljs.so_number,
            ljs.so_date,
            ljs.client_code,
            ljs.client_name,
            ljs.sales_rep,
            ljs.line_no,
            ljs.division,
            ljs.product_code,
            ljs.product_name,
            ljs.category,
            ljs.unit,
            ljs.rate,
            ljs.job_type,
            ljs.so_line_status,
            ljs.job_key,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(ljs.job_reference, ''::text), ljs.job_key) AS job_reference,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(ljs.artwork_no, ''::text), ''::text) AS artwork_no,
            ljs.wo_count,
            COALESCE(ljs.wo_numbers, ''::text) AS wo_numbers,
            round((COALESCE(ljs.order_qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(ljs.job_share, (0)::numeric)), 3) AS order_qty,
            round((COALESCE(db.dispatched_qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(ljs.job_share, (0)::numeric)), 3) AS dispatched_qty,
            round((COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(ljs.job_share, (0)::numeric)), 3) AS billed_qty,
            round((COALESCE(ljs.order_value, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(ljs.job_share, (0)::numeric)), 2) AS order_value,
            round((COALESCE(ib.billed_value, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(ljs.job_share, (0)::numeric)), 2) AS billed_value,
            round(COALESCE(jrm.rm_issued_qty, (0)::numeric), 3) AS rm_issued_qty,
            round(COALESCE(jrm.rm_issued_value, (0)::numeric), 2) AS rm_issued_value,
            round((COALESCE(lt.plate_cost, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(ljs.job_share, (0)::numeric)), 2) AS plate_cost,
            round((COALESCE(lt.die_cost, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(ljs.job_share, (0)::numeric)), 2) AS die_cost,
            ib.invoice_nos,
            ib.first_invoice_date,
            ib.last_invoice_date,
            db.last_dispatch_date
           FROM ((((line_job_share ljs
             LEFT JOIN dispatch_base db ON ((db.so_line_id = ljs.so_line_id)))
             LEFT JOIN invoice_base ib ON ((ib.so_line_id = ljs.so_line_id)))
             LEFT JOIN job_rm_cost jrm ON (((jrm.so_line_id = ljs.so_line_id) AND (jrm.job_key = ljs.job_key))))
             LEFT JOIN line_tooling lt ON ((lt.so_line_id = ljs.so_line_id)))
        )
 SELECT so_id,
    so_line_id,
    so_number,
    so_date,
    client_code,
    client_name,
    sales_rep,
    line_no,
    division,
    product_code,
    product_name,
    category,
    unit,
    rate,
    job_type,
    so_line_status,
        CASE
            WHEN ((COALESCE(billed_qty, (0)::numeric) >= COALESCE(order_qty, (0)::numeric)) AND (COALESCE(order_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric)) THEN 'CLOSED'::text
            ELSE 'PENDING'::text
        END AS billing_status,
    job_key,
    job_reference,
    artwork_no,
    wo_count,
    wo_numbers,
    order_qty,
    dispatched_qty,
    round(
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(order_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN ((COALESCE(dispatched_qty, (0)::numeric) / NULLIF(order_qty, (0)::numeric)) * 100.0)
            ELSE (0)::numeric
        END, 2) AS dispatched_pct,
    billed_qty,
    round(
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(order_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN ((COALESCE(billed_qty, (0)::numeric) / NULLIF(order_qty, (0)::numeric)) * 100.0)
            ELSE (0)::numeric
        END, 2) AS billed_pct,
    order_value,
    billed_value,
    rm_issued_qty,
    rm_issued_value,
    plate_cost,
    die_cost,
    round((COALESCE(plate_cost, (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(die_cost, (0)::numeric)), 2) AS tooling_cost,
    round(((COALESCE(rm_issued_value, (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(plate_cost, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(die_cost, (0)::numeric)), 2) AS actual_cost_till_date,
    round((COALESCE(order_value, (0)::numeric) - ((COALESCE(rm_issued_value, (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(plate_cost, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(die_cost, (0)::numeric))), 2) AS profit_on_order_value,
    round(
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(order_value, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN (((COALESCE(order_value, (0)::numeric) - ((COALESCE(rm_issued_value, (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(plate_cost, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(die_cost, (0)::numeric))) / NULLIF(order_value, (0)::numeric)) * 100.0)
            ELSE (0)::numeric
        END, 2) AS profit_pct_on_order_value,
    round((COALESCE(billed_value, (0)::numeric) - ((COALESCE(rm_issued_value, (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(plate_cost, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(die_cost, (0)::numeric))), 2) AS profit_on_billed_value,
    round(
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(billed_value, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN (((COALESCE(billed_value, (0)::numeric) - ((COALESCE(rm_issued_value, (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(plate_cost, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(die_cost, (0)::numeric))) / NULLIF(billed_value, (0)::numeric)) * 100.0)
            ELSE (0)::numeric
        END, 2) AS profit_pct_on_billed_value,
    COALESCE(invoice_nos, ''::text) AS invoice_nos,
    first_invoice_date,
    last_invoice_date,
    last_dispatch_date
   FROM final_base fb;


--
-- Name: v_report_machine_load_lines; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_machine_load_lines AS
 WITH machine_standards(machine_key, machine, standard_make_ready_minutes, target_output) AS (
         VALUES ('SM74'::text,'Heidelberg SM74'::text,(30)::numeric,(4125)::numeric), ('CD102'::text,'CD102'::text,(30)::numeric,(4125)::numeric), ('LAMINATION'::text,'Lamination'::text,(10)::numeric,(2200)::numeric), ('LAM1'::text,'Lamination Roll-1'::text,(10)::numeric,(1100)::numeric), ('LAM2'::text,'Lamination 02'::text,(10)::numeric,(1100)::numeric), ('LAM3'::text,'Lamination 03'::text,(10)::numeric,(1100)::numeric), ('DIEOFFSET'::text,'Die Cutting - Offset'::text,(30)::numeric,(2400)::numeric), ('DICORR'::text,'Die Cutting - Corrugation'::text,(30)::numeric,(1600)::numeric), ('DIE1'::text,'Die Cutting 01'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE2'::text,'Die Cutting 02'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE3'::text,'Die Cutting 03'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE4'::text,'Die Cutting 04'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE5'::text,'Die Cutting 05'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('FLEXOPRINT'::text,'Markany Flexo E5'::text,(30)::numeric,(55)::numeric), ('FLEXODIE'::text,'Flexo Die Cutting Offline'::text,(30)::numeric,(44)::numeric), ('FLEXOSLIT'::text,'Rhyguan'::text,(30)::numeric,(88)::numeric), ('DIGITAL'::text,'Canon Digital Printing'::text,(10)::numeric,(660)::numeric), ('2PLY'::text,'2 Ply Making Machine'::text,(15)::numeric,(2000)::numeric), ('ROTARY'::text,'Rotary Cutting'::text,(0)::numeric,(650)::numeric), ('MSP1'::text,'Manual Sheet Pasting'::text,(0)::numeric,(750)::numeric), ('MSP2'::text,'Manual Pasting'::text,(0)::numeric,(600)::numeric), ('FMZ'::text,'Flute Laminator'::text,(0)::numeric,(2000)::numeric), ('DCMAN1'::text,'Manual Die Cutting 04'::text,(45)::numeric,(800)::numeric), ('DCMAN2'::text,'Manual Die Cutting 05'::text,(45)::numeric,(800)::numeric), ('DCAUTO'::text,'Automatic Die Cutting'::text,(90)::numeric,(2500)::numeric), ('STMAN1'::text,'Stitching Manual 01'::text,(0)::numeric,(250)::numeric), ('STMAN2'::text,'Stitching Manual 02'::text,(0)::numeric,(250)::numeric), ('STAUTO'::text,'Auto Stitching'::text,(0)::numeric,(200)::numeric), ('FOLDER'::text,'Folder Gluer'::text,(45)::numeric,(15000)::numeric), ('RS4'::text,'RS4 Printing'::text,(60)::numeric,(1500)::numeric)
        ), job_status_base AS (
         SELECT woj.wo_id,
            woj.so_number,
            woj.line_no,
            woj.artwork_no,
            woj.client_name,
            woj.product_name,
            woj.expected_delivery,
            COALESCE(woj.qty, (0)::numeric) AS job_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['CLOSED'::text, 'CANCELLED'::text])) AND (upper(COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['CLOSED'::text, 'CANCELLED'::text]))) THEN true
                    ELSE false
                END AS is_open_job
           FROM ((public.work_order_jobs woj
             LEFT JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.so_number = woj.so_number)))
             LEFT JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON (((sol.so_id = so.id) AND ((sol.line_no)::text = (woj.line_no)::text))))
        ), job_scope AS (
         SELECT job_status_base.wo_id,
            count(*) AS total_job_count,
            count(*) FILTER (WHERE job_status_base.is_open_job) AS open_job_count,
            COALESCE(sum(job_status_base.job_qty), (0)::numeric) AS total_job_qty,
            COALESCE(sum(job_status_base.job_qty) FILTER (WHERE job_status_base.is_open_job), (0)::numeric) AS open_job_qty,
            string_agg(DISTINCT job_status_base.so_number, ', '::text ORDER BY job_status_base.so_number) FILTER (WHERE (job_status_base.is_open_job AND (COALESCE(job_status_base.so_number, ''::text) <> ''::text))) AS open_so_numbers,
            string_agg(DISTINCT (job_status_base.line_no)::text, ', '::text ORDER BY (job_status_base.line_no)::text) FILTER (WHERE (job_status_base.is_open_job AND (job_status_base.line_no IS NOT NULL))) AS open_line_nos,
            string_agg(DISTINCT job_status_base.artwork_no, ', '::text ORDER BY job_status_base.artwork_no) FILTER (WHERE (job_status_base.is_open_job AND (COALESCE(job_status_base.artwork_no, ''::text) <> ''::text))) AS open_artwork_nos,
            max(NULLIF(job_status_base.client_name, ''::text)) FILTER (WHERE job_status_base.is_open_job) AS open_client_name,
            string_agg(DISTINCT job_status_base.product_name, ' / '::text ORDER BY job_status_base.product_name) FILTER (WHERE (job_status_base.is_open_job AND (COALESCE(job_status_base.product_name, ''::text) <> ''::text))) AS open_product_names,
            max(job_status_base.expected_delivery) FILTER (WHERE job_status_base.is_open_job) AS open_expected_delivery
           FROM job_status_base
          GROUP BY job_status_base.wo_id
        ), queue_base AS (
         SELECT q.routing_id,
            q.wo_id,
            q.wo_number,
            q.wo_date,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(js.open_so_numbers, ''::text), q.so_numbers) AS so_numbers,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(js.open_line_nos, ''::text), q.line_nos) AS line_nos,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(js.open_artwork_nos, ''::text), q.artwork_nos) AS artwork_nos,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(js.open_client_name, ''::text), q.client_name) AS client_name,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(js.open_product_names, ''::text), q.product_names) AS product_names,
            q.department_category,
            q.process_name,
            q.department,
            q.planned_machine,
            q.sequence_no,
            COALESCE(js.open_expected_delivery, q.expected_delivery) AS expected_delivery,
            q.job_priority,
            round((COALESCE(q.planned_qty, (0)::numeric) *
                CASE
                    WHEN ((COALESCE(js.total_job_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(js.open_job_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric)) THEN (js.open_job_qty / NULLIF(js.total_job_qty, (0)::numeric))
                    ELSE (1)::numeric
                END), 2) AS planned_qty,
            round((COALESCE(q.produced_qty, (0)::numeric) *
                CASE
                    WHEN ((COALESCE(js.total_job_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(js.open_job_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric)) THEN (js.open_job_qty / NULLIF(js.total_job_qty, (0)::numeric))
                    ELSE (1)::numeric
                END), 2) AS produced_qty,
            round((COALESCE(q.balance_qty, (0)::numeric) *
                CASE
                    WHEN ((COALESCE(js.total_job_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(js.open_job_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric)) THEN (js.open_job_qty / NULLIF(js.total_job_qty, (0)::numeric))
                    ELSE (1)::numeric
                END), 2) AS balance_qty,
            q.status,
            COALESCE(su.plan_unit, ''::text) AS plan_unit,
            COALESCE(fm.flexo_total_rm, (0)::numeric) AS flexo_total_rm,
            COALESCE(fm.flexo_total_job_qty, (0)::numeric) AS flexo_total_job_qty,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(q.planned_machine, ''::text), NULLIF(q.process_name, ''::text), NULLIF(q.department, ''::text), 'Unassigned'::text) AS raw_machine,
            upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(q.planned_machine, ''::text), NULLIF(q.process_name, ''::text), NULLIF(q.department, ''::text), 'UNASSIGNED'::text)) AS raw_machine_upper,
            upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(q.process_name, ''::text), NULLIF(q.department, ''::text), ''::text)) AS raw_process_upper,
            upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(q.department_category, ''::text), ''::text)) AS raw_category_upper
           FROM (((public.v_production_stage_queue_fast q
             LEFT JOIN ( SELECT v_production_stage_rows_fast.routing_id,
                    string_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(v_production_stage_rows_fast.plan_unit, ''::text), ', '::text ORDER BY NULLIF(v_production_stage_rows_fast.plan_unit, ''::text)) AS plan_unit
                   FROM public.v_production_stage_rows_fast
                  GROUP BY v_production_stage_rows_fast.routing_id) su ON ((su.routing_id = q.routing_id)))
             LEFT JOIN ( SELECT wf.wo_id,
                    wf.flexo_total_rm,
                    COALESCE(jt.flexo_total_job_qty, (0)::numeric) AS flexo_total_job_qty
                   FROM (( SELECT wo.id AS wo_id,
                            COALESCE(
                                CASE
                                    WHEN ((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{flexoDetails,totalRunningMeter}'::text[]) ~ '^[0-9]+([.][0-9]+)?$'::text) THEN ((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{flexoDetails,totalRunningMeter}'::text[]))::numeric
                                    ELSE NULL::numeric
                                END,
                                CASE
                                    WHEN ((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{flexoDetails,baseRunningMeter}'::text[]) ~ '^[0-9]+([.][0-9]+)?$'::text) THEN ((wo.snapshot_json #>> '{flexoDetails,baseRunningMeter}'::text[]))::numeric
                                    ELSE NULL::numeric
                                END, (0)::numeric) AS flexo_total_rm
                           FROM public.work_orders wo) wf
                     LEFT JOIN ( SELECT work_order_jobs.wo_id,
                            COALESCE(sum(work_order_jobs.qty), (0)::numeric) AS flexo_total_job_qty
                           FROM public.work_order_jobs
                          GROUP BY work_order_jobs.wo_id) jt ON ((jt.wo_id = wf.wo_id)))) fm ON ((fm.wo_id = q.wo_id)))
             LEFT JOIN job_scope js ON ((js.wo_id = q.wo_id)))
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(q.status, 'PENDING'::text)) <> 'COMPLETED'::text) AND (COALESCE(q.balance_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND ((js.wo_id IS NULL) OR (COALESCE(js.open_job_count, (0)::bigint) > 0)))
        ), queue_normalized AS (
         SELECT qb.routing_id,
            qb.wo_id,
            qb.wo_number,
            qb.wo_date,
            qb.so_numbers,
            qb.line_nos,
            qb.artwork_nos,
            qb.client_name,
            qb.product_names,
            qb.department_category,
            qb.process_name,
            qb.department,
            qb.planned_machine,
            qb.sequence_no,
            qb.expected_delivery,
            qb.job_priority,
            qb.planned_qty,
            qb.produced_qty,
            qb.balance_qty,
            qb.status,
            qb.plan_unit,
            qb.flexo_total_rm,
            qb.flexo_total_job_qty,
            qb.raw_machine,
            qb.raw_machine_upper,
            qb.raw_process_upper,
            qb.raw_category_upper,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%SM74%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%SM-74%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%HEIDELBERG%'::text)) THEN 'SM74'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CD102%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CD-102%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CD 102%'::text)) THEN 'CD102'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 01%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 1%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION ROLL-1%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION ROLL 1%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAM-1%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper = 'LAM1'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 02%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 2%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAM-2%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper = 'LAM2'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper = 'LAMINATION'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper = 'LAMINATION'::text)) THEN 'LAMINATION'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 03%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 3%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAM-3%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper = 'LAM3'::text)) THEN 'LAM3'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%RHYGUAN%'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%SLIT%'::text)) THEN 'FLEXOSLIT'::text
                    WHEN (((qb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) AND ((qb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%DIE%'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%CUT%'::text))) OR ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) AND ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CUT%'::text)))) THEN 'FLEXODIE'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MARKANY%'::text)) THEN 'FLEXOPRINT'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CANON%'::text)) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                    WHEN (((qb.raw_machine_upper = 'PRINTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper = 'OFFSET PRINTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper = 'PRINTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper = 'OFFSET PRINTING'::text)) AND (qb.raw_category_upper ~~ '%CORRUGATION%'::text)) THEN 'CD102'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper = 'PRINTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper = 'OFFSET PRINTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper = 'PRINTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper = 'OFFSET PRINTING'::text)) THEN 'SM74'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper = 'AUTOMATIC'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper = 'AUTO'::text)) THEN '2PLY'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%2 PLY%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%TWO PLY%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%PLY MAKING%'::text)) THEN '2PLY'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%ROTARY%'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%ROTARY%'::text)) THEN 'ROTARY'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FMZ%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FLUTE%'::text)) THEN 'FMZ'::text
                    WHEN (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL SHEET PASTING%'::text) THEN 'MSP1'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper = 'PASTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper = 'SIDE PASTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper = 'PASTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper = 'SIDE PASTING'::text)) THEN 'FOLDER'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL PASTING%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL SHEET PASTING 2%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL SHEET PASTING - 2%'::text)) THEN 'MSP2'::text
                    WHEN (((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE CUTTING 05%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL 2%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL - 2%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE CUTTING 04%'::text)) AND (qb.raw_category_upper ~~ '%CORRUGATION%'::text)) THEN 'DICORR'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE CUTTING 05%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL 2%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL - 2%'::text)) THEN 'DCMAN2'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE CUTTING 04%'::text)) THEN 'DCMAN1'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) AND (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTOMATIC DIE%'::text)) THEN 'FLEXODIE'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTOMATIC DIE%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTO DIE%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING AUTO%'::text)) THEN 'DCAUTO'::text
                    WHEN (((qb.raw_machine_upper = 'DIE CUTTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper = 'DIE CUTTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 01%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-01%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-01%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-1%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-1%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 02%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-02%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-02%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-2%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-2%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 03%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-03%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-03%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-3%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-3%'::text)) AND (qb.raw_category_upper ~~ '%CORRUGATION%'::text)) THEN 'DICORR'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper = 'DIE CUTTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_process_upper = 'DIE CUTTING'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 01%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-01%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-01%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-1%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-1%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 02%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-02%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-02%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-2%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-2%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 03%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-03%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-03%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-3%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-3%'::text)) THEN 'DIEOFFSET'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 04%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-4%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-4%'::text)) THEN 'DIE4'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 05%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-5%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-5%'::text)) THEN 'DIE5'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL 02%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL - 2%'::text)) THEN 'STMAN2'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL 01%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL - 1%'::text)) THEN 'STMAN1'::text
                    WHEN ((qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTO STITCH%'::text) OR (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING AUTO%'::text)) THEN 'STAUTO'::text
                    WHEN (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FOLDER GLUER%'::text) THEN 'FOLDER'::text
                    WHEN (qb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%RS4%'::text) THEN 'RS4'::text
                    ELSE regexp_replace(qb.raw_machine_upper, '[^A-Z0-9]+'::text, ''::text, 'g'::text)
                END AS machine_key
           FROM queue_base qb
        ), load_base AS (
         SELECT qn.routing_id,
            qn.wo_id,
            qn.wo_number,
            qn.wo_date,
            qn.so_numbers,
            qn.line_nos,
            qn.artwork_nos,
            qn.client_name,
            qn.product_names,
            qn.department_category,
            qn.process_name,
            qn.department,
            qn.planned_machine,
            qn.sequence_no,
            qn.expected_delivery,
            qn.job_priority,
            qn.planned_qty,
            qn.produced_qty,
            qn.balance_qty,
            qn.status,
            qn.plan_unit,
            qn.flexo_total_rm,
            qn.flexo_total_job_qty,
            qn.raw_machine,
            qn.raw_machine_upper,
            qn.raw_process_upper,
            qn.raw_category_upper,
            qn.machine_key,
            COALESCE(ms.machine, NULLIF(qn.planned_machine, ''::text), qn.raw_machine) AS normalized_machine,
            COALESCE(ms.standard_make_ready_minutes, (0)::numeric) AS standard_make_ready_minutes,
            COALESCE(ms.target_output, (0)::numeric) AS target_output,
                CASE
                    WHEN (qn.machine_key = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXOPRINT'::text, 'FLEXODIE'::text, 'FLEXOSLIT'::text])) THEN 'meter/min'::text
                    ELSE 'qty/hour'::text
                END AS target_unit,
                CASE
                    WHEN (qn.machine_key = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXOPRINT'::text, 'FLEXODIE'::text, 'FLEXOSLIT'::text])) THEN (COALESCE(ms.target_output, (0)::numeric) * (60)::numeric)
                    ELSE COALESCE(ms.target_output, (0)::numeric)
                END AS target_hourly_capacity,
                CASE
                    WHEN (COALESCE(qn.balance_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN COALESCE(qn.balance_qty, (0)::numeric)
                    ELSE COALESCE(qn.planned_qty, (0)::numeric)
                END AS base_load_qty
           FROM (queue_normalized qn
             LEFT JOIN machine_standards ms ON ((ms.machine_key = qn.machine_key)))
        ), load_unit_calc AS (
         SELECT lb.routing_id,
            lb.wo_id,
            lb.wo_number,
            lb.wo_date,
            lb.so_numbers,
            lb.line_nos,
            lb.artwork_nos,
            lb.client_name,
            lb.product_names,
            lb.department_category,
            lb.process_name,
            lb.department,
            lb.planned_machine,
            lb.sequence_no,
            lb.expected_delivery,
            lb.job_priority,
            lb.planned_qty,
            lb.produced_qty,
            lb.balance_qty,
            lb.status,
            lb.plan_unit,
            lb.flexo_total_rm,
            lb.flexo_total_job_qty,
            lb.raw_machine,
            lb.raw_machine_upper,
            lb.raw_process_upper,
            lb.raw_category_upper,
            lb.machine_key,
            lb.normalized_machine,
            lb.standard_make_ready_minutes,
            lb.target_output,
            lb.target_unit,
            lb.target_hourly_capacity,
            lb.base_load_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN (lb.machine_key = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXOPRINT'::text, 'FLEXODIE'::text, 'FLEXOSLIT'::text])) THEN 'meter'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(lb.plan_unit, ''::text)) <> ''::text) THEN lower(lb.plan_unit)
                    ELSE 'qty'::text
                END AS load_qty_unit,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((lb.machine_key = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXOPRINT'::text, 'FLEXODIE'::text, 'FLEXOSLIT'::text])) AND (COALESCE(lb.flexo_total_rm, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(lb.flexo_total_job_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND ((upper(COALESCE(lb.plan_unit, ''::text)) ~~ '%PCS%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(lb.plan_unit, ''::text)) ~~ '%UNIT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(lb.plan_unit, ''::text)) ~~ '%SHEET%'::text) OR (COALESCE(lb.base_load_qty, (0)::numeric) > COALESCE(lb.flexo_total_rm, (0)::numeric)) OR (lb.machine_key = 'FLEXODIE'::text))) THEN round(((COALESCE(lb.base_load_qty, (0)::numeric) * lb.flexo_total_rm) / NULLIF(lb.flexo_total_job_qty, (0)::numeric)), 2)
                    ELSE COALESCE(lb.base_load_qty, (0)::numeric)
                END AS load_qty
           FROM load_base lb
        ), load_calc AS (
         SELECT luc.routing_id,
            luc.wo_id,
            luc.wo_number,
            luc.wo_date,
            luc.so_numbers,
            luc.line_nos,
            luc.artwork_nos,
            luc.client_name,
            luc.product_names,
            luc.department_category,
            luc.process_name,
            luc.department,
            luc.planned_machine,
            luc.sequence_no,
            luc.expected_delivery,
            luc.job_priority,
            luc.planned_qty,
            luc.produced_qty,
            luc.balance_qty,
            luc.status,
            luc.plan_unit,
            luc.flexo_total_rm,
            luc.flexo_total_job_qty,
            luc.raw_machine,
            luc.raw_machine_upper,
            luc.raw_process_upper,
            luc.raw_category_upper,
            luc.machine_key,
            luc.normalized_machine,
            luc.standard_make_ready_minutes,
            luc.target_output,
            luc.target_unit,
            luc.target_hourly_capacity,
            luc.base_load_qty,
            luc.load_qty_unit,
            luc.load_qty,
            round((COALESCE(luc.standard_make_ready_minutes, (0)::numeric) / 60.0), 2) AS make_ready_hours,
            round((COALESCE(luc.load_qty, (0)::numeric) / NULLIF(luc.target_hourly_capacity, (0)::numeric)), 2) AS running_load_hours
           FROM load_unit_calc luc
        )
 SELECT routing_id,
    wo_id,
    wo_number,
    wo_date,
    so_numbers,
    line_nos,
    artwork_nos,
    client_name,
    product_names,
    department_category,
    process_name,
    department,
    planned_machine,
    machine_key,
    normalized_machine AS machine,
    expected_delivery,
    planned_qty,
    produced_qty,
    balance_qty,
    plan_unit,
    base_load_qty,
    load_qty,
    load_qty_unit,
    flexo_total_rm,
    flexo_total_job_qty,
    status,
    standard_make_ready_minutes,
    target_output,
    target_unit,
    round(target_hourly_capacity, 2) AS target_hourly_capacity,
    make_ready_hours,
    COALESCE(running_load_hours, (0)::numeric) AS running_load_hours,
    round((COALESCE(running_load_hours, (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(make_ready_hours, (0)::numeric)), 2) AS total_load_hours,
    round(((COALESCE(running_load_hours, (0)::numeric) + COALESCE(make_ready_hours, (0)::numeric)) / 8.0), 2) AS load_days,
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(target_hourly_capacity, (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN 'NO_TARGET'::text
            ELSE 'OPEN_LOAD'::text
        END AS load_status
   FROM load_calc;


--
-- Name: v_report_machine_load_missing_targets; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_machine_load_missing_targets AS
 SELECT routing_id,
    wo_id,
    wo_number,
    wo_date,
    so_numbers,
    line_nos,
    artwork_nos,
    client_name,
    product_names,
    department_category,
    department,
    process_name,
    planned_machine AS current_planned_machine,
    machine_key AS normalized_machine_key,
    machine AS report_machine_label,
    planned_qty,
    produced_qty,
    balance_qty,
    load_qty,
    status,
    load_status,
    expected_delivery,
        CASE
