            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(planned_machine, process_name, department, ''::text)) ~~ '%PRINT%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(department, process_name, planned_machine, ''::text)) ~~ '%PRINT%'::text)) THEN 'Heidelberg SM74 | CD102 | RS4 Printing | Canon Digital Printing | Markany Flexo E5'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(planned_machine, process_name, department, ''::text)) ~~ '%LAMINATION%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(department, process_name, planned_machine, ''::text)) ~~ '%LAMINATION%'::text)) THEN 'Lamination Roll-1 | Lamination 02 | Lamination 03'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(planned_machine, process_name, department, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(department, process_name, planned_machine, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIE%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(planned_machine, process_name, department, ''::text)) ~~ '%AUTOMATIC%'::text)) THEN 'Die Cutting 01 | Die Cutting 02 | Die Cutting 03 | Automatic Die Cutting | Manual Die Cutting 04 | Manual Die Cutting 05'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(planned_machine, process_name, department, ''::text)) ~~ '%PAST%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(department, process_name, planned_machine, ''::text)) ~~ '%PAST%'::text)) THEN 'Manual Sheet Pasting | Manual Pasting | Flute Laminator | Folder Gluer'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(planned_machine, process_name, department, ''::text)) ~~ '%STITCH%'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(department, process_name, planned_machine, ''::text)) ~~ '%STITCH%'::text)) THEN 'Stitching Manual 01 | Stitching Manual 02 | Auto Stitching'::text
            ELSE 'Review routing department/process and assign a machine that has a Machine Load target'::text
        END AS suggested_target_machines,
    'work_order_routing'::text AS update_table,
    'planned_machine'::text AS update_column,
    'Update work_order_routing.planned_machine where id = routing_id'::text AS update_instruction
   FROM public.v_report_machine_load_lines
  WHERE (load_status = 'NO_TARGET'::text);


--
-- Name: v_report_machine_load_machine_cleanup; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_machine_load_machine_cleanup AS
 SELECT v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.routing_id,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.wo_id,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.wo_number,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.wo_date,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.so_numbers,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.line_nos,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.artwork_nos,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.client_name,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.product_names,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.department_category,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.department,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.process_name,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.current_planned_machine,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.normalized_machine_key,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.report_machine_label,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.planned_qty,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.produced_qty,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.balance_qty,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.load_qty,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.status,
    v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.expected_delivery,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.current_planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.report_machine_label, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['AUTOMATIC'::text, 'AUTO'::text])) THEN '2 Ply Making Machine'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.current_planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.report_machine_label, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['PASTING'::text, 'SIDE PASTING'::text])) THEN 'Folder Gluer'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.current_planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.process_name, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.department, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['PRINTING'::text, 'OFFSET PRINTING'::text])) AND (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.department_category, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORRUGATION%'::text)) THEN 'CD102'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.current_planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.process_name, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.department, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['PRINTING'::text, 'OFFSET PRINTING'::text])) THEN 'Heidelberg SM74'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.current_planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.process_name, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.department, ''::text)) ~~ '%ROTARY%'::text) THEN 'Rotary Cutting'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.current_planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.process_name, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.department, ''::text)) = 'LAMINATION'::text) THEN 'Review: report bucket is Lamination; keep/select actual lamination machine if scheduling requires it'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.current_planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.process_name, v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.department, ''::text)) = 'DIE CUTTING'::text) THEN 'Review: report separates Die Cutting by article department; keep generic only if this is intentional'::text
            WHEN (v_report_machine_load_missing_targets.load_status = 'NO_TARGET'::text) THEN 'Review and select a valid planned_machine with target'::text
            ELSE ''::text
        END AS suggested_planned_machine_update,
    'work_order_routing'::text AS update_table,
    'planned_machine'::text AS update_column,
    'Update work_order_routing.planned_machine where id = routing_id after review'::text AS update_instruction
   FROM public.v_report_machine_load_missing_targets
UNION
 SELECT v_report_machine_load_lines.routing_id,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.wo_id,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.wo_number,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.wo_date,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.so_numbers,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.line_nos,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.artwork_nos,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.client_name,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.product_names,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.department_category,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.department,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.process_name,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.planned_machine AS current_planned_machine,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.machine_key AS normalized_machine_key,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.machine AS report_machine_label,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.planned_qty,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.produced_qty,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.balance_qty,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.load_qty,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.status,
    v_report_machine_load_lines.expected_delivery,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_lines.planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_lines.process_name, v_report_machine_load_lines.department, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['AUTOMATIC'::text, 'AUTO'::text])) THEN '2 Ply Making Machine'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_lines.planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_lines.process_name, v_report_machine_load_lines.department, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['PASTING'::text, 'SIDE PASTING'::text])) THEN 'Folder Gluer'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_lines.planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_lines.process_name, v_report_machine_load_lines.department, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['PRINTING'::text, 'OFFSET PRINTING'::text])) AND (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_lines.department_category, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORRUGATION%'::text)) THEN 'CD102'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_lines.planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_lines.process_name, v_report_machine_load_lines.department, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['PRINTING'::text, 'OFFSET PRINTING'::text])) THEN 'Heidelberg SM74'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_lines.planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_lines.process_name, v_report_machine_load_lines.department, ''::text)) ~~ '%ROTARY%'::text) THEN 'Rotary Cutting'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_lines.planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_lines.process_name, v_report_machine_load_lines.department, ''::text)) = 'LAMINATION'::text) THEN 'Review: report bucket is Lamination; keep/select actual lamination machine if scheduling requires it'::text
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_lines.planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_lines.process_name, v_report_machine_load_lines.department, ''::text)) = 'DIE CUTTING'::text) THEN 'Review: report separates Die Cutting by article department; keep generic only if this is intentional'::text
            ELSE ''::text
        END AS suggested_planned_machine_update,
    'work_order_routing'::text AS update_table,
    'planned_machine'::text AS update_column,
    'Update work_order_routing.planned_machine where id = routing_id after review'::text AS update_instruction
   FROM public.v_report_machine_load_lines
  WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_lines.planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_lines.process_name, v_report_machine_load_lines.department, ''::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['AUTOMATIC'::text, 'AUTO'::text, 'PASTING'::text, 'SIDE PASTING'::text, 'PRINTING'::text, 'OFFSET PRINTING'::text, 'LAMINATION'::text, 'DIE CUTTING'::text])) OR (upper(COALESCE(v_report_machine_load_lines.planned_machine, v_report_machine_load_lines.process_name, v_report_machine_load_lines.department, ''::text)) ~~ '%ROTARY%'::text));


--
-- Name: v_report_order_line_traceability; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_order_line_traceability AS
 WITH artwork_base AS (
         SELECT a.so_id,
            a.line_no,
            max(a.artwork_no) AS artwork_no,
            max(a.status) AS artwork_status,
            max(a.plate_status) AS plate_status,
            max(a.die_status) AS die_status
           FROM public.artworks a
          GROUP BY a.so_id, a.line_no
        ), wo_base AS (
         SELECT woj.so_number,
            woj.line_no,
            count(DISTINCT woj.wo_id) AS wo_count,
            string_agg(DISTINCT wo.wo_number, ', '::text ORDER BY wo.wo_number) AS wo_numbers
           FROM (public.work_order_jobs woj
             JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.id = woj.wo_id)))
          GROUP BY woj.so_number, woj.line_no
        ), pack_base AS (
         SELECT pr.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(pr.packed_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS packed_qty
           FROM public.packing_records pr
          GROUP BY pr.so_line_id
        ), dispatch_base AS (
         SELECT dr.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS dispatched_qty,
            max(dr.dispatch_date) AS last_dispatch_date
           FROM public.dispatch_records dr
          GROUP BY dr.so_line_id
        ), invoice_base AS (
         SELECT il.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS billed_qty
           FROM public.invoice_lines il
          GROUP BY il.so_line_id
        )
 SELECT so.id AS so_id,
    so.so_number,
    so.so_date,
    so.client_code,
    COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code) AS client_name,
    so.sales_rep,
    so.status AS so_status,
    sol.id AS so_line_id,
    sol.line_no,
    sol.product_code,
    sol.product_name,
    COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) AS order_qty,
    sol.expected_delivery,
    sol.final_delivery,
    sol.accounts_status,
    sol.business_status,
    ab.artwork_no,
    ab.artwork_status,
    ab.plate_status,
    ab.die_status,
    COALESCE(wb.wo_count, (0)::bigint) AS wo_count,
    COALESCE(wb.wo_numbers, ''::text) AS wo_numbers,
    COALESCE(pb.packed_qty, (0)::numeric) AS packed_qty,
    COALESCE(db.dispatched_qty, (0)::numeric) AS dispatched_qty,
    db.last_dispatch_date,
    COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) AS billed_qty
   FROM (((((((public.sales_orders so
     JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON ((sol.so_id = so.id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
     LEFT JOIN artwork_base ab ON (((ab.so_id = so.id) AND (ab.line_no = sol.line_no))))
     LEFT JOIN wo_base wb ON (((wb.so_number = so.so_number) AND (wb.line_no = sol.line_no))))
     LEFT JOIN pack_base pb ON ((pb.so_line_id = sol.id)))
     LEFT JOIN dispatch_base db ON ((db.so_line_id = sol.id)))
     LEFT JOIN invoice_base ib ON ((ib.so_line_id = sol.id)))
  WHERE (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text);


--
-- Name: v_report_planning_material_status; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_planning_material_status AS
 WITH job_qty AS (
         SELECT woj.wo_id,
            woj.so_number,
            woj.line_no,
            sum(COALESCE(woj.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS line_qty
           FROM public.work_order_jobs woj
          GROUP BY woj.wo_id, woj.so_number, woj.line_no
        ), job_share AS (
         SELECT jq.wo_id,
            jq.so_number,
            jq.line_no,
            jq.line_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN (sum(jq.line_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY jq.wo_id) > (0)::numeric) THEN (jq.line_qty / NULLIF(sum(jq.line_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY jq.wo_id), (0)::numeric))
                    ELSE (1.0 / (NULLIF(count(*) OVER (PARTITION BY jq.wo_id), 0))::numeric)
                END AS share_ratio
           FROM job_qty jq
        ), material_rows AS (
         SELECT wom.wo_id,
            wom.material_key,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(max(wom.item_name), ''::text), NULLIF(split_part(wom.material_key, '|'::text, 1), ''::text), ''::text) AS item_name,
            max(wom.uom) AS uom,
            sum(COALESCE(wom.required_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS required_qty
           FROM public.work_order_materials wom
          GROUP BY wom.wo_id, wom.material_key
        ), issued_rows AS (
         SELECT wo.id AS wo_id,
            il.remarks AS material_key,
            sum(COALESCE(il.qty_out, (0)::numeric)) AS issued_qty
           FROM (public.inv_ledger il
             JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.wo_number = il.ref_no)))
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(il.ref_type, ''::text)) = 'ISSUE'::text) AND (COALESCE(il.qty_out, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(il.remarks, ''::text) <> ''::text) AND (NOT (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
                   FROM public.inv_ledger rev
                  WHERE ((rev.ref_no = (il.id)::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(rev.ref_type, ''::text)) ~~ 'REV-%'::text))))))
          GROUP BY wo.id, il.remarks
        ), line_materials AS (
         SELECT js.so_number,
            js.line_no,
            mr.material_key,
            mr.item_name,
            mr.uom,
            round((COALESCE(mr.required_qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(js.share_ratio, (1)::numeric)), 3) AS required_qty,
            round((COALESCE(ir.issued_qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(js.share_ratio, (1)::numeric)), 3) AS issued_qty,
            round((GREATEST((COALESCE(mr.required_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(ir.issued_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(js.share_ratio, (1)::numeric)), 3) AS pending_qty
           FROM ((job_share js
             JOIN material_rows mr ON ((mr.wo_id = js.wo_id)))
             LEFT JOIN issued_rows ir ON (((ir.wo_id = mr.wo_id) AND (ir.material_key = mr.material_key))))
        ), stock_by_item AS (
         SELECT upper(COALESCE(inv_stock_mv.item_name, ''::text)) AS item_key,
            sum(COALESCE(inv_stock_mv.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS stock_qty
           FROM public.inv_stock_mv
          GROUP BY (upper(COALESCE(inv_stock_mv.item_name, ''::text)))
        ), receipt_by_line AS (
         SELECT purchase_po_receipts.po_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(purchase_po_receipts.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS received_qty,
            max(purchase_po_receipts.receipt_date) AS last_receipt_date,
            string_agg(DISTINCT purchase_po_receipts.challan_no, ', '::text ORDER BY purchase_po_receipts.challan_no) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(purchase_po_receipts.challan_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS receipt_refs
           FROM public.purchase_po_receipts
          GROUP BY purchase_po_receipts.po_line_id
        ), open_po_by_item AS (
         SELECT upper(COALESCE(pol.item_name, ''::text)) AS item_key,
            sum(COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS ordered_qty,
            sum(COALESCE(r.received_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS received_qty,
            sum(GREATEST((COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(r.received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric)) AS pending_receipt_qty,
            min(po.order_date) FILTER (WHERE (GREATEST((COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(r.received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric)) AS first_open_po_date,
            string_agg(DISTINCT pol.po_no, ', '::text ORDER BY pol.po_no) FILTER (WHERE ((GREATEST((COALESCE(pol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(r.received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) AND (COALESCE(pol.po_no, ''::text) <> ''::text))) AS po_nos,
            max(r.last_receipt_date) AS latest_receipt_date,
            string_agg(DISTINCT r.receipt_refs, ', '::text ORDER BY r.receipt_refs) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(r.receipt_refs, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS receipt_refs
           FROM ((public.purchase_order_lines pol
             LEFT JOIN public.purchase_orders po ON ((po.po_no = pol.po_no)))
             LEFT JOIN receipt_by_line r ON ((r.po_line_id = pol.id)))
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(pol.source_type, ''::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['ARTWORK_PLATE'::text, 'ARTWORK_DIE'::text, 'DIRECT_PLATE'::text, 'DIRECT_DIE'::text]))
          GROUP BY (upper(COALESCE(pol.item_name, ''::text)))
        ), open_pr_by_item AS (
         SELECT upper(COALESCE(inv_purchase_requests.item_name, ''::text)) AS item_key,
            sum(GREATEST((COALESCE(inv_purchase_requests.requested_qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(inv_purchase_requests.received_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric)) AS pending_pr_qty,
            string_agg(DISTINCT inv_purchase_requests.pr_no, ', '::text ORDER BY inv_purchase_requests.pr_no) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(inv_purchase_requests.pr_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS pr_nos
           FROM public.inv_purchase_requests
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(inv_purchase_requests.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CLOSED'::text)
          GROUP BY (upper(COALESCE(inv_purchase_requests.item_name, ''::text)))
        ), line_material_enriched AS (
         SELECT lm.so_number,
            lm.line_no,
            lm.material_key,
            lm.item_name,
            lm.uom,
            lm.required_qty,
            lm.issued_qty,
            lm.pending_qty,
            COALESCE(st.stock_qty, (0)::numeric) AS stock_qty,
            COALESCE(po.ordered_qty, (0)::numeric) AS ordered_qty,
            COALESCE(po.received_qty, (0)::numeric) AS received_qty,
            COALESCE(po.pending_receipt_qty, (0)::numeric) AS pending_receipt_qty,
            po.first_open_po_date,
            COALESCE(po.po_nos, ''::text) AS po_nos,
            po.latest_receipt_date,
            COALESCE(po.receipt_refs, ''::text) AS receipt_refs,
            COALESCE(pr.pending_pr_qty, (0)::numeric) AS pending_pr_qty,
            COALESCE(pr.pr_nos, ''::text) AS pr_nos,
                CASE
                    WHEN (COALESCE(lm.pending_qty, (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN 'ISSUED'::text
                    WHEN (COALESCE(st.stock_qty, (0)::numeric) >= COALESCE(lm.pending_qty, (0)::numeric)) THEN 'IN_STOCK'::text
                    WHEN (COALESCE(po.pending_receipt_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN 'ORDERED_PENDING_RECEIPT'::text
                    WHEN (COALESCE(pr.pending_pr_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric) THEN 'PR_PENDING_PO'::text
                    ELSE 'SHORTAGE'::text
                END AS material_line_status
           FROM (((line_materials lm
             LEFT JOIN stock_by_item st ON ((st.item_key = upper(COALESCE(lm.item_name, ''::text)))))
             LEFT JOIN open_po_by_item po ON ((po.item_key = upper(COALESCE(lm.item_name, ''::text)))))
             LEFT JOIN open_pr_by_item pr ON ((pr.item_key = upper(COALESCE(lm.item_name, ''::text)))))
        )
 SELECT so_number,
    line_no,
        CASE
            WHEN (sum(pending_qty) <= (0)::numeric) THEN 'ISSUED'::text
            WHEN bool_and((material_line_status = ANY (ARRAY['ISSUED'::text, 'IN_STOCK'::text]))) THEN 'IN_STOCK'::text
            WHEN bool_or((material_line_status = 'ORDERED_PENDING_RECEIPT'::text)) THEN 'ORDERED_PENDING_RECEIPT'::text
            WHEN bool_or((material_line_status = 'PR_PENDING_PO'::text)) THEN 'PR_PENDING_PO'::text
            ELSE 'SHORTAGE'::text
        END AS rm_availability_status,
    round(sum(required_qty), 3) AS rm_required_qty,
    round(sum(issued_qty), 3) AS rm_issued_qty,
    round(sum(pending_qty), 3) AS rm_pending_qty,
    round(sum(stock_qty), 3) AS rm_in_stock_qty,
    round(sum(GREATEST(((pending_qty - stock_qty) - pending_receipt_qty), (0)::numeric)), 3) AS rm_shortage_qty,
    round(sum(pending_receipt_qty), 3) AS rm_pending_receipt_qty,
    string_agg(DISTINCT po_nos, ', '::text ORDER BY po_nos) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(po_nos, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS rm_po_nos,
    min(first_open_po_date) AS rm_first_po_date,
    max(latest_receipt_date) AS rm_latest_grn_date,
    string_agg(DISTINCT receipt_refs, ', '::text ORDER BY receipt_refs) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(receipt_refs, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS rm_receipt_refs,
    string_agg(DISTINCT pr_nos, ', '::text ORDER BY pr_nos) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(pr_nos, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS rm_pr_nos,
    string_agg(((((((((((item_name || ': Req '::text) || TRIM(BOTH FROM to_char(required_qty, 'FM999999990.###'::text))) || ', Pending '::text) || TRIM(BOTH FROM to_char(pending_qty, 'FM999999990.###'::text))) || ', Stock '::text) || TRIM(BOTH FROM to_char(stock_qty, 'FM999999990.###'::text))) ||
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(po_nos, ''::text) <> ''::text) THEN (', PO '::text || po_nos)
            ELSE ''::text
        END) || ' ['::text) || material_line_status) || ']'::text), ' | '::text ORDER BY item_name, material_key) AS rm_detail
   FROM line_material_enriched
  GROUP BY so_number, line_no;


--
-- Name: v_report_planning_lines; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_planning_lines AS
 WITH artwork_ranked AS (
         SELECT a.so_id,
            a.line_no,
            a.id,
            a.artwork_no,
            a.product_type,
            a.plate_status,
            a.die_status,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(a.status, ''::text),
                CASE
                    WHEN (COALESCE(a.artwork_no, ''::text) <> ''::text) THEN 'PENDING_APPROVAL'::text
                    ELSE 'NO_ART'::text
                END) AS artwork_status,
            a.artwork_at,
            a.approved_at,
            row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY a.so_id, a.line_no ORDER BY a.approved_at DESC NULLS LAST, a.artwork_at DESC NULLS LAST, a.id DESC) AS rn
           FROM public.artworks a
        ), artwork_base AS (
         SELECT artwork_ranked.so_id,
            artwork_ranked.line_no,
            artwork_ranked.artwork_no,
            artwork_ranked.product_type,
            artwork_ranked.plate_status,
            artwork_ranked.die_status,
            artwork_ranked.artwork_status,
            artwork_ranked.artwork_at,
            artwork_ranked.approved_at AS artwork_approved_at
           FROM artwork_ranked
          WHERE (artwork_ranked.rn = 1)
        ), wo_base AS (
         SELECT woj.so_number,
            woj.line_no,
            count(DISTINCT woj.wo_id) AS wo_count,
            string_agg(DISTINCT wo.wo_number, ', '::text ORDER BY wo.wo_number) AS wo_numbers,
            min(wo.wo_date) AS first_wo_at,
            max(wo.wo_date) AS last_wo_at
           FROM (public.work_order_jobs woj
             JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.id = woj.wo_id)))
          GROUP BY woj.so_number, woj.line_no
        ), routing_meta AS (
         SELECT woj.so_number,
            woj.line_no,
            count(DISTINCT r.id) AS routing_count,
            min(wo.wo_date) AS routing_marked_at
           FROM ((public.work_order_jobs woj
             JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.id = woj.wo_id)))
             JOIN public.work_order_routing r ON ((r.wo_id = woj.wo_id)))
          GROUP BY woj.so_number, woj.line_no
        ), routing_steps AS (
         SELECT step_rows.so_number,
            step_rows.line_no,
            string_agg(step_rows.step_name, ' | '::text ORDER BY step_rows.sequence_no, step_rows.step_name) AS routing_steps
           FROM ( SELECT DISTINCT woj.so_number,
                    woj.line_no,
                    COALESCE(r.sequence_no, 0) AS sequence_no,
                    COALESCE(NULLIF(r.process_name, ''::text), NULLIF(r.department, ''::text), 'PROCESS'::text) AS step_name
                   FROM (public.work_order_jobs woj
                     JOIN public.work_order_routing r ON ((r.wo_id = woj.wo_id)))) step_rows
          GROUP BY step_rows.so_number, step_rows.line_no
        ), routing_base AS (
         SELECT rm.so_number,
            rm.line_no,
            rm.routing_count,
            rm.routing_marked_at,
            rs.routing_steps
           FROM (routing_meta rm
             LEFT JOIN routing_steps rs ON (((rs.so_number = rm.so_number) AND (rs.line_no = rm.line_no))))
        ), stage_rows AS (
         SELECT woj.so_number,
            woj.line_no,
            COALESCE(psq.process_name, 'PROCESS'::text) AS process_name,
            min(COALESCE(psq.sequence_no, 0)) AS sequence_no,
            sum(COALESCE(psq.planned_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS planned_qty,
            sum(COALESCE(psq.produced_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS produced_qty,
            max(pe.entry_datetime) AS stage_updated_at,
                CASE
                    WHEN bool_or((upper(COALESCE(psq.status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'SHORT_CLOSED'::text)) THEN 'SHORT_CLOSED'::text
                    WHEN bool_or((upper(COALESCE(psq.status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'HOLD'::text)) THEN 'HOLD'::text
                    WHEN bool_or((upper(COALESCE(psq.status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'IN_PROGRESS'::text)) THEN 'IN_PROGRESS'::text
                    WHEN bool_and((upper(COALESCE(psq.status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'COMPLETED'::text)) THEN 'COMPLETED'::text
                    ELSE 'PENDING'::text
                END AS stage_status
           FROM ((public.work_order_jobs woj
             JOIN public.v_production_stage_queue_fast psq ON ((psq.wo_id = woj.wo_id)))
             LEFT JOIN public.production_entries pe ON ((pe.routing_id = psq.routing_id)))
          GROUP BY woj.so_number, woj.line_no, COALESCE(psq.process_name, 'PROCESS'::text)
        ), stage_base AS (
         SELECT stage_rows.so_number,
            stage_rows.line_no,
            string_agg((((((((stage_rows.process_name || ': '::text) || TRIM(BOTH FROM to_char(COALESCE(stage_rows.produced_qty, (0)::numeric), 'FM999999990.###'::text))) || '/'::text) || TRIM(BOTH FROM to_char(COALESCE(stage_rows.planned_qty, (0)::numeric), 'FM999999990.###'::text))) || ' ['::text) || stage_rows.stage_status) || ']'::text), ' | '::text ORDER BY stage_rows.sequence_no, stage_rows.process_name) AS stage_summary,
            max(stage_rows.stage_updated_at) AS stage_last_updated_at
           FROM stage_rows
          GROUP BY stage_rows.so_number, stage_rows.line_no
        ), current_stage AS (
         SELECT DISTINCT ON (stage_rows.so_number, stage_rows.line_no) stage_rows.so_number,
            stage_rows.line_no,
            stage_rows.process_name AS current_stage,
            stage_rows.stage_status AS current_stage_status
           FROM stage_rows
          WHERE (upper(COALESCE(stage_rows.stage_status, 'PENDING'::text)) <> 'COMPLETED'::text)
          ORDER BY stage_rows.so_number, stage_rows.line_no,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(stage_rows.stage_status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'IN_PROGRESS'::text) THEN 0
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(stage_rows.stage_status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'PENDING'::text) THEN 1
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(stage_rows.stage_status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'HOLD'::text) THEN 2
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(stage_rows.stage_status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'SHORT_CLOSED'::text) THEN 3
                    ELSE 4
                END, stage_rows.sequence_no, stage_rows.process_name
        ), invoice_base AS (
         SELECT il.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS billed_qty,
            count(DISTINCT il.invoice_id) AS invoice_count,
            min(inv.invoice_date) AS first_invoice_date,
            max(inv.invoice_date) AS last_invoice_date,
            max(inv.created_at) AS last_billed_at,
            string_agg(DISTINCT inv.invoice_no, ', '::text ORDER BY inv.invoice_no) FILTER (WHERE (COALESCE(inv.invoice_no, ''::text) <> ''::text)) AS invoice_nos,
            string_agg(DISTINCT to_char((inv.invoice_date)::timestamp with time zone, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text), ', '::text ORDER BY (to_char((inv.invoice_date)::timestamp with time zone, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text))) FILTER (WHERE (inv.invoice_date IS NOT NULL)) AS invoice_dates
           FROM (public.invoice_lines il
             LEFT JOIN public.invoices inv ON ((inv.id = il.invoice_id)))
          WHERE (il.so_line_id IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY il.so_line_id
        )
 SELECT so.id AS so_id,
    sol.id AS so_line_id,
    so.so_number,
    so.so_date,
    so.po_number,
    so.po_date,
    COALESCE(so.created_at, (so.so_date)::timestamp without time zone) AS so_datetime,
    so.created_at AS so_created_at,
    to_char(COALESCE(so.created_at, (so.so_date)::timestamp without time zone), 'HH24:MI'::text) AS so_time,
    so.client_code,
    COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code) AS client_name,
    so.sales_rep,
    sol.line_no,
    COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text) AS so_line_status,
    sol.product_code,
    sol.product_name,
    sol.category,
    sol.division,
    COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) AS order_qty,
    sol.unit,
    sol.rate,
    sol.expected_delivery,
    sol.final_delivery,
    COALESCE(sol.accounts_status, 'PENDING'::text) AS accounts_status,
    sol.accounts_at,
    COALESCE(sol.business_status, 'PENDING'::text) AS business_status,
    sol.business_at,
        CASE
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(sol.accounts_status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'APPROVED'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(sol.business_status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'APPROVED'::text)) THEN 'APPROVED'::text
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(sol.accounts_status, ''::text)) = 'HOLD'::text) OR (upper(COALESCE(sol.business_status, ''::text)) = 'HOLD'::text)) THEN 'HOLD'::text
            ELSE 'PENDING'::text
        END AS sales_approval_status,
        CASE
            WHEN ((upper(COALESCE(sol.accounts_status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'APPROVED'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(sol.business_status, 'PENDING'::text)) = 'APPROVED'::text)) THEN GREATEST(COALESCE(sol.accounts_at, sol.business_at), COALESCE(sol.business_at, sol.accounts_at))
            ELSE NULL::timestamp with time zone
        END AS sales_approval_at,
    ab.artwork_no,
    ab.product_type,
    ab.plate_status,
    ab.die_status,
    COALESCE(ab.artwork_status, 'NO_ART'::text) AS artwork_status,
    ab.artwork_at,
    ab.artwork_approved_at,
    COALESCE(wb.wo_count, (0)::bigint) AS wo_count,
    COALESCE(wb.wo_numbers, ''::text) AS wo_numbers,
    wb.first_wo_at,
    wb.last_wo_at,
    COALESCE(rb.routing_count, (0)::bigint) AS routing_count,
    COALESCE(rb.routing_steps, ''::text) AS routing_steps,
    rb.routing_marked_at,
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(rb.routing_count, (0)::bigint) > 0) THEN 'MARKED'::text
            ELSE 'PENDING'::text
        END AS routing_status,
    COALESCE(cs.current_stage, ''::text) AS current_stage,
    COALESCE(cs.current_stage_status, ''::text) AS current_stage_status,
    COALESCE(sb.stage_summary, ''::text) AS stage_summary,
    sb.stage_last_updated_at,
    COALESCE(ms.rm_availability_status, 'MATERIAL_NOT_DEFINED'::text) AS rm_availability_status,
    COALESCE(ms.rm_required_qty, (0)::numeric) AS rm_required_qty,
    COALESCE(ms.rm_issued_qty, (0)::numeric) AS rm_issued_qty,
    COALESCE(ms.rm_pending_qty, (0)::numeric) AS rm_pending_qty,
    COALESCE(ms.rm_in_stock_qty, (0)::numeric) AS rm_in_stock_qty,
    COALESCE(ms.rm_shortage_qty, (0)::numeric) AS rm_shortage_qty,
    COALESCE(ms.rm_pending_receipt_qty, (0)::numeric) AS rm_pending_receipt_qty,
    COALESCE(ms.rm_po_nos, ''::text) AS rm_po_nos,
    ms.rm_first_po_date,
    ms.rm_latest_grn_date,
    COALESCE(ms.rm_receipt_refs, ''::text) AS rm_receipt_refs,
    COALESCE(ms.rm_pr_nos, ''::text) AS rm_pr_nos,
    COALESCE(ms.rm_detail, ''::text) AS rm_detail,
    COALESCE(ts.plate_procurement_status, ''::text) AS plate_procurement_status,
    COALESCE(ts.plate_po_nos, ''::text) AS plate_po_nos,
    ts.plate_ordered_on,
    ts.plate_received_on,
    COALESCE(ts.plate_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric) AS plate_ordered_qty,
    COALESCE(ts.plate_received_qty, (0)::numeric) AS plate_received_qty,
    COALESCE(ts.plate_pending_qty, (0)::numeric) AS plate_pending_qty,
    COALESCE(ts.plate_vendors, ''::text) AS plate_vendors,
    COALESCE(ts.plate_receipt_refs, ''::text) AS plate_receipt_refs,
    COALESCE(ts.die_procurement_status, ''::text) AS die_procurement_status,
    COALESCE(ts.die_po_nos, ''::text) AS die_po_nos,
    ts.die_ordered_on,
    ts.die_received_on,
    COALESCE(ts.die_ordered_qty, (0)::numeric) AS die_ordered_qty,
    COALESCE(ts.die_received_qty, (0)::numeric) AS die_received_qty,
    COALESCE(ts.die_pending_qty, (0)::numeric) AS die_pending_qty,
    COALESCE(ts.die_vendors, ''::text) AS die_vendors,
    COALESCE(ts.die_receipt_refs, ''::text) AS die_receipt_refs,
    COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) AS billed_qty,
    GREATEST((COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS billing_pending_qty,
    COALESCE(ib.invoice_count, (0)::bigint) AS invoice_count,
    COALESCE(ib.invoice_nos, ''::text) AS invoice_nos,
    COALESCE(ib.invoice_dates, ''::text) AS invoice_dates,
    ib.first_invoice_date,
    ib.last_invoice_date,
    ib.last_billed_at,
        CASE
            WHEN ((COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) >= COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric)) AND (COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric)) THEN 'CLOSED'::text
            ELSE 'PENDING'::text
        END AS billing_status
   FROM ((((((((((public.sales_orders so
     JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON ((sol.so_id = so.id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
     LEFT JOIN artwork_base ab ON (((ab.so_id = so.id) AND (ab.line_no = sol.line_no))))
     LEFT JOIN wo_base wb ON (((wb.so_number = so.so_number) AND (wb.line_no = sol.line_no))))
     LEFT JOIN routing_base rb ON (((rb.so_number = so.so_number) AND (rb.line_no = sol.line_no))))
     LEFT JOIN stage_base sb ON (((sb.so_number = so.so_number) AND (sb.line_no = sol.line_no))))
     LEFT JOIN current_stage cs ON (((cs.so_number = so.so_number) AND (cs.line_no = sol.line_no))))
     LEFT JOIN public.v_report_planning_material_status ms ON (((ms.so_number = so.so_number) AND ((ms.line_no)::text = (sol.line_no)::text))))
     LEFT JOIN public.v_report_planning_tooling_status ts ON (((ts.so_number = so.so_number) AND ((ts.line_no)::text = (sol.line_no)::text))))
     LEFT JOIN invoice_base ib ON ((ib.so_line_id = sol.id)))
  WHERE (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text);


--
-- Name: v_report_production_bottleneck; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_production_bottleneck AS
 SELECT routing_id,
    wo_id,
    wo_number,
    wo_date,
    so_numbers,
    line_nos,
    artwork_nos,
    client_name,
    product_names,
    process_name,
    department,
    planned_machine,
    expected_delivery,
    planned_qty,
    produced_qty,
    balance_qty,
    status,
    department_category
   FROM public.v_production_stage_queue_fast
  WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(status, 'PENDING'::text)) <> 'COMPLETED'::text) AND (COALESCE(balance_qty, (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric));


--
-- Name: v_report_production_nop_daily; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_production_nop_daily AS
 WITH machine_standards(machine_key, machine, standard_make_ready_minutes, target_hourly_output) AS (
         VALUES ('SM74'::text,'Heidelberg SM74'::text,(30)::numeric,(4125)::numeric), ('CD102'::text,'CD102'::text,(30)::numeric,(4125)::numeric), ('LAM1'::text,'Lamination Roll-1'::text,(10)::numeric,(1100)::numeric), ('LAM2'::text,'Lamination 02'::text,(10)::numeric,(1100)::numeric), ('LAM3'::text,'Lamination 03'::text,(10)::numeric,(1100)::numeric), ('DIE1'::text,'Die Cutting 01'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE2'::text,'Die Cutting 02'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE3'::text,'Die Cutting 03'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE4'::text,'Die Cutting 04'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE5'::text,'Die Cutting 05'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('FLEXOPRINT'::text,'Markany Flexo E5'::text,(30)::numeric,(55)::numeric), ('FLEXODIE'::text,'Flexo Die Cutting Offline'::text,(30)::numeric,(44)::numeric), ('FLEXOSLIT'::text,'Rhyguan'::text,(30)::numeric,(88)::numeric), ('DIGITAL'::text,'Canon Digital Printing'::text,(10)::numeric,(660)::numeric), ('2PLY'::text,'2 Ply Making Machine'::text,(15)::numeric,(2000)::numeric), ('ROTARY'::text,'Rotary Cutting'::text,(0)::numeric,(650)::numeric), ('AUTOWINDOW'::text,'Auto Window Pasting'::text,(0)::numeric,(1500)::numeric), ('MSP1'::text,'Manual Sheet Pasting'::text,(0)::numeric,(600)::numeric), ('MSP2'::text,'Manual Pasting'::text,(0)::numeric,(600)::numeric), ('FMZ'::text,'Flute Laminator'::text,(0)::numeric,(2000)::numeric), ('DCMAN1'::text,'Manual Die Cutting 04'::text,(45)::numeric,(800)::numeric), ('DCMAN2'::text,'Manual Die Cutting 05'::text,(45)::numeric,(800)::numeric), ('DCAUTO'::text,'Automatic Die Cutting'::text,(90)::numeric,(2500)::numeric), ('STMAN1'::text,'Stitching Manual 01'::text,(0)::numeric,(250)::numeric), ('STMAN2'::text,'Stitching Manual 02'::text,(0)::numeric,(250)::numeric), ('STAUTO'::text,'Auto Stitching'::text,(0)::numeric,(200)::numeric), ('FOLDER'::text,'Folder Gluer'::text,(45)::numeric,(15000)::numeric), ('RS4'::text,'RS4 Printing'::text,(60)::numeric,(1500)::numeric)
        ), entry_base AS (
         SELECT pe.id,
            pe.routing_id,
            (timezone('Asia/Kolkata'::text, pe.entry_datetime))::date AS production_date,
            timezone('Asia/Kolkata'::text, pe.entry_datetime) AS entry_local_ts,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(pe.machine, ''::text), NULLIF(r.planned_machine, ''::text), 'Unassigned'::text) AS raw_machine,
            upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(pe.machine, ''::text), NULLIF(r.planned_machine, ''::text), 'UNASSIGNED'::text)) AS raw_machine_upper,
            upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(r.process_name, ''::text), NULLIF(r.department, ''::text), ''::text)) AS raw_process_upper,
            NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM pe.operator_name), ''::text) AS operator_name,
            GREATEST(COALESCE(pe.produced_qty, (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric) AS produced_qty,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(pe.job_reference, ''::text), ((NULLIF(pe.so_number, ''::text) || '-'::text) || NULLIF(pe.line_no, ''::text)), (pe.routing_id)::text, (pe.id)::text) AS job_key,
            COALESCE((NULLIF("substring"(COALESCE(pe.downtime_reason, ''::text), 'DOWN:[[:space:]]*([0-9]+[.]?[0-9]*)[[:space:]]*min'::text), ''::text))::numeric, (0)::numeric) AS downtime_minutes,
            NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM regexp_replace(COALESCE("substring"(COALESCE(pe.downtime_reason, ''::text), 'DOWN:([^|]+)'::text), ''::text), '^[[:space:]]*[0-9]+[.]?[0-9]*[[:space:]]*min[[:space:]]*-?[[:space:]]*'::text, ''::text)), ''::text) AS downtime_label
           FROM (public.production_entries pe
             LEFT JOIN public.work_order_routing r ON ((r.id = pe.routing_id)))
          WHERE (pe.entry_datetime IS NOT NULL)
        ), entry_normalized AS (
         SELECT eb.id,
            eb.routing_id,
            eb.production_date,
            eb.entry_local_ts,
            eb.raw_machine,
            eb.raw_machine_upper,
            eb.raw_process_upper,
            eb.operator_name,
            eb.produced_qty,
            eb.job_key,
            eb.downtime_minutes,
            eb.downtime_label,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%SM74%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%SM-74%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%HEIDELBERG%'::text)) THEN 'SM74'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CD102%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CD-102%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CD 102%'::text)) THEN 'CD102'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 01%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 1%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION ROLL-1%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION ROLL 1%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAM-1%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper = 'LAM1'::text)) THEN 'LAM1'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 02%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 2%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAM-2%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper = 'LAM2'::text)) THEN 'LAM2'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 03%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 3%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAM-3%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper = 'LAM3'::text)) THEN 'LAM3'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%RHYGUAN%'::text) OR (eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) OR (eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text) OR (eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%SLIT%'::text)) THEN 'FLEXOSLIT'::text
                    WHEN (((eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) AND ((eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%DIE%'::text) OR (eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%CUT%'::text))) OR ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) AND ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CUT%'::text)))) THEN 'FLEXODIE'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MARKANY%'::text)) THEN 'FLEXOPRINT'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CANON%'::text)) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper = ANY (ARRAY['AUTOMATIC'::text, 'AUTO'::text])) THEN '2PLY'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%2 PLY%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%TWO PLY%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%PLY MAKING%'::text)) THEN '2PLY'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%ROTARY%'::text) THEN 'ROTARY'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTO WINDOW%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTOMATIC WINDOW%'::text) OR ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%WINDOW%'::text) AND (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%PAST%'::text) AND (eb.raw_machine_upper !~~ '%MANUAL%'::text))) THEN 'AUTOWINDOW'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FMZ%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FLUTE%'::text)) THEN 'FMZ'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL PASTING%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL SHEET PASTING 2%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL SHEET PASTING - 2%'::text)) THEN 'MSP2'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL SHEET PASTING%'::text) THEN 'MSP1'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE CUTTING 05%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL 2%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL - 2%'::text)) THEN 'DCMAN2'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE CUTTING 04%'::text)) THEN 'DCMAN1'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTOMATIC DIE%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTO DIE%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING AUTO%'::text)) THEN 'DCAUTO'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 01%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-01%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-01%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-1%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-1%'::text)) THEN 'DIE1'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 02%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-02%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-02%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-2%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-2%'::text)) THEN 'DIE2'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 03%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-03%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-03%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-3%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-3%'::text)) THEN 'DIE3'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 04%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-4%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-4%'::text)) THEN 'DIE4'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 05%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-5%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-5%'::text)) THEN 'DIE5'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL 02%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL - 2%'::text)) THEN 'STMAN2'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL 01%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL - 1%'::text)) THEN 'STMAN1'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTO STITCH%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING AUTO%'::text)) THEN 'STAUTO'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper = 'STITCHING'::text) THEN 'STMAN1'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FOLDER GLUER%'::text) THEN 'FOLDER'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper = ANY (ARRAY['PASTING'::text, 'SIDE PASTING'::text])) THEN 'FOLDER'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%RS4%'::text) THEN 'RS4'::text
                    ELSE regexp_replace(eb.raw_machine_upper, '[^A-Z0-9]+'::text, ''::text, 'g'::text)
                END AS machine_key
           FROM entry_base eb
        ), daily_agg AS (
         SELECT en.production_date,
            en.machine_key,
            COALESCE(ms.machine, max(en.raw_machine)) AS machine,
            COALESCE(ms.standard_make_ready_minutes, (0)::numeric) AS standard_make_ready_minutes,
            COALESCE(ms.target_hourly_output, (0)::numeric) AS target_hourly_output,
            string_agg(DISTINCT en.operator_name, ', '::text ORDER BY en.operator_name) FILTER (WHERE (en.operator_name IS NOT NULL)) AS operator_names,
            max(en.entry_local_ts) AS last_completion_at,
            count(DISTINCT en.job_key) AS make_ready_jobs,
            sum(en.produced_qty) AS actual_production,
            (sum(en.downtime_minutes) / 60.0) AS downtime_hours,
            string_agg(DISTINCT en.downtime_label, ', '::text ORDER BY en.downtime_label) FILTER (WHERE (en.downtime_label IS NOT NULL)) AS downtime_reasons
           FROM (entry_normalized en
             LEFT JOIN machine_standards ms ON ((ms.machine_key = en.machine_key)))
          GROUP BY en.production_date, en.machine_key, ms.machine, ms.standard_make_ready_minutes, ms.target_hourly_output
        ), daily_calc AS (
         SELECT da.production_date,
            da.machine_key,
            da.machine,
            da.standard_make_ready_minutes,
            da.target_hourly_output,
            da.operator_names,
            da.last_completion_at,
            da.make_ready_jobs,
            da.actual_production,
            da.downtime_hours,
            da.downtime_reasons,
            round(((8)::numeric +
                CASE
                    WHEN ((da.last_completion_at)::time without time zone > '17:30:00'::time without time zone) THEN (EXTRACT(epoch FROM (da.last_completion_at - ((da.production_date)::timestamp without time zone + ('17:30:00'::time without time zone)::interval))) / 3600.0)
                    ELSE (0)::numeric
                END), 2) AS working_hours,
            round((((da.make_ready_jobs)::numeric * da.standard_make_ready_minutes) / 60.0), 2) AS total_make_ready_hours
           FROM daily_agg da
        ), final_calc AS (
         SELECT dc.production_date,
            dc.machine_key,
            dc.machine,
            dc.standard_make_ready_minutes,
            dc.target_hourly_output,
            dc.operator_names,
            dc.last_completion_at,
            dc.make_ready_jobs,
            dc.actual_production,
            dc.downtime_hours,
            dc.downtime_reasons,
            dc.working_hours,
            dc.total_make_ready_hours,
            round(GREATEST(((dc.working_hours - COALESCE(dc.downtime_hours, (0)::numeric)) - dc.total_make_ready_hours), (0)::numeric), 2) AS actual_running_hours
           FROM daily_calc dc
        )
 SELECT production_date,
    machine_key,
    machine,
    COALESCE(operator_names, ''::text) AS operator_names,
    round(working_hours, 2) AS working_hours,
    COALESCE(downtime_reasons, ''::text) AS downtime_reasons,
    round(COALESCE(downtime_hours, (0)::numeric), 2) AS downtime_hours,
    make_ready_jobs,
    standard_make_ready_minutes,
    total_make_ready_hours,
    actual_running_hours,
    target_hourly_output,
    round((actual_running_hours *
        CASE
            WHEN (machine_key = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXOPRINT'::text, 'FLEXODIE'::text, 'FLEXOSLIT'::text])) THEN (target_hourly_output * (60)::numeric)
            ELSE target_hourly_output
        END), 2) AS target_production,
    round(actual_production, 2) AS actual_production,
    round(((actual_production / NULLIF(actual_running_hours, (0)::numeric)) / (
        CASE
            WHEN (machine_key = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXOPRINT'::text, 'FLEXODIE'::text, 'FLEXOSLIT'::text])) THEN 60
            ELSE 1
        END)::numeric), 2) AS actual_nop,
    round((((actual_production / NULLIF(actual_running_hours, (0)::numeric)) / (
        CASE
            WHEN (machine_key = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXOPRINT'::text, 'FLEXODIE'::text, 'FLEXOSLIT'::text])) THEN 60
            ELSE 1
        END)::numeric) - NULLIF(target_hourly_output, (0)::numeric)), 2) AS nop_deviation,
    round(((((actual_production / NULLIF(actual_running_hours, (0)::numeric)) / (
        CASE
            WHEN (machine_key = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXOPRINT'::text, 'FLEXODIE'::text, 'FLEXOSLIT'::text])) THEN 60
            ELSE 1
        END)::numeric) / NULLIF(target_hourly_output, (0)::numeric)) * 100.0), 2) AS nop_achievement_pct,
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(target_hourly_output, (0)::numeric) <= (0)::numeric) THEN 'NO_TARGET'::text
            WHEN (((actual_production / NULLIF(actual_running_hours, (0)::numeric)) / (
            CASE
                WHEN (machine_key = ANY (ARRAY['FLEXOPRINT'::text, 'FLEXODIE'::text, 'FLEXOSLIT'::text])) THEN 60
                ELSE 1
            END)::numeric) >= target_hourly_output) THEN 'ON_TARGET'::text
            ELSE 'BELOW_TARGET'::text
        END AS performance_status,
    last_completion_at
   FROM final_calc;


--
-- Name: v_report_production_nop_machine_cleanup; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_production_nop_machine_cleanup AS
 WITH machine_standards(machine_key, machine, standard_make_ready_minutes, target_hourly_output) AS (
         VALUES ('SM74'::text,'Heidelberg SM74'::text,(30)::numeric,(4125)::numeric), ('CD102'::text,'CD102'::text,(30)::numeric,(4125)::numeric), ('LAM1'::text,'Lamination Roll-1'::text,(10)::numeric,(1100)::numeric), ('LAM2'::text,'Lamination 02'::text,(10)::numeric,(1100)::numeric), ('LAM3'::text,'Lamination 03'::text,(10)::numeric,(1100)::numeric), ('DIE1'::text,'Die Cutting 01'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE2'::text,'Die Cutting 02'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE3'::text,'Die Cutting 03'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE4'::text,'Die Cutting 04'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('DIE5'::text,'Die Cutting 05'::text,(30)::numeric,(880)::numeric), ('FLEXOPRINT'::text,'Markany Flexo E5'::text,(30)::numeric,(55)::numeric), ('FLEXODIE'::text,'Flexo Die Cutting Offline'::text,(30)::numeric,(44)::numeric), ('FLEXOSLIT'::text,'Rhyguan'::text,(30)::numeric,(88)::numeric), ('DIGITAL'::text,'Canon Digital Printing'::text,(10)::numeric,(660)::numeric), ('2PLY'::text,'2 Ply Making Machine'::text,(15)::numeric,(2000)::numeric), ('ROTARY'::text,'Rotary Cutting'::text,(0)::numeric,(650)::numeric), ('AUTOWINDOW'::text,'Auto Window Pasting'::text,(0)::numeric,(1500)::numeric), ('MSP1'::text,'Manual Sheet Pasting'::text,(0)::numeric,(600)::numeric), ('MSP2'::text,'Manual Pasting'::text,(0)::numeric,(600)::numeric), ('FMZ'::text,'Flute Laminator'::text,(0)::numeric,(2000)::numeric), ('DCMAN1'::text,'Manual Die Cutting 04'::text,(45)::numeric,(800)::numeric), ('DCMAN2'::text,'Manual Die Cutting 05'::text,(45)::numeric,(800)::numeric), ('DCAUTO'::text,'Automatic Die Cutting'::text,(90)::numeric,(2500)::numeric), ('STMAN1'::text,'Stitching Manual 01'::text,(0)::numeric,(250)::numeric), ('STMAN2'::text,'Stitching Manual 02'::text,(0)::numeric,(250)::numeric), ('STAUTO'::text,'Auto Stitching'::text,(0)::numeric,(200)::numeric), ('FOLDER'::text,'Folder Gluer'::text,(45)::numeric,(15000)::numeric), ('RS4'::text,'RS4 Printing'::text,(60)::numeric,(1500)::numeric)
        ), entry_base AS (
         SELECT pe.id AS production_entry_id,
            pe.routing_id,
            r.wo_id,
            wo.wo_number,
            pe.so_number,
            pe.line_no,
            pe.job_reference,
            timezone('Asia/Kolkata'::text, pe.entry_datetime) AS entry_local_ts,
            (timezone('Asia/Kolkata'::text, pe.entry_datetime))::date AS production_date,
            NULLIF(pe.machine, ''::text) AS production_entry_machine,
            NULLIF(r.planned_machine, ''::text) AS routing_planned_machine,
            NULLIF(r.process_name, ''::text) AS routing_process_name,
            NULLIF(r.department, ''::text) AS routing_department,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(pe.machine, ''::text), NULLIF(r.planned_machine, ''::text), 'Unassigned'::text) AS raw_machine,
            upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(pe.machine, ''::text), NULLIF(r.planned_machine, ''::text), 'UNASSIGNED'::text)) AS raw_machine_upper,
            upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(r.process_name, ''::text), NULLIF(r.department, ''::text), ''::text)) AS raw_process_upper,
            NULLIF(TRIM(BOTH FROM pe.operator_name), ''::text) AS operator_name,
            GREATEST(COALESCE(pe.produced_qty, (0)::numeric), (0)::numeric) AS produced_qty
           FROM ((public.production_entries pe
             LEFT JOIN public.work_order_routing r ON ((r.id = pe.routing_id)))
             LEFT JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.id = r.wo_id)))
          WHERE (pe.entry_datetime IS NOT NULL)
        ), entry_normalized AS (
         SELECT eb.production_entry_id,
            eb.routing_id,
            eb.wo_id,
            eb.wo_number,
            eb.so_number,
            eb.line_no,
            eb.job_reference,
            eb.entry_local_ts,
            eb.production_date,
            eb.production_entry_machine,
            eb.routing_planned_machine,
            eb.routing_process_name,
            eb.routing_department,
            eb.raw_machine,
            eb.raw_machine_upper,
            eb.raw_process_upper,
            eb.operator_name,
            eb.produced_qty,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%SM74%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%SM-74%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%HEIDELBERG%'::text)) THEN 'SM74'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CD102%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CD-102%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CD 102%'::text)) THEN 'CD102'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 01%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 1%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION ROLL-1%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION ROLL 1%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAM-1%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper = 'LAM1'::text)) THEN 'LAM1'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 02%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 2%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAM-2%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper = 'LAM2'::text)) THEN 'LAM2'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 03%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAMINATION 3%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%LAM-3%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper = 'LAM3'::text)) THEN 'LAM3'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%RHYGUAN%'::text) OR (eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%INSPECTION%'::text) OR (eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%SLITTING%'::text) OR (eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%SLIT%'::text)) THEN 'FLEXOSLIT'::text
                    WHEN (((eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) AND ((eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%DIE%'::text) OR (eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%CUT%'::text))) OR ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) AND ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CUT%'::text)))) THEN 'FLEXODIE'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_process_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MARKANY%'::text)) THEN 'FLEXOPRINT'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%CANON%'::text)) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper = ANY (ARRAY['AUTOMATIC'::text, 'AUTO'::text])) THEN '2PLY'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%2 PLY%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%TWO PLY%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%PLY MAKING%'::text)) THEN '2PLY'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%ROTARY%'::text) THEN 'ROTARY'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTO WINDOW%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTOMATIC WINDOW%'::text) OR ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%WINDOW%'::text) AND (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%PAST%'::text) AND (eb.raw_machine_upper !~~ '%MANUAL%'::text))) THEN 'AUTOWINDOW'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FMZ%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FLUTE%'::text)) THEN 'FMZ'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL PASTING%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL SHEET PASTING 2%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL SHEET PASTING - 2%'::text)) THEN 'MSP2'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL SHEET PASTING%'::text) THEN 'MSP1'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE CUTTING 05%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL 2%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL - 2%'::text)) THEN 'DCMAN2'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING MANUAL%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%MANUAL DIE CUTTING 04%'::text)) THEN 'DCMAN1'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTOMATIC DIE%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTO DIE%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING AUTO%'::text)) THEN 'DCAUTO'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 01%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-01%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-01%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-1%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-1%'::text)) THEN 'DIE1'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 02%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-02%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-02%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-2%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-2%'::text)) THEN 'DIE2'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 03%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING-03%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-CUTTING-03%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-3%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-3%'::text)) THEN 'DIE3'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 04%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-4%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-4%'::text)) THEN 'DIE4'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE CUTTING 05%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DIE-5%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%DYE-5%'::text)) THEN 'DIE5'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL 02%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL - 2%'::text)) THEN 'STMAN2'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL 01%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING MANUAL - 1%'::text)) THEN 'STMAN1'::text
                    WHEN ((eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%AUTO STITCH%'::text) OR (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%STITCHING AUTO%'::text)) THEN 'STAUTO'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper = 'STITCHING'::text) THEN 'STMAN1'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%FOLDER GLUER%'::text) THEN 'FOLDER'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper = ANY (ARRAY['PASTING'::text, 'SIDE PASTING'::text])) THEN 'FOLDER'::text
                    WHEN (eb.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%RS4%'::text) THEN 'RS4'::text
                    ELSE regexp_replace(eb.raw_machine_upper, '[^A-Z0-9]+'::text, ''::text, 'g'::text)
                END AS normalized_machine_key
           FROM entry_base eb
        )
 SELECT en.production_entry_id,
    en.routing_id,
    en.wo_id,
    en.wo_number,
    en.so_number,
    en.line_no,
    en.job_reference,
    en.production_date,
    en.entry_local_ts,
    en.production_entry_machine,
    en.routing_planned_machine,
    en.routing_process_name,
    en.routing_department,
    en.raw_machine AS current_machine_input,
    en.normalized_machine_key,
    COALESCE(ms.machine, en.raw_machine) AS report_machine_label,
    COALESCE(ms.target_hourly_output, (0)::numeric) AS target_hourly_output,
    en.operator_name,
    en.produced_qty,
        CASE
            WHEN (en.raw_machine_upper = 'MANUAL'::text) THEN 'Review production_entries.machine / work_order_routing.planned_machine and assign the actual manual process machine.'::text
            WHEN (en.raw_machine_upper = ANY (ARRAY['AUTOMATIC'::text, 'AUTO'::text])) THEN 'Reporting maps this to 2 Ply Making Machine. Update source only after confirming.'::text
            WHEN (en.raw_machine_upper = ANY (ARRAY['PASTING'::text, 'SIDE PASTING'::text])) THEN 'Reporting maps this to Folder Gluer. Update source only after confirming.'::text
            WHEN (en.raw_machine_upper = 'STITCHING'::text) THEN 'Reporting maps this to Stitching Manual 01. Update source only after confirming.'::text
            WHEN (en.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%UV%'::text) THEN 'Target not configured yet. Confirm whether this should be RS4 Printing or a separate UV target.'::text
            WHEN (ms.machine_key IS NULL) THEN 'No NOP target found for this machine input. Review and map safely before updating live data.'::text
            ELSE ''::text
        END AS review_note,
    'production_entries.machine / work_order_routing.planned_machine'::text AS review_columns,
    'Use this view to identify records first; do not update live machine values without operational confirmation.'::text AS safe_update_note
   FROM (entry_normalized en
     LEFT JOIN machine_standards ms ON ((ms.machine_key = en.normalized_machine_key)))
  WHERE ((en.raw_machine_upper = ANY (ARRAY['MANUAL'::text, 'AUTOMATIC'::text, 'AUTO'::text, 'PASTING'::text, 'SIDE PASTING'::text, 'STITCHING'::text])) OR (en.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%ROTARY%'::text) OR (en.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%WINDOW%'::text) OR (en.raw_machine_upper ~~ '%UV%'::text) OR (ms.machine_key IS NULL));


--
-- Name: v_report_unbilled_dispatch; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_report_unbilled_dispatch AS
 WITH dispatch_base AS (
         SELECT dr.so_line_id,
            max(dr.so_number) AS so_number,
            max(dr.line_no) AS line_no,
            max(dr.product_name) AS product_name,
            max(dr.dispatch_date) AS dispatch_date,
            sum(COALESCE(dr.dispatch_qty, (0)::numeric)) AS dispatched_qty
           FROM public.dispatch_records dr
          GROUP BY dr.so_line_id
        ), invoice_base AS (
         SELECT il.so_line_id,
            sum(COALESCE(il.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS billed_qty
           FROM public.invoice_lines il
          GROUP BY il.so_line_id
        )
 SELECT db.so_line_id,
    db.so_number,
    db.line_no,
    db.product_name,
    db.dispatch_date,
    db.dispatched_qty,
    COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric) AS billed_qty,
    GREATEST((db.dispatched_qty - COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS unbilled_qty
   FROM (dispatch_base db
     LEFT JOIN invoice_base ib ON ((ib.so_line_id = db.so_line_id)))
  WHERE (GREATEST((db.dispatched_qty - COALESCE(ib.billed_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) > (0)::numeric);


--
-- Name: v_sales_order_advance_payment_clearance; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_sales_order_advance_payment_clearance WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 SELECT so.id AS so_id,
    so.so_number,
    so.so_date,
    so.created_at AS so_created_at,
    so.client_code,
    COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code, ''::text) AS client_name,
    COALESCE(so.sales_rep, ''::text) AS sales_rep,
    (count(l.id))::integer AS line_count,
    (COALESCE(sum(COALESCE(l.qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric))::numeric(18,3) AS total_qty,
    string_agg(DISTINCT COALESCE(NULLIF(l.product_name, ''::text), NULLIF(l.product_code, ''::text), 'Item'::text), ' | '::text ORDER BY COALESCE(NULLIF(l.product_name, ''::text), NULLIF(l.product_code, ''::text), 'Item'::text)) AS product_summary,
    string_agg(DISTINCT COALESCE(NULLIF(l.category, ''::text), 'Uncategorised'::text), ' | '::text ORDER BY COALESCE(NULLIF(l.category, ''::text), 'Uncategorised'::text)) AS category_summary,
    string_agg(DISTINCT COALESCE(l.accounts_status, 'PENDING'::text), ' | '::text ORDER BY COALESCE(l.accounts_status, 'PENDING'::text)) AS accounts_status_summary,
    string_agg(DISTINCT COALESCE(l.business_status, 'PENDING'::text), ' | '::text ORDER BY COALESCE(l.business_status, 'PENDING'::text)) AS business_status_summary,
    COALESCE(so.advance_payment_required, false) AS advance_payment_required,
    COALESCE(so.advance_payment_received, false) AS advance_payment_received,
        CASE
            WHEN (COALESCE(so.advance_payment_required, false) AND COALESCE(so.advance_payment_received, false)) THEN 'PAYMENT_RECEIVED'::text
            WHEN COALESCE(so.advance_payment_required, false) THEN 'PAYMENT_PENDING'::text
            ELSE 'NOT_REQUIRED'::text
        END AS advance_payment_status,
    lower(concat_ws(' '::text, COALESCE(so.so_number, ''::text), COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code, ''::text), COALESCE(so.sales_rep, ''::text), string_agg(DISTINCT COALESCE(l.product_name, ''::text), ' '::text), string_agg(DISTINCT COALESCE(l.product_code, ''::text), ' '::text), string_agg(DISTINCT COALESCE(l.category, ''::text), ' '::text),
        CASE
            WHEN COALESCE(so.advance_payment_received, false) THEN 'payment received'::text
            ELSE 'payment pending'::text
        END)) AS match_text
   FROM ((public.sales_orders so
     JOIN public.sales_order_lines l ON ((l.so_id = so.id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
  WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['CANCELLED'::text, 'CLOSED'::text])) AND (upper(COALESCE(l.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['CANCELLED'::text, 'CLOSED'::text])) AND (COALESCE(so.advance_payment_required, false) = true))
  GROUP BY so.id, so.so_number, so.so_date, so.created_at, so.client_code, c.client_name, so.sales_rep, so.advance_payment_required, so.advance_payment_received;

CREATE VIEW public.v_sales_order_item_picker AS

    COALESCE(i.item_name, ''::text) AS item_name,
    COALESCE(i.category, ''::text) AS category,
    COALESCE(i.hsn_group, ''::text) AS hsn_group,
    COALESCE(i.unit, ''::text) AS unit,
    COALESCE(i.default_rate, (0)::numeric) AS default_rate,
    COALESCE(i.gst_pct, (0)::numeric) AS gst_pct,
    COALESCE(i.description, ''::text) AS description,
    COALESCE(i.client_code, ''::text) AS client_code,
    COALESCE(i.client_name, ''::text) AS client_name,
    COALESCE(i.active, true) AS active,
    false AS is_virtual,
    'DB'::text AS prefix_scope,
    20 AS sort_rank,
    i.length_mm,
    i.width_mm,
    i.height_mm,
    i.dimension_unit
   FROM public.items i
  WHERE (COALESCE(i.active, true) = true)
UNION ALL
 SELECT virtual_items.item_code,
    virtual_items.item_name,

    virtual_items.hsn_group,

    NULL::numeric AS length_mm,
    NULL::numeric AS width_mm,
    NULL::numeric AS height_mm,
    NULL::text AS dimension_unit
   FROM ( VALUES ('MTR001'::text,'Ball Pen'::text,'Ball Pen'::text,'BALL PEN'::text,'Pcs'::text,(0)::numeric,(18)::numeric,''::text,''::text,''::text,true,true,'M'::text,10), ('MTR006'::text,'Cup'::text,'Cup'::text,'CUP'::text,'Pcs'::text,(0)::numeric,(18)::numeric,''::text,''::text,''::text,true,true,'M'::text,10), ('SLC003'::text,'Die'::text,'Die'::text,'DIE'::text,'Pcs'::text,(0)::numeric,(18)::numeric,''::text,''::text,''::text,true,true,'SL'::text,30)) virtual_items(item_code, item_name, category, hsn_group, unit, default_rate, gst_pct, description, client_code, client_name, active, is_virtual, prefix_scope, sort_rank);

-- Name: VIEW v_sales_order_item_picker; Type: COMMENT; Schema: public; Owner: -
