COMMENT ON VIEW public.v_sales_order_item_picker IS 'Unified sales-order item picker view for DB items plus M-prefix trading items and SL-prefix common items.';


--
-- Name: v_sales_order_line_details; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_sales_order_line_details AS
 SELECT sol.id,
    sol.so_id,
    so.so_number,
    so.so_date,
    sol.line_no,
    sol.product_code,
    sol.product_name,
    sol.category,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(sol.hsn_group, ''::text), i.hsn_group, ''::text) AS hsn_group,
    COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) AS qty,
    COALESCE(sol.unit, ''::text) AS unit,
    COALESCE(sol.rate, (0)::numeric) AS rate,
    COALESCE(sol.gst_pct, (0)::numeric) AS gst_pct,
    COALESCE(sol.disc_pct, (0)::numeric) AS disc_pct,
    COALESCE(sol.amount, (COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(sol.rate, (0)::numeric))) AS amount,
    COALESCE(sol.disc_amount, (0)::numeric) AS disc_amount,
    COALESCE(sol.cgst, (0)::numeric) AS cgst,
    COALESCE(sol.sgst, (0)::numeric) AS sgst,
    COALESCE(sol.igst, (0)::numeric) AS igst,
    COALESCE(sol.line_total, ((((COALESCE(sol.amount, (COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) * COALESCE(sol.rate, (0)::numeric))) - COALESCE(sol.disc_amount, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(sol.cgst, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(sol.sgst, (0)::numeric)) + COALESCE(sol.igst, (0)::numeric))) AS line_total,
    COALESCE(sol.job_type, ''::text) AS job_type,
    COALESCE(sol.job_reference, ''::text) AS job_reference,
    COALESCE(sol.job_priority, ''::text) AS job_priority,
    COALESCE(sol.division, ''::text) AS division,
    COALESCE(sol.quote_no, ''::text) AS quote_no,
    COALESCE(sol.pm_code, ''::text) AS pm_code,
    sol.expected_delivery,
    sol.final_delivery,
    COALESCE(sol.prepress_remarks, ''::text) AS prepress_remarks,
    COALESCE(sol.product_remarks, ''::text) AS product_remarks,
    COALESCE(sol.accounts_status, 'PENDING'::text) AS accounts_status,
    COALESCE(sol.business_status, 'PENDING'::text) AS business_status,
    COALESCE(src.wo_status, 'PENDING'::text) AS wo_status,
    COALESCE(sol.final_delivery, sol.expected_delivery) AS delivery_display,
    concat_ws(' | '::text, NULLIF(sol.product_remarks, ''::text), NULLIF(sol.prepress_remarks, ''::text)) AS remarks_display,
    so.order_prefix,
    so.client_code,
    c.id AS client_id,
    COALESCE(c.client_name, ''::text) AS client_name,
    COALESCE(c.state, ''::text) AS client_state,
    COALESCE(c.gstin, ''::text) AS client_gstin,
    COALESCE(c.bill_to_address, ''::text) AS client_bill_to_address,
    COALESCE(c.bill_to_city, ''::text) AS client_bill_to_city,
    COALESCE(c.bill_to_state, ''::text) AS client_bill_to_state,
    COALESCE(c.bill_to_pincode, ''::text) AS client_bill_to_pincode,
    COALESCE(c.ship_to_address, ''::text) AS client_ship_to_address,
    COALESCE(c.ship_to_city, ''::text) AS client_ship_to_city,
    COALESCE(c.ship_to_state, ''::text) AS client_ship_to_state,
    COALESCE(c.ship_to_pincode, ''::text) AS client_ship_to_pincode,
    upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) AS status,
    COALESCE(src.line_count, 0) AS line_count,
    COALESCE(src.total_qty, (0)::numeric) AS total_qty,
    src.latest_delivery,
    COALESCE(src.is_approval_hold, false) AS is_approval_hold,
    COALESCE(src.can_edit, (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text)) AS can_edit,
    COALESCE(src.can_hold, (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text)) AS can_hold,
    COALESCE(src.can_cancel, (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text)) AS can_cancel,
    COALESCE(src.hold_target,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = 'HOLD'::text) THEN 'OPEN'::text
            ELSE 'HOLD'::text
        END) AS hold_target,
    COALESCE(src.hold_label,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = 'HOLD'::text) THEN 'Reopen'::text
            ELSE 'Hold'::text
        END) AS hold_label
   FROM ((((public.sales_order_lines sol
     JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = sol.so_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
     LEFT JOIN public.items i ON ((i.item_code = sol.product_code)))
     LEFT JOIN public.sales_order_rollup_cache src ON ((src.so_id = (so.id)::text)));


--
-- Name: v_sales_order_lines_for_approval; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_sales_order_lines_for_approval WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 SELECT so.so_number,
    so.so_date,
    so.created_at AS so_created_at,
    so.client_code,
    c.client_name,
    so.sales_rep,
    l.line_no,
    l.product_name,
    l.category,
    l.qty,
    l.rate,
    l.accounts_status,
    l.accounts_at,
    l.business_status,
    l.business_at,
    so.sales_type,
    so.order_prefix,
    upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) AS header_status,
    COALESCE(l.status, 'OPEN'::text) AS line_status,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['CANCELLED'::text, 'CLOSED'::text])) THEN false
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(l.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = ANY (ARRAY['CANCELLED'::text, 'CLOSED'::text])) THEN false
            ELSE true
        END AS is_line_active,
    COALESCE(so.advance_payment_required, false) AS advance_payment_required,
    COALESCE(so.advance_payment_received, false) AS advance_payment_received
   FROM ((public.sales_order_lines l
     JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = l.so_id)))
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
  WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['CANCELLED'::text, 'CLOSED'::text])) AND (upper(COALESCE(l.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['CANCELLED'::text, 'CLOSED'::text])) AND (upper(COALESCE(so.sales_type, ''::text)) <> 'MANUAL_BILLING'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(so.order_prefix, ''::text)) <> 'MB'::text) AND (so.so_number !~* '^MB[/-]'::text));


--
-- Name: v_sales_orders_list_fast; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_sales_orders_list_fast AS
 SELECT so.id,
    so.so_number,
    so.so_date,
    to_char((so.so_date)::timestamp with time zone, 'YYYY-MM-DD'::text) AS so_date_text,
    so.order_prefix,
    so.client_code,
    COALESCE(c.client_name, ''::text) AS client_name,
    btrim(concat_ws(' '::text, COALESCE(so.client_code, ''::text), COALESCE(c.client_name, ''::text))) AS client_search_text,
    so.sales_rep,
    so.po_number,
    so.sales_type,
    so.currency,
    so.subtotal,
    so.discount_total,
    so.cgst_total,
    so.sgst_total,
    so.igst_total,
    so.grand_total,
    so.remarks,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(so.mode_of_transport, ''::text), ''::text) AS mode_of_transport,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(so.transport_preference, ''::text), ''::text) AS transport_preference,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(so.transport_payment, ''::text), ''::text) AS transport_payment,
    COALESCE(NULLIF(so.billing_remarks, ''::text), ''::text) AS billing_remarks,
    COALESCE(src.accounts_status, NULLIF(upper(COALESCE(so.accounts_approved, ''::text)), ''::text), 'PENDING'::text) AS accounts_status,
    COALESCE(src.business_status, NULLIF(upper(COALESCE(so.business_approved, ''::text)), ''::text), 'PENDING'::text) AS business_status,
    upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) AS status,
    COALESCE(src.wo_status, NULLIF(upper(COALESCE(so.wo_status, ''::text)), ''::text), 'PENDING'::text) AS wo_status,
    COALESCE(src.line_count, 0) AS line_count,
    COALESCE(src.total_qty, (0)::numeric) AS total_qty,
    src.latest_delivery,
    COALESCE(src.is_approval_hold, false) AS is_approval_hold,
    COALESCE(src.can_edit, (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text)) AS can_edit,
    COALESCE(src.can_hold, (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text)) AS can_hold,
    COALESCE(src.can_cancel, (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text)) AS can_cancel,
    COALESCE(src.hold_target,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = 'HOLD'::text) THEN 'OPEN'::text
            ELSE 'HOLD'::text
        END) AS hold_target,
    COALESCE(src.hold_label,
        CASE
            WHEN (upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) = 'HOLD'::text) THEN 'Reopen'::text
            ELSE 'Hold'::text
        END) AS hold_label
   FROM ((public.sales_orders so
     LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
     LEFT JOIN public.sales_order_rollup_cache src ON ((src.so_id = (so.id)::text)));


--
-- Name: v_workorder_candidates; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_workorder_candidates WITH (security_invoker='on') AS
 WITH service_only_items AS (
         SELECT v.item_code,
            v.item_name,
            v.category
           FROM ( VALUES ('SLC001'::text,'FLEXO PRINTING PLATE'::text,'FLEXO PRINTING PLATE'::text), ('SLC002'::text,'OFFSET PRINTING PLATE'::text,'OFFSET PRINTING PLATE'::text), ('SLC003'::text,'DIE'::text,'DIE'::text)) v(item_code, item_name, category)
        ), artwork_ranked AS (
         SELECT a.so_id,
            a.line_no,
            a.artwork_no,
            a.product_type,
            a.sheet_length,
            a.sheet_width,
            a.sheet_ups,
            a.printing_colors,
            a.status,
            a.approved_at,
            row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY a.so_id, a.line_no ORDER BY COALESCE(a.approved_at, a.artwork_at) DESC NULLS LAST, a.id DESC) AS rn
           FROM public.artworks a
        ), artwork_pick AS (
         SELECT artwork_ranked.so_id,
            artwork_ranked.line_no,
            artwork_ranked.artwork_no,
            artwork_ranked.product_type,
            artwork_ranked.sheet_length,
            artwork_ranked.sheet_width,
            artwork_ranked.sheet_ups,
            artwork_ranked.printing_colors,
            artwork_ranked.status,
            artwork_ranked.approved_at
           FROM artwork_ranked
          WHERE (artwork_ranked.rn = 1)
        ), wo_totals AS (
         SELECT woj.so_number,
            woj.line_no,
            sum(COALESCE(woj.qty, (0)::numeric)) AS processed_qty,
            string_agg(DISTINCT NULLIF(wo.wo_number, ''::text), ', '::text ORDER BY NULLIF(wo.wo_number, ''::text)) AS wo_list
           FROM (public.work_order_jobs woj
             LEFT JOIN public.work_orders wo ON ((wo.id = woj.wo_id)))
          GROUP BY woj.so_number, woj.line_no
        ), base AS (
         SELECT so.id AS so_id,
            sol.id AS so_line_id,
            so.so_number,
            so.so_date,
            so.created_at AS so_created_at,
            to_char(COALESCE(so.created_at, (so.so_date)::timestamp without time zone), 'HH24:MI'::text) AS so_time,
            so.sales_rep,
            so.client_code,
            COALESCE(c.client_name, so.client_code, ''::text) AS client_name,
            sol.line_no,
            sol.product_code,
            sol.product_name,
            sol.category,
            sol.qty,
            sol.unit,
            so.po_number,
            sol.expected_delivery,
            sol.job_priority,
            sol.job_type,
            so.remarks AS so_remarks,
            sol.product_remarks,
            sol.prepress_remarks AS prepress_remark,
            sol.job_reference,
            upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(sol.accounts_status, ''::text), 'PENDING'::text)) AS accounts_status,
            upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(sol.business_status, ''::text), 'PENDING'::text)) AS business_status,
            ap.artwork_no,
            COALESCE(NULLIF(ap.product_type, ''::text),
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(sol.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'Flexo'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(sol.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'Corrugated'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(sol.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'Digital'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(sol.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'Offset'::text
                    ELSE ''::text
                END) AS product_type,
            ap.sheet_length,
            ap.sheet_width,
            ap.sheet_ups,
            ap.printing_colors,
                CASE
                    WHEN (ap.so_id IS NULL) THEN 'NO_ART'::text
                    ELSE upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(ap.status, ''::text), 'PENDING'::text))
                END AS artwork_status,
            ap.approved_at AS artwork_approved_at,
            COALESCE(wt.processed_qty, (0)::numeric) AS processed_qty,
            GREATEST((COALESCE(sol.qty, (0)::numeric) - COALESCE(wt.processed_qty, (0)::numeric)), (0)::numeric) AS remaining_qty,
            COALESCE(wt.wo_list, ''::text) AS wo_list,
                CASE
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(ap.product_type, ''::text), sol.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%FLEXO%'::text) THEN 'FLEXO'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(ap.product_type, ''::text), sol.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%CORR%'::text) THEN 'CORRUGATION'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(ap.product_type, ''::text), sol.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%DIGITAL%'::text) THEN 'DIGITAL'::text
                    WHEN (upper(COALESCE(NULLIF(ap.product_type, ''::text), sol.category, ''::text)) ~~ '%OFFSET%'::text) THEN 'OFFSET'::text
                    ELSE upper(COALESCE(sol.category, ''::text))
                END AS department_category
           FROM ((((public.sales_order_lines sol
             JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.id = sol.so_id)))
             LEFT JOIN public.clients c ON ((c.client_code = so.client_code)))
             LEFT JOIN artwork_pick ap ON (((ap.so_id = sol.so_id) AND (ap.line_no = sol.line_no))))
             LEFT JOIN wo_totals wt ON (((wt.so_number = so.so_number) AND (wt.line_no = sol.line_no))))
          WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(so.status, ''::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text) AND (NOT (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
                   FROM service_only_items s
                  WHERE ((s.item_code = upper(TRIM(BOTH FROM COALESCE(sol.product_code, ''::text)))) OR (s.item_name = upper(TRIM(BOTH FROM COALESCE(sol.product_name, ''::text)))) OR (s.category = upper(TRIM(BOTH FROM COALESCE(sol.category, ''::text)))))))))
        ), scored AS (
         SELECT b.so_id,
            b.so_line_id,
            b.so_number,
            b.so_date,
            b.so_created_at,
            b.so_time,
            b.sales_rep,
            b.client_code,
            b.client_name,
            b.line_no,
            b.product_code,
            b.product_name,
            b.category,
            b.qty,
            b.unit,
            b.po_number,
            b.expected_delivery,
            b.job_priority,
            b.job_type,
            b.so_remarks,
            b.product_remarks,
            b.prepress_remark,
            b.job_reference,
            b.accounts_status,
            b.business_status,
            b.artwork_no,
            b.product_type,
            b.sheet_length,
            b.sheet_width,
            b.sheet_ups,
            b.printing_colors,
            b.artwork_status,
            b.artwork_approved_at,
            b.processed_qty,
            b.remaining_qty,
            b.wo_list,
            b.department_category,
            concat_ws(' / '::text,
                CASE
                    WHEN (b.accounts_status = 'REJECTED'::text) THEN 'Accounts'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END,
                CASE
                    WHEN (b.business_status = 'REJECTED'::text) THEN 'Business'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END,
                CASE
                    WHEN (b.artwork_status = 'REJECTED'::text) THEN 'Artwork'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END) AS rejected_stage_list,
            concat_ws(' / '::text,
                CASE
                    WHEN (b.accounts_status <> 'APPROVED'::text) THEN 'Accounts'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END,
                CASE
                    WHEN (b.business_status <> 'APPROVED'::text) THEN 'Business'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END,
                CASE
                    WHEN (b.artwork_status <> 'APPROVED'::text) THEN 'Artwork'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END) AS pending_stage_list
           FROM base b
          WHERE (b.department_category !~~ '%FLEXO%'::text)
        )
 SELECT so_id,
    so_line_id,
    so_number,
    so_date,
    so_created_at,
    so_time,
    sales_rep,
    client_code,
    client_name,
    line_no,
    product_code,
    product_name,
    category,
    department_category,
    qty,
    unit,
    po_number,
    expected_delivery,
    job_priority,
    job_type,
    so_remarks,
    product_remarks,
    prepress_remark,
    job_reference,
    accounts_status,
    business_status,
    artwork_no,
    product_type,
    sheet_length,
    sheet_width,
    sheet_ups,
    printing_colors,
    artwork_status,
    artwork_approved_at,
    processed_qty,
    remaining_qty,
    wo_list,
        CASE
            WHEN (rejected_stage_list <> ''::text) THEN ('Rejected at '::text || rejected_stage_list)
            WHEN (pending_stage_list <> ''::text) THEN ('Pending at '::text || pending_stage_list)
            ELSE 'Approved'::text
        END AS approval_stage,
    ((rejected_stage_list = ''::text) AND (pending_stage_list = ''::text)) AS can_create_wo
   FROM scored;


--
-- Name: v_workorder_candidates_active; Type: VIEW; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE VIEW public.v_workorder_candidates_active AS
 SELECT wc.so_id,
    wc.so_line_id,
    wc.so_number,
    wc.so_date,
    wc.so_created_at,
    wc.so_time,
    wc.sales_rep,
    wc.client_code,
    wc.client_name,
    wc.line_no,
    wc.product_code,
    wc.product_name,
    wc.category,
    wc.department_category,
    wc.qty,
    wc.unit,
    wc.po_number,
    wc.expected_delivery,
    wc.job_priority,
    wc.job_type,
    wc.so_remarks,
    wc.product_remarks,
    wc.prepress_remark,
    wc.job_reference,
    wc.accounts_status,
    wc.business_status,
    wc.artwork_no,
    wc.product_type,
    wc.sheet_length,
    wc.sheet_width,
    wc.sheet_ups,
    wc.printing_colors,
    wc.artwork_status,
    wc.artwork_approved_at,
    wc.processed_qty,
    wc.remaining_qty,
    wc.wo_list,
    wc.approval_stage,
    wc.can_create_wo
   FROM ((public.v_workorder_candidates wc
     JOIN public.sales_orders so ON ((so.so_number = wc.so_number)))
     JOIN public.sales_order_lines sol ON (((sol.so_id = so.id) AND ((sol.line_no)::text = (wc.line_no)::text))))
  WHERE ((upper(COALESCE(so.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> 'CANCELLED'::text) AND (upper(COALESCE(sol.status, 'OPEN'::text)) <> ALL (ARRAY['CLOSED'::text, 'CANCELLED'::text])));


--
-- Name: wo_sequence; Type: TABLE; Schema: public; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE public.wo_sequence (
    id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
    last_no integer DEFAULT 0
);


--
-- Name: messages; Type: TABLE; Schema: realtime; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE realtime.messages (
    topic text NOT NULL,
    extension text NOT NULL,
    payload jsonb,
    event text,
    private boolean DEFAULT false,
    updated_at timestamp without time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
    inserted_at timestamp without time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
    id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL
)
PARTITION BY RANGE (inserted_at);


--
-- Name: schema_migrations; Type: TABLE; Schema: realtime; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE realtime.schema_migrations (
    version bigint NOT NULL,
    inserted_at timestamp(0) without time zone
);


--
-- Name: subscription; Type: TABLE; Schema: realtime; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE realtime.subscription (
    id bigint NOT NULL,
    subscription_id uuid NOT NULL,
    entity regclass NOT NULL,
    filters realtime.user_defined_filter[] DEFAULT '{}'::realtime.user_defined_filter[] NOT NULL,
    claims jsonb NOT NULL,
    claims_role regrole GENERATED ALWAYS AS (realtime.to_regrole((claims ->> 'role'::text))) STORED NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamp without time zone DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()) NOT NULL,
    action_filter text DEFAULT '*'::text,
    CONSTRAINT subscription_action_filter_check CHECK ((action_filter = ANY (ARRAY['*'::text, 'INSERT'::text, 'UPDATE'::text, 'DELETE'::text])))
);


--
-- Name: subscription_id_seq; Type: SEQUENCE; Schema: realtime; Owner: -
--

ALTER TABLE realtime.subscription ALTER COLUMN id ADD GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY (
    SEQUENCE NAME realtime.subscription_id_seq
    START WITH 1
    INCREMENT BY 1
    NO MINVALUE
    NO MAXVALUE
    CACHE 1
);


--
-- Name: buckets; Type: TABLE; Schema: storage; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE storage.buckets (
    id text NOT NULL,
    name text NOT NULL,
    owner uuid,
    created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
    public boolean DEFAULT false,
    avif_autodetection boolean DEFAULT false,
    file_size_limit bigint,
    allowed_mime_types text[],
    owner_id text,
    type storage.buckettype DEFAULT 'STANDARD'::storage.buckettype NOT NULL
);


--
-- Name: COLUMN buckets.owner; Type: COMMENT; Schema: storage; Owner: -
--

COMMENT ON COLUMN storage.buckets.owner IS 'Field is deprecated, use owner_id instead';


--
-- Name: buckets_analytics; Type: TABLE; Schema: storage; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE storage.buckets_analytics (
    name text NOT NULL,
    type storage.buckettype DEFAULT 'ANALYTICS'::storage.buckettype NOT NULL,
    format text DEFAULT 'ICEBERG'::text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
    updated_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
    id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
    deleted_at timestamp with time zone
);


--
-- Name: buckets_vectors; Type: TABLE; Schema: storage; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE storage.buckets_vectors (
    id text NOT NULL,
    type storage.buckettype DEFAULT 'VECTOR'::storage.buckettype NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
    updated_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL
);


--
-- Name: migrations; Type: TABLE; Schema: storage; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE storage.migrations (
    id integer NOT NULL,
    name character varying(100) NOT NULL,
    hash character varying(40) NOT NULL,
    executed_at timestamp without time zone DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);


--
-- Name: objects; Type: TABLE; Schema: storage; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE storage.objects (
    id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
    bucket_id text,
    name text,
    owner uuid,
    created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
    last_accessed_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now(),
    metadata jsonb,
    path_tokens text[] GENERATED ALWAYS AS (string_to_array(name, '/'::text)) STORED,
    version text,
    owner_id text,
    user_metadata jsonb
);


--
-- Name: COLUMN objects.owner; Type: COMMENT; Schema: storage; Owner: -
--

COMMENT ON COLUMN storage.objects.owner IS 'Field is deprecated, use owner_id instead';


--
-- Name: s3_multipart_uploads; Type: TABLE; Schema: storage; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE storage.s3_multipart_uploads (
    id text NOT NULL,
    in_progress_size bigint DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    upload_signature text NOT NULL,
    bucket_id text NOT NULL,
    key text NOT NULL COLLATE pg_catalog."C",
    version text NOT NULL,
    owner_id text,
    created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
    user_metadata jsonb,
    metadata jsonb
);


--
-- Name: s3_multipart_uploads_parts; Type: TABLE; Schema: storage; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE storage.s3_multipart_uploads_parts (
    id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
    upload_id text NOT NULL,
    size bigint DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
    part_number integer NOT NULL,
    bucket_id text NOT NULL,
    key text NOT NULL COLLATE pg_catalog."C",
    etag text NOT NULL,
    owner_id text,
    version text NOT NULL,
    created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL
);


--
-- Name: vector_indexes; Type: TABLE; Schema: storage; Owner: -
--

CREATE TABLE storage.vector_indexes (
    id text DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
    name text NOT NULL COLLATE pg_catalog."C",
    bucket_id text NOT NULL,
    data_type text NOT NULL,
    dimension integer NOT NULL,
    distance_metric text NOT NULL,
    metadata_configuration jsonb,
    created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
    updated_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL
);

--
-- Name: issue_graphql_placeholder; Type: EVENT TRIGGER; Schema: -; Owner: -
--

CREATE EVENT TRIGGER issue_graphql_placeholder ON sql_drop
         WHEN TAG IN ('DROP EXTENSION')
   EXECUTE FUNCTION extensions.set_graphql_placeholder();
